create_appropriate_relic_artifact_effect = {
	hidden_effect_new_object = {
		if = {
			limit = { like_christianity_religion_trigger = yes } #Warcraft
			create_artifact_pedestal_reliquary_christian_effect = { OWNER = $OWNER$ }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { like_islam_religion_trigger = yes }
			create_artifact_pedestal_reliquary_islam_effect = { OWNER = $OWNER$ }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { like_buddhism_religion_trigger = yes }
			create_artifact_pedestal_reliquary_buddhism_effect = { OWNER = $OWNER$ }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { like_judaism_religion_trigger = yes }
			create_artifact_pedestal_reliquary_judaism_effect = { OWNER = $OWNER$ }
		}
		else = {
			create_artifact_sculpture_religious_effect = { OWNER = $OWNER$ CREATOR = $CREATOR$ }
		}
		scope:newly_created_artifact = {
			set_variable = relic # Adds the Relic flag
		}
		if = { # Makes a regular character artifact if you don't have a Royal Court
			limit = {
				has_royal_court = no
			}
			scope:newly_created_artifact = {
				reforge_artifact = { type = miscellaneous }
			}
		}
	}
}

create_artifact_pedestal_bible_effect = {
	$OWNER$ = { save_scope_as = owner }
	$CREATOR$ = { save_scope_as = creator }

	get_artifact_quality_effect = yes
	get_artifact_wealth_effect = yes

	scope:owner = {
		create_artifact = {
			name = artifact_pedestal_bible_name
			creator = scope:creator
			description = artifact_pedestal_bible_description
			visuals = pam_bible
			type = pedestal
			template = cross_template
			modifier = artifact_placeholder_modifier
			save_scope_as = newly_created_artifact
			wealth = scope:wealth
			quality = scope:quality
		}
	}

	scope:newly_created_artifact = {
		add_scaled_artifact_modifier_devotion_effect = yes
		add_scaled_artifact_modifier_grandeur_small_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					rarity = famed
					rarity = illustrious
				}
			}
			add_scaled_artifact_modifier_piety_effect = yes
		}
		remove_artifact_modifier = artifact_placeholder_modifier
	}
}

create_christian_religious_artifact_effect = {
	hidden_effect_new_object = {
		random_list = {
			1 = {
				create_artifact_pedestal_cross_effect = { OWNER = $OWNER$ CREATOR = $CREATOR$ }
			}
			1 = {
				trigger = {
					rite = {
						NOT = { rite_has_tenet = tenet_aniconism }
					}
				}
				create_artifact_pedestal_crucifix_effect = { OWNER = $OWNER$ CREATOR = $CREATOR$ }
			}
			1 = {
				create_artifact_sculpture_religious_effect = { OWNER = $OWNER$ CREATOR = $CREATOR$ }
			}
			1 = {
				create_artifact_pedestal_bible_effect = { OWNER = $OWNER$ CREATOR = $CREATOR$ }
			}
			1 = {
				create_artifact_pedestal_thurible_effect = { OWNER = $OWNER$ CREATOR = $CREATOR$ }
			}
			1 = {
				create_artifact_pedestal_situla_effect = { OWNER = $OWNER$ CREATOR = $CREATOR$ }
			}
			1 = {
				create_artifact_pedestal_crozier_effect = { OWNER = $OWNER$ CREATOR = $CREATOR$ }
			}
		}
		if = { # Makes a regular character artifact if you don't have a Royal Court
			limit = {
				has_royal_court = no
			}
			scope:newly_created_artifact = { reforge_artifact = { type = miscellaneous } }
		}
	}
}

# Called from wars on_white_peace
pam_miles_christi_white_peace_effect = {
	scope:defender = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				rite = { rite_has_parameter = tenet_miles_christi_white_peace_defender_bonus }
				scope:attacker = {
					faith = scope:defender.faith
				}
			}
			add_piety = {
				value = medium_piety_gain
				multiply = scope:attacker.primary_title.tier
				multiply = scope:defender.primary_title.tier
			}
			change_spiritual_fulfillment = medium_spiritual_fulfillment_value
			add_legitimacy = {
				value = medium_legitimacy_gain
				multiply = scope:attacker.primary_title.tier
				multiply = scope:defender.primary_title.tier
			}
		}
	}
}
# Both on_combat_end_loser and on_combat_end_winner fire this
pam_miles_christi_fulfillment_from_combat_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			rite = { rite_has_parameter = tenet_miles_christi_combat_fulfillment }
		}
		send_interface_toast = {
			title = successful_battles_and_sieges_give_sp_toast
			left_icon = root
			change_spiritual_fulfillment = minor_spiritual_fulfillment_value
		}
	}
}

# Called from on_combat_end_winner in the scope of the winning combat side.
pam_miles_christi_holy_weapon_victory_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = enemy_side.side_primary_participant
		}
		enemy_side.side_primary_participant = {
			save_temporary_scope_as = pam_holy_weapon_enemy
		}
		every_side_commander = {
			limit = {
				rite = { rite_has_parameter = tenet_miles_christi_holy_weapon_blessing }
				NOT = { faith.religion = scope:pam_holy_weapon_enemy.faith.religion }
			}
			every_character_artifact = {
				limit = { pam_holy_weapon_trigger = yes }
				pam_miles_christi_holy_weapon_gain_victory_effect = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

# Artifact scope. Tallies one holy victory and steps up the blessing when a threshold is crossed.
pam_miles_christi_holy_weapon_gain_victory_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = pam_holy_weapon_victories_var } }
		set_variable = {
			name = pam_holy_weapon_victories_var
			value = 1
		}
	}
	else = {
		change_variable = {
			name = pam_holy_weapon_victories_var
			add = 1
		}
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_variable = pam_holy_weapon_blessing_var }
			var:pam_holy_weapon_victories_var >= 5
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = pam_holy_weapon_blessing_var
			value = 1
		}
		pam_miles_christi_holy_weapon_blessing_effect = { MODIFIER = pam_holy_weapon_1_artifact_modifier }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = pam_holy_weapon_blessing_var
			var:pam_holy_weapon_blessing_var = 1
			var:pam_holy_weapon_victories_var >= 15
		}
		change_variable = {
			name = pam_holy_weapon_blessing_var
			add = 1
		}
		pam_miles_christi_holy_weapon_blessing_effect = { MODIFIER = pam_holy_weapon_2_artifact_modifier }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = pam_holy_weapon_blessing_var
			var:pam_holy_weapon_blessing_var = 2
			var:pam_holy_weapon_victories_var >= 30
		}
		change_variable = {
			name = pam_holy_weapon_blessing_var
			add = 1
		}
		pam_miles_christi_holy_weapon_blessing_effect = { MODIFIER = pam_holy_weapon_3_artifact_modifier }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = pam_holy_weapon_blessing_var
			var:pam_holy_weapon_blessing_var = 3
			var:pam_holy_weapon_victories_var >= 50
		}
		change_variable = {
			name = pam_holy_weapon_blessing_var
			add = 1
		}
		pam_miles_christi_holy_weapon_blessing_effect = { MODIFIER = pam_holy_weapon_4_artifact_modifier }
	}
}

# Artifact scope
# MODIFIER = the artifact modifier for the tier being reached
pam_miles_christi_holy_weapon_blessing_effect = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = pam_holy_weapon
	add_artifact_modifier = $MODIFIER$
	artifact_owner ?= {
		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = pam_holy_weapon_blessing_toast
			desc = pam_holy_weapon_blessing_toast_desc
			left_icon = this
			right_icon = scope:pam_holy_weapon
			show_as_tooltip = {
				scope:pam_holy_weapon = {
					add_artifact_modifier = $MODIFIER$
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

pam_pursuit_of_power_war_victory_fulfillment_effect = {
	scope:winner = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_personal_tenet_flag = tenet_pursuit_of_power_war_victory_fulfillment
				scope:war.casus_belli = {
					any_target_title = { is_landless_type_title = no }
				}
			}
			send_interface_toast = {
				title = pam_pursuit_of_power_war_victory_toast
				left_icon = root
				change_spiritual_fulfillment = medium_spiritual_fulfillment_value
			}
		}
	}
}

# Only rewards paying for someone else's freedom in gold; self-ransoms and favour settlements do not count.
pam_philanthropia_ransom_payer_effect = {
	scope:payer = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_personal_tenet_flag = tenet_philanthropia_ransom_captives
				this != scope:prisoner
				pam_philanthropia_ransom_gold_value > 0
			}
			send_interface_toast = {
				title = pam_philanthropia_ransom_toast
				left_icon = scope:prisoner
				add_piety = pam_philanthropia_ransom_piety_value
				change_spiritual_fulfillment = pam_philanthropia_ransom_fulfillment_value
			}
		}
	}
}

# Rewards releasing or exiling a prisoner outright; anything extracted from them first makes it a bargain.
tenet_benevolent_governance_clemency_effect = {
	scope:puppet_or_actor = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_personal_tenet_flag = tenet_benevolent_governance_clemency_fulfillment
				OR = {
					tenet_benevolent_governance_unconditional_release_trigger = yes
					AND = {
						scope:banish = yes
						has_execute_reason = scope:recipient
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_ai = no }
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_generic_good
					title = benevolent_governance_clemency_fulfillment_toast
					left_icon = scope:puppet_or_actor
					right_icon = scope:recipient
					change_spiritual_fulfillment = tenet_benevolent_governance_clemency_fulfillment_value
				}
			}
			else = {
				change_spiritual_fulfillment = tenet_benevolent_governance_clemency_fulfillment_value
			}
		}
	}
}

pam_be_fruitful_fruitful_upbringing_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:father
			OR = {
				scope:mother ?= { has_personal_tenet_flag = tenet_be_fruitful_and_multiply_fruitful_upbringing }
				scope:father ?= { has_personal_tenet_flag = tenet_be_fruitful_and_multiply_fruitful_upbringing }
			}
		}
		scope:child = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:mother ?= { has_relation_soulmate = scope:father }
				}
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = tenet_be_fruitful_and_multiply_fruitful_upbringing_devoted
					years = 16
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					scope:mother ?= {
						OR = {
							has_relation_lover = scope:father
							has_relation_best_friend = scope:father
						}
					}
				}
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = tenet_be_fruitful_and_multiply_fruitful_upbringing_close
					years = 16
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					scope:mother ?= {
						OR = {
							has_relation_friend = scope:father
							opinion = {
								target = scope:father
								value >= medium_positive_opinion
							}
						}
					}
				}
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = tenet_be_fruitful_and_multiply_fruitful_upbringing_fond
					years = 16
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# PAM Scholasticism tenet effect
pam_tenet_scholasticism_university_bonus_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			rite = { rite_has_parameter = tenet_scholasticism_university_bonus }
		}
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = tenet_scholasticism_modifier
			years = 20
		}
	}
}

pam_pentarchy_circuit_entourage_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { exists = involved_activity }
		save_temporary_scope_as = pam_pentarchy_circuit_pilgrim
		involved_activity = {
			every_attending_character = {
				limit = { this != scope:pam_pentarchy_circuit_pilgrim }
				custom = pam_pentarchy_circuit_entourage_tt
				hidden_effect = {
					local_court_type_skill_point_effect = {
						PROVINCE = scope:pam_pentarchy_circuit_pilgrim.location
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Pentarchy tenet is already checked when pilgrimage_additional_reward_effect fires this
pam_pentarchy_pilgrim_circuit_effect = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = pam_pentarchy_pilgrim
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_character_flag = pam_pentarchy_circuit_completed }
			exists = scope:pam_pentarchy_pilgrim.location.barony
			faith = {
				any_holy_site_barony = { this = scope:pam_pentarchy_pilgrim.location.barony }
			}
		}
		scope:pam_pentarchy_pilgrim.location.barony = {
			save_temporary_scope_as = pam_pentarchy_see
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable_list = pam_pentarchy_sees_visited }
					NOT = {
						is_target_in_variable_list = {
							name = pam_pentarchy_sees_visited
							target = scope:pam_pentarchy_see
						}
					}
				}
			}
			add_to_variable_list = {
				name = pam_pentarchy_sees_visited
				target = scope:pam_pentarchy_see
			}

			# Recount vs current holy sites so a conversion since visiting self-corrects
			set_variable = {
				name = pam_pentarchy_sees_total
				value = 0
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = pam_pentarchy_sees_count
				value = 0
			}
			faith = {
				every_holy_site = {
					limit = { exists = holy_site_barony }
					holy_site_barony = { save_temporary_scope_as = pam_pentarchy_counted_see }
					scope:pam_pentarchy_pilgrim = {
						change_variable = {
							name = pam_pentarchy_sees_total
							add = 1
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
								is_target_in_variable_list = {
									name = pam_pentarchy_sees_visited
									target = scope:pam_pentarchy_counted_see
								}
							}
							change_variable = {
								name = pam_pentarchy_sees_count
								add = 1
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					# The log entry's description is drawn before the counters above are set
					has_variable = pam_pentarchy_sees_total
					var:pam_pentarchy_sees_total > 0
					var:pam_pentarchy_sees_count >= var:pam_pentarchy_sees_total
				}
				add_character_flag = pam_pentarchy_circuit_completed
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_text_and_effect_good
					title = pam_pentarchy_circuit_completed_toast
					desc = pam_pentarchy_circuit_completed_toast_desc
					left_icon = scope:pam_pentarchy_pilgrim
					add_character_modifier = {
						modifier = pam_pilgrim_of_every_holy_site_modifier
					}
					add_piety = massive_piety_gain
					change_spiritual_fulfillment = major_spiritual_fulfillment_value
					if = {
						limit = { has_personal_tenet_flag = tenet_pentarchy_pilgrim_circuit_companions }
						pam_pentarchy_circuit_entourage_effect = yes
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				send_interface_message = {
					type = event_generic_good_with_text
					title = pam_pentarchy_see_visited_message
					desc = pam_pentarchy_see_visited_message_desc
					left_icon = scope:pam_pentarchy_pilgrim
					add_piety = medium_piety_gain
					change_spiritual_fulfillment = minor_spiritual_fulfillment_value
					if = {
						limit = { has_personal_tenet_flag = tenet_pentarchy_pilgrim_circuit_companions }
						pam_pentarchy_circuit_entourage_effect = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

pam_tenet_scholasticism_yearly_effect = {
	random = {
		chance = {
			value = 5
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = no
				}
				add = 5
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					learning >= 30
				}
				add = 2
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					learning >= 40
				}
				add = 3
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					piety_level >= 4
				}
				add = 2
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					piety_level >= 5
				}
				add = 3
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					num_of_known_languages >= 3
				}
				add = 2
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					num_of_known_languages >= 5
				}
				add = 3
			}
		}
		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = pam_tenet_scholasticism_toast_title
			left_icon = root
			add_character_modifier = {
				modifier = tenet_scholasticism_modifier
				years = 20
			}
		}
	}
}

pam_tenet_scholasticism_studium_effect = {
	save_scope_as = studium_master
	current_travel_plan ?= {
		random_entourage_character = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { this = scope:studium_master }
				is_physically_able_ai_adult = yes
				ep1_is_valid_character_for_inspiration_trigger = yes
			}
			random = {
				chance = pam_scholasticism_studium_inspiration_chance_value
				create_inspiration = book_inspiration
				save_scope_as = pam_studium_scholar
				scope:studium_master = {
					send_interface_toast = {
						type = event_toast_effect_good
						title = pam_tenet_scholasticism_studium_toast_title
						desc = pam_tenet_scholasticism_studium_toast_desc
						left_icon = scope:pam_studium_scholar
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
add_scholar_trait_or_xp_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_trait = lifestyle_scholar }
		}
		add_trait = lifestyle_scholar
	}
	else = {
		add_trait_xp = {
			trait = lifestyle_scholar
			value = $VALUE$
		}
	}
}

save_most_suitable_monastic_leader_effect = {
	#Patronized by puppet_or_actor
	random_faith_holy_order = {
		limit = {
			take_the_vows_valid_monastic_order_trigger = { CHARACTER = scope:puppet_or_actor }
			holy_order_patron = scope:puppet_or_actor
		}
		leader = {
			save_scope_as = archabbot
		}
	}
	#leases from puppet_or_actor
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:archabbot
			}
		}
		random_faith_holy_order = {
			limit = {
				take_the_vows_valid_monastic_order_trigger = { CHARACTER = scope:puppet_or_actor }
				any_leased_title = {
					county.holder ?= scope:puppet_or_actor
				}
			}
			leader = {
				save_scope_as = archabbot
			}
		}
	}
	#patronized by puppet_or_actor's superior
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:archabbot
			}
		}
		random_faith_holy_order = {
			limit = {
				take_the_vows_valid_monastic_order_trigger = { CHARACTER = scope:puppet_or_actor }
				holy_order_patron = {
					is_liege_or_above_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
				}
			}
			leader = {
				save_scope_as = archabbot
			}
		}
	}
	#in same realm as puppet_or_actor
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:archabbot
			}
		}
		random_faith_holy_order = {
			limit = {
				take_the_vows_valid_monastic_order_trigger = { CHARACTER = scope:puppet_or_actor }
				any_leased_title = {
					county.holder.top_liege ?= scope:puppet_or_actor.top_liege
				}
			}
			leader = {
				save_scope_as = archabbot
			}
		}
	}
	#in diplo range of puppet_or_actor
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:archabbot
			}
		}
		random_faith_holy_order = {
			limit = {
				take_the_vows_valid_monastic_order_trigger = { CHARACTER = scope:puppet_or_actor }
				leader = {
					in_diplomatic_range = scope:puppet_or_actor
				}
			}
			leader = {
				save_scope_as = archabbot
			}
		}
	}
	#any order
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:archabbot
			}
		}
		random_faith_holy_order = {
			limit = {
				take_the_vows_valid_monastic_order_trigger = { CHARACTER = scope:puppet_or_actor }
			}
			leader = {
				save_scope_as = archabbot
			}
		}
	}
}

# Saeculum Obscurum ends - time to get divorced
pam_the_christian_church_saeculum_obscurum_end_clergy_divorce_effect = {
	every_situation_participant = {
		limit = {
			is_clergy = yes
			rite = { rite_has_doctrine = doctrine_clerical_marriage_disallowed }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_married = yes
					is_ai = yes
				}
				every_spouse = {
					limit = { is_ai = yes }
					save_temporary_scope_as = saeculum_clergy_divorcee
					less_verbose_divorce_effect = {
						DIVORCER = prev
						DIVORCEE = scope:saeculum_clergy_divorcee
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { exists = betrothed }
				break_betrothal = betrothed
			}
		}
	}
}

#The Christian Church phase start effect -
# Phase on_start runs with situation as root; every_player does not iterate players from that context. Iterate situation participants instead (cf. the_great_steppe phase on_start).
the_christian_church_fire_event_for_participant_players_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_variable = pam_skip_tcc_phase_start_event }
		}
		every_player = {
			limit = {
				# Saeculum Obscurum on_start fires the schism start event
				trigger_if = {
					limit = {
						situation:the_christian_church ?= { situation_current_phase = saeculum_obscurum }
					}
					situation:the_christian_church ?= {
						NOT = { has_variable = pam_great_schism_status }
					}
				}
				OR = {
					government_has_flag = government_is_theocracy
					years_from_game_start >= 1
				}
				any_character_situation = {
					this = situation:the_christian_church
				}
			}
			trigger_event = $EVENT$
		}
	}
}

#The Christian Church situation effects
the_christian_church_fire_correct_phase_start_event_for_player_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_ai = no
			is_player_tutorial_character = no
			NOT = { has_global_variable = pam_skip_tcc_phase_start_event }
			OR = {
				government_has_flag = government_is_theocracy
				years_from_game_start >= 1
			}
		}
		save_scope_as = situation_participant
		situation:the_christian_church = {
			switch = {
				trigger = situation_current_phase
				fragile_unity = {
					scope:situation_participant = {
						trigger_event = pam_the_christian_church.0051
					}
				}
				saeculum_obscurum = {
					scope:situation_participant = {
						trigger_event = pam_the_christian_church.0052
					}
				}
				conversion_phase = {
					scope:situation_participant = {
						trigger_event = pam_the_christian_church.0053
					}
				}
				reform_phase = {
					scope:situation_participant = {
						trigger_event = pam_the_christian_church.0054
					}
				}
				investiture_crisis = {
					scope:situation_participant = {
						trigger_event = pam_the_christian_church.0055
					}
				}
				crusade_phase = {
					scope:situation_participant = {
						trigger_event = pam_the_christian_church.0056
					}
				}
				concord = {
					scope:situation_participant = {
						trigger_event = pam_the_christian_church.0057
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

pam_remove_forbidden_personal_tenet_secret_if_invalid_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_secret = { type = secret_forbidden_personal_tenet }
			NOT = { pam_has_forbidden_personal_tenet_trigger = yes }
		}
		every_secret = {
			type = secret_forbidden_personal_tenet
			remove_secret = yes
		}
	}
}

# Faith scope
pam_faith_cleanup_forbidden_personal_tenet_secrets_effect = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_faith_character = {
			pam_remove_forbidden_personal_tenet_secret_if_invalid_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

# Faith scope. TENET is the tenet just prohibited
pam_faith_grant_forbidden_personal_tenet_secrets_effect = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_faith_character = {
			limit = {
				has_personal_tenet = $TENET$
				NOT = { any_secret = { type = secret_forbidden_personal_tenet } }
			}
			on_personal_tenet_gain_secret_gain_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

on_personal_tenet_gain_secret_gain_effect = {
	# AI on_personal_tenet_gain is delayed, so the prohibited tenet may already be gone
	if = {
		limit = { pam_has_forbidden_personal_tenet_trigger = yes }
		send_interface_toast = {
			title = on_personal_tenet_gain_secret_gain_effect_tt
			left_icon = root
			add_secret = {
				type = secret_forbidden_personal_tenet
				target = this
			}
			if = {
				limit = { exists = var:tenet_evangelized_by }
				var:tenet_evangelized_by = { save_temporary_scope_as = tenet_evangelizer }
				every_secret = {
					type = secret_forbidden_personal_tenet
					reveal_to = scope:tenet_evangelizer
				}
				remove_variable = tenet_evangelized_by
			}
		}
	}
}

# Called from five_year_playable_pulse. Root is the character.
pam_ai_personal_tenet_pulse_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_physically_able_ai = yes
		}

		# Phase 0: cardinals may hold beliefs their rite prohibits, so a new pontiff can arrive carrying one
		# Ahead of Phase 1 so the freed slot refills this pulse; on_personal_tenet_loss clears the secret
		if = {
			limit = {
				pam_holds_college_elected_church_seat_trigger = yes
				pam_has_forbidden_personal_tenet_trigger = yes
			}
			faith.main_rite = { save_temporary_scope_as = pam_pontiff_rite }
			while = {
				count = 8
				limit = { pam_has_forbidden_personal_tenet_trigger = yes }
				random_personal_tenet = {
					limit = {
						scope:pam_pontiff_rite = {
							has_tenet_status = {
								tenet = prev
								status = prohibited
							}
						}
					}
					root = { remove_personal_tenet = prev }
				}
			}
		}

		# Phase 1: fill every empty personal tenet slot. No chance gate - empty slots are wasted SF and immersion.
		# 8 = safety upper bound on slot cap (base 1 + up to ~5 from piety levels, with headroom).
		while = {
			count = 8
			limit = {
				free_personal_tenet_slots > 0
				any_desired_tenet = {
					only_positive = yes
					threshold = 1
					selection_count = 97
					save_temporary_scope_as = pam_personal_tenet_candidate
					NOT = {
						root = {
							any_personal_tenet = {
								this = scope:pam_personal_tenet_candidate
							}
						}
					}
					OR = {
						NOT = {
							root.faith.main_rite = {
								has_tenet_status = {
									tenet = scope:pam_personal_tenet_candidate
									status = prohibited
								}
							}
						}
						root = { pam_ai_can_consider_forbidden_personal_tenet_trigger = yes }
					}
				}
			}

			random_desired_tenet = {
				only_positive = yes
				threshold = 1
				selection_count = 97
				limit = {
					save_temporary_scope_as = pam_personal_tenet_candidate
					NOT = {
						root = {
							any_personal_tenet = {
								this = scope:pam_personal_tenet_candidate
							}
						}
					}
					OR = {
						NOT = {
							root.faith.main_rite = {
								has_tenet_status = {
									tenet = scope:pam_personal_tenet_candidate
									status = prohibited
								}
							}
						}
						root = { pam_ai_can_consider_forbidden_personal_tenet_trigger = yes }
					}
				}
				root = { set_personal_tenet = prev }
			}
		}

		# Phase 2: chance-gated swap, only when there are no free slots left to fill.
		if = {
			limit = {
				free_personal_tenet_slots <= 0
				any_desired_tenet = {
					only_positive = yes
					threshold = 1
					selection_count = 97
					save_temporary_scope_as = pam_personal_tenet_candidate
					NOT = {
						root = {
							any_personal_tenet = {
								this = scope:pam_personal_tenet_candidate
							}
						}
					}
					OR = {
						NOT = {
							root.faith.main_rite = {
								has_tenet_status = {
									tenet = scope:pam_personal_tenet_candidate
									status = prohibited
								}
							}
						}
						root = { pam_ai_can_consider_forbidden_personal_tenet_trigger = yes }
					}
				}
			}

			random = {
				chance = 30
				modifier = {
					add = 15
					has_trait = zealous
				}
				modifier = {
					add = -15
					has_trait = cynical
				}
				modifier = {
					add = {
						value = ai_zeal
						divide = 5
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					add = {
						value = ai_boldness
						divide = 5
					}
				}

				# Pick the new tenet - Random of any positive-SF known tenet, not strictly the best.
				random_desired_tenet = {
					only_positive = yes
					threshold = 1
					selection_count = 97
					limit = {
						save_temporary_scope_as = pam_personal_tenet_candidate
						NOT = {
							root = {
								any_personal_tenet = {
									this = scope:pam_personal_tenet_candidate
								}
							}
						}
						OR = {
							NOT = {
								root.faith.main_rite = {
									has_tenet_status = {
										tenet = scope:pam_personal_tenet_candidate
										status = prohibited
									}
								}
							}
							root = { pam_ai_can_consider_forbidden_personal_tenet_trigger = yes }
						}
					}
					root = { force_set_personal_tenet = prev }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

conversion_tenet_acts_of_the_apostles_effect = {
	scope:puppet_or_actor = {
		if = {
			limit = { # Legitimacy if core to the faith
				rite = {
					rite_has_parameter = tenet_acts_of_apostles_legitimacy_from_converts
				}
				is_valid_for_legitimacy_change = yes
			}
			send_interface_toast = {
				title = conversion_tenet_acts_of_the_apostles_effect_legitimacy_tt
				left_icon = scope:puppet_or_actor
				add_legitimacy = minor_legitimacy_gain
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { # SP if personal tenet - converting the mighty pays more
				has_personal_tenet_flag = tenet_acts_of_apostles_fulfillment_from_converts
				scope:recipient ?= { is_landed = yes }
			}
			send_interface_toast = {
				title = conversion_tenet_acts_of_the_apostles_effect_sp_tt
				left_icon = scope:puppet_or_actor
				change_spiritual_fulfillment = major_spiritual_fulfillment_value
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_personal_tenet_flag = tenet_acts_of_apostles_fulfillment_from_converts
			}
			send_interface_toast = {
				title = conversion_tenet_acts_of_the_apostles_effect_sp_tt
				left_icon = scope:puppet_or_actor
				change_spiritual_fulfillment = minor_spiritual_fulfillment_value
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { # Piety if core to the faith, scaled steeply by the convert's rank
				rite = { rite_has_parameter = tenet_acts_of_apostles_piety_from_converts }
				scope:recipient ?= { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_barony }
			}
			send_interface_toast = {
				title = conversion_tenet_acts_of_the_apostles_effect_piety_tt
				left_icon = scope:puppet_or_actor
				add_piety = pam_acts_conversion_piety_value
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				rite = { rite_has_parameter = tenet_acts_of_apostles_legitimacy_from_converts }
				is_valid_for_legitimacy_change = yes
			}
		}
		scope:actor = {
			send_interface_toast = {
				title = conversion_tenet_acts_of_the_apostles_effect_legitimacy_tt_puppeteer
				left_icon = scope:puppet_or_actor
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes
			scope:puppet_or_actor = { has_personal_tenet_flag = tenet_acts_of_apostles_fulfillment_from_converts }
		}
		scope:actor = {
			send_interface_toast = {
				title = conversion_tenet_acts_of_the_apostles_effect_sp_tt_puppeteer
				left_icon = scope:puppet_or_actor
			}
		}
	}
}

pam_tenet_filial_piety_education_completion_reward_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_character_flag = pam_filial_piety_education_rewarded }
			exists = scope:educator
			scope:educator = {
				is_parent_of = root
				has_personal_tenet_flag = tenet_filial_piety_education_completion_reward
			}
		}
		add_character_flag = pam_filial_piety_education_rewarded
		scope:educator = {
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = pam_filial_piety_education_completion_parent_toast
				left_icon = scope:educator
				right_icon = root
				add_piety = major_piety_value
				change_spiritual_fulfillment = major_spiritual_fulfillment_value
			}
		}
		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = pam_filial_piety_education_completion_child_toast
			left_icon = root
			right_icon = scope:educator
			add_piety = major_piety_value
			if = {
				limit = { is_playable_character = yes }
				change_spiritual_fulfillment = major_spiritual_fulfillment_value
			}
		}
	}
}

# An activity already under way was planned without the vow, so its reward is forfeit
pam_vows_of_poverty_block_ongoing_activity_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_personal_tenet_flag = tenet_vows_of_poverty_humble_host
			exists = involved_activity
			involved_activity.activity_host = root
		}
		involved_activity = {
			set_variable = pam_vows_of_poverty_completion_piety_paid
		}
	}
}

pam_vows_of_poverty_host_completion_piety_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			this = scope:host
			scope:host = {
				has_personal_tenet_flag = tenet_vows_of_poverty_humble_host
			}
			scope:activity = {
				NOT = { has_variable = pam_vows_of_poverty_completion_piety_paid }
			}
		}
		scope:activity = {
			set_variable = pam_vows_of_poverty_completion_piety_paid
			scope:host = {
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_effect_good
					title = pam_vows_of_poverty_activity_completion_piety_toast
					left_icon = scope:host
					add_piety = pam_vows_of_poverty_host_piety_value
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

hedonistic_obesity_modifier_sync_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			current_weight >= overweight_value
			has_personal_tenet_flag = tenet_hedonistic_obesity_fulfillment
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = tenet_hedonistic_obese_modifier }
			}
			add_character_modifier = {
				modifier = tenet_hedonistic_obese_modifier
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_character_modifier = tenet_hedonistic_obese_modifier
		}
		remove_character_modifier = tenet_hedonistic_obese_modifier
	}
}

hedonistic_feast_gout_risk_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_personal_tenet_flag = tenet_hedonistic_feast_gout_risk
			current_weight >= overweight_value
			can_contract_disease_trigger = { DISEASE = gout_ridden }
		}
		random_list = {
			75 = {}
			25 = {
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_effect_bad
					title = hedonistic_feast_gout_risk_toast
					left_icon = root
					show_as_tooltip = { add_trait_force_tooltip = gout_ridden }
					hidden_effect = {
						contract_disease_effect = { DISEASE = gout_ridden TREATMENT_EVENT = no }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Revel in Pain belongs to Exaltation of Pain
pam_mortify_flesh_scourge_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_personal_tenet_flag = tenet_mortification_revel_bonus }
		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_generic_good
			title = pam_mortification_revel_toast
			left_icon = root
			if = { # If you don't have the trait, it's guaranteed
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_trait = flagellant }
				}
				add_trait = flagellant
			}
			change_spiritual_fulfillment = medium_spiritual_fulfillment_value
		}
	}

	if = {
		limit = { has_personal_tenet_flag = tenet_mortification_holy_scars }
		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_generic_good
			title = pam_mortification_holy_scars_toast
			left_icon = root
			add_trait = scarred
			add_trait_xp = {
				trait = scarred
				value = { integer_range = { min = 10 max = 25 } }
			}
		}
	}
}

# PAM - Reincarnation: claim a forebear's personality trait.
# Opposing traits are replaced first; otherwise the least-fulfilling personality trait is replaced.
pam_claim_remembered_personality_trait_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			$TRAIT$ = {
				any_opposite_trait = {
					root = { has_trait = prev }
				}
			}
		}
		$TRAIT$ = {
			every_opposite_trait = {
				limit = {
					root = { has_trait = prev }
				}
				root = { remove_trait = prev }
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { number_of_personality_traits >= 1 }
		ordered_character_trait = {
			limit = { has_trait_category = personality }
			order_by = pam_recall_worst_personality_order_value
			save_scope_as = pam_recall_replaced_personality
		}
		remove_trait = scope:pam_recall_replaced_personality
	}
	add_trait = $TRAIT$
	change_spiritual_fulfillment = medium_spiritual_fulfillment_value
}

pam_great_project_contribution_catalyst_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			this != scope:owner
			this != scope:founder
			scope:owner ?= {
				any_character_situation = {
					situation_type = the_christian_church
				}
			}
			OR = {
				scope:great_project.great_project_type = great_project_type:pam_cathedral_01
				scope:great_project.great_project_type = great_project_type:pam_cathedral_02
				scope:great_project.great_project_type = great_project_type:pam_cathedral_03
				scope:great_project.great_project_type = great_project_type:pam_mass_rite_conversion
			}
		}
		random_character_situation = {
			limit = { situation_type = the_christian_church }
			switch = {
				trigger = scope:great_project.great_project_type
				great_project_type:pam_cathedral_01 = {
					if = {
						limit = { situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_great_project_cathedral_contribution }
						trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_great_project_cathedral_contribution
					}
				}
				great_project_type:pam_cathedral_02 = {
					if = {
						limit = { situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_great_project_cathedral_contribution }
						trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_great_project_cathedral_contribution
					}
				}
				great_project_type:pam_cathedral_03 = {
					if = {
						limit = { situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_great_project_cathedral_contribution }
						trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_great_project_cathedral_contribution
					}
				}
				great_project_type:pam_mass_rite_conversion = {
					if = {
						limit = { situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_great_project_mass_rite_conversion_contribution }
						trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_great_project_mass_rite_conversion_contribution
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

pam_levy_the_tithes_decision_effects = {
	every_vassal = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				government_has_flag = government_is_theocracy
				government_has_flag = government_is_ecclesiastical
			}
		}
		custom = every_theocratic_vassal_tt
		pay_short_term_gold = {
			target = root
			gold = minor_gold_value
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { exists = cp:councillor_court_chaplain }
		cp:councillor_court_chaplain = {
			progress_towards_rival_effect = {
				CHARACTER = root
				REASON = rival_levied_tithes
				OPINION = default_rival_opinion
			}
		}
	}
}

#The flag is set in give_cardinal_order_interaction this provides the appropriate event option to accept or deny, along with communicating to players (via toast) who rule the county(s) affected by the below modifiers that a change has taken place
pam_choose_cardinal_order_switch_scripted_effect = {
	switch = {
		trigger = yes
		scope:cardinal_improve_development ?= {
			capital_county.clerical_region_title = {
				every_county_in_clerical_region = {
					custom = cardinal_order_improve_development
					limit = {
						faith = scope:recipient.faith
					}
					add_county_modifier = { 
						modifier = pam_cardinal_order_improve_development_modifier
						years = 10
					}
					holder = {
						if = {
							limit = {
								is_ai = no
							}
							add_to_list = county_holders
						}	
					}
				}
			}
		}

		scope:cardinal_convert_faith ?= {
			capital_county ?= {
				clerical_region_title ?= {
					every_county_in_clerical_region = {
						custom = cardinal_order_convert_faith
						limit = {
							pam_cardinal_order_convert_faith_in_region_county_trigger = { CARDINAL = scope:recipient }
						}
						add_county_modifier = { 
							modifier = pam_cardinal_order_convert_faith_modifier
							years = 10
						}
						holder = {
							if = {
								limit = {
									is_ai = no
								}
								add_to_list = county_holders
							}	
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							any_county_in_clerical_region = {
								any_neighboring_county = {
									pam_cardinal_order_convert_faith_neighboring_county_trigger = { CARDINAL = scope:recipient }
								}
							}
						}
						custom_tooltip = {
							text = cardinal_order_convert_faith_neighboring
							every_county_in_clerical_region = {
								every_neighboring_county = {
									limit = {
										pam_cardinal_order_convert_faith_neighboring_county_trigger = { CARDINAL = scope:recipient }
										NOT = { has_county_modifier = pam_cardinal_order_convert_faith_modifier }
									}
									add_county_modifier = { 
										modifier = pam_cardinal_order_convert_faith_modifier
										years = 10
									}
									holder = {
										if = {
											limit = {
												is_ai = no
											}
											add_to_list = county_holders
										}	
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}

		scope:cardinal_increase_control ?= {
			capital_county.clerical_region_title = {
				every_county_in_clerical_region = {
					custom = cardinal_order_increase_control
					limit = {
						holder.top_liege.faith = scope:recipient.faith
					}
					add_county_modifier = { 
						modifier = pam_cardinal_order_increase_control_modifier
						years = 10
					}
					holder = {
						if = {
							limit = {
								is_ai = no
							}
							add_to_list = county_holders
						}	
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		scope:cardinal_accelerate_cultural_fascination ?= {
			scope:puppet_or_actor.culture.culture_head = {
				custom_tooltip = cardinal_order_accelerate_cultural_fascination
				add_character_modifier = { 
					modifier = pam_cardinal_order_accelerate_cultural_fascination_modifier
					years = 10
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_ai = no
					}
					add_to_list = county_holders
				}	
			}
		}

		scope:cardinal_improve_epidemic_resistance ?= {
			capital_county.clerical_region_title = {
				every_county_in_clerical_region = {
					custom = cardinal_order_improve_epidemic_resistance
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_county_modifier = pam_cardinal_order_improve_epidemic_resistance_modifier
						}
						faith = scope:recipient.faith
					}
					add_county_modifier = {
						modifier = pam_cardinal_order_improve_epidemic_resistance_modifier
						years = 10
					}
					holder = {
						if = {
							limit = {
								is_ai = no
							}
							add_to_list = county_holders
						}	
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		scope:cardinal_improve_legitimacy ?= {
			capital_county.clerical_region_title = {
				every_county_in_clerical_region = {
					custom = cardinal_order_improve_legitimacy
					limit = {
						holder.top_liege = {
							has_legitimacy = yes
							NOT = {
								has_character_modifier = pam_cardinal_order_improve_legitimacy_modifier
							}
							faith = scope:recipient.faith
						}
					}
					holder.top_liege = {
						add_character_modifier = { 
							modifier = pam_cardinal_order_improve_legitimacy_modifier
							years = 10
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
								is_ai = no
							}
							add_to_list = county_holders
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}

		scope:cardinal_undermine_legitimacy ?= {
			capital_county.clerical_region_title = {
				every_county_in_clerical_region = {
					custom = cardinal_order_undermine_legitimacy
					limit = {
						holder.top_liege = {
							has_legitimacy = yes
							NOT = {
								has_character_modifier = pam_cardinal_order_undermine_legitimacy_modifier
							}
							faith = scope:recipient.faith
						}
					}
					holder.top_liege = {
						add_character_modifier = { 
							modifier = pam_cardinal_order_undermine_legitimacy_modifier
							years = 10
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
								is_ai = no
							}
							add_to_list = county_holders
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

pam_cardinal_order_accept_notify_players_effect = {
	$HOF$ = {
		if = {
			limit = { is_ai = no }
			hidden_effect = {
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_effect_neutral
					title = give_cardinal_order_interaction_toast
					left_icon = $HOF$
					right_icon = scope:recipient
					scope:recipient = {
						show_as_tooltip = {
							pam_choose_cardinal_order_switch_scripted_effect = yes
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:actor != $HOF$
			scope:actor = { is_ai = no }
		}
		scope:actor = {
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_neutral
				title = give_cardinal_order_interaction_toast
				left_icon = $HOF$
				right_icon = scope:recipient
				scope:recipient = {
					show_as_tooltip = {
						pam_choose_cardinal_order_switch_scripted_effect = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	every_in_list = {
		list = county_holders
		limit = {
			NOT = { this = $HOF$ }
			NOT = { this = scope:actor }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_neutral
				title = give_cardinal_order_interaction_toast
				left_icon = $HOF$
				right_icon = scope:recipient
				scope:recipient = {
					show_as_tooltip = {
						pam_choose_cardinal_order_switch_scripted_effect = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

pam_cardinal_order_decline_effect = {
	$CARDINAL$ = {
		add_prestige = medium_prestige_gain
		add_piety = medium_piety_loss
		if = {
			limit = {
				$HOF$ = { is_alive = yes }
				can_set_relation_rival_trigger = { CHARACTER = $HOF$ }
			}
			progress_towards_rival_effect = {
				REASON = rival_ignored_orders
				CHARACTER = $HOF$
				OPINION = default_rival_opinion
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				$HOF$ = { is_alive = yes }
			}
			add_opinion = {
				target = $HOF$
				modifier = disrespect_opinion
				opinion = -60
			}
		}
	}
}

# Shared handler for the offer_pilgrimage send option: ACTOR promises the pilgrimage, CLERIC is rewarded
pam_offer_pilgrimage_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:offer_pilgrimage ?= yes
		}
		$ACTOR$ = {
			add_character_modifier = {
				modifier = promised_pilgrimage_to_clergy_modifier
				years = 10
			}
			custom_tooltip = actor_promised_pilgrimage_tt
		}
		$CLERIC$ = {
			add_piety_experience = {
				value = minor_piety_gain
				add = {
					value = $ACTOR$.highest_held_title_tier
					subtract = 1.5
					multiply = 20
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

request_legitimacy_interaction_effect = {
	add_legitimacy = {
		value = medium_legitimacy_gain
		multiply = 1.5
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:monastic_support = yes
			}
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				every_patroned_holy_order = {
					limit = {
						leader = {
							government_has_flag = government_is_monastic_holy_order
						}
					}
					add = minor_legitimacy_gain
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
					has_personal_tenet_flag = tenet_apostolic_succession_papal_legitimacy
					faith.religious_head ?= scope:recipient
				}
			}
			add = medium_legitimacy_gain
		}
		max = massive_legitimacy_gain
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			rite = {
				has_tenet_status = {
					tenet = tenet_simony
					status = prohibited
				}
			}
		}
		change_spiritual_fulfillment = medium_spiritual_fulfillment_loss
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_vassal = {
				NOT = {
					faith = scope:puppet_or_actor.faith
				}
			}
		}
		every_vassal = {
			custom = custom.every_astray_faith_vassal
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					faith = scope:puppet_or_actor.faith
				}
				faith = {
					faith_hostility_level = {
						target = scope:puppet_or_actor.faith
						value >= faith_astray_level
					}
				}
			}
			add_opinion = {
				target = scope:puppet_or_actor
				modifier = heathen_mandate_opinion
				opinion = -20
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_held_title = {
				tier = tier_county
				NOT = {
					faith = scope:puppet_or_actor.faith
				}
				faith = {
					faith_hostility_level = {
						target = scope:puppet_or_actor.faith
						value >= faith_astray_level
					}
				}
			}
		}
		every_held_title = {
			custom = custom.every_astray_faith_domain_county
			limit = {
				tier = tier_county
				NOT = {
					faith = scope:puppet_or_actor.faith
				}
				faith = {
					faith_hostility_level = {
						target = scope:puppet_or_actor.faith
						value >= faith_astray_level
					}
				}
			}
			add_county_modifier = {
				modifier = heathen_mandate_modifier
				years = 5
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:monastic_support = yes
		}
		every_patroned_holy_order = {
			limit = {
				leader = {
					government_has_flag = government_is_monastic_holy_order
				}
			}
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				pay_short_term_gold = {
					target = prev.leader
					gold = minor_gold_value
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:tithe = yes
		}
		every_held_title = {
			limit = {
				tier = tier_county
				NOT = {
					any_county_province = {
						is_occupied = yes
					}
				}
				development_level >= 1
			}
			add_to_list = tithed_counties
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			add_gold = {
				value = 0
				every_in_list = {
					list = tithed_counties
					add = scope:recipient.miniscule_gold_value
					add = {
						value = development_level
					}
				}
				max = monumental_gold_value
			}
		}
		every_in_list = {
			list = tithed_counties
			custom = custom.every_domain_county_1_plus_development
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:recipient.culture = {
						has_cultural_era_or_later = culture_era_late_medieval
					}
				}
				change_development_progress = massive_development_progress_loss
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					scope:recipient.culture = {
						has_cultural_era_or_later = culture_era_high_medieval
					}
				}
				change_development_progress = major_development_progress_loss
			}
			else = {
				change_development_progress = medium_development_progress_loss
			}
		}
		#Secretly take gold from wealthy-ass barons
		hidden_effect = {
			every_in_list = {
				list = tithed_counties
				every_county_province = {
					limit = {
						barony.holder ?= {
							highest_held_title_tier = tier_barony
							is_ai = yes
							gold > medium_gold_value
						}
					}
					barony.holder ?= {
						remove_short_term_gold = medium_gold_value
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_vassal = {
					has_vassal_stance = barons_and_minor_landholders
				}
			}
			every_vassal = {
				custom = custom.every_barons_and_minor_landholders_vassal
				limit = {
					has_vassal_stance = barons_and_minor_landholders
				}
				add_opinion = {
					modifier = tithed_opinion
					target = scope:puppet_or_actor
					opinion = -25
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = tithe_takes_baron_gold_tt
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:donate_faith = yes
		}
		pay_short_term_gold = {
			target = scope:recipient
			gold = scope:puppet_or_actor.major_gold_value
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:use_hook = yes
			has_usable_hook = scope:recipient
		}
		use_hook = scope:recipient
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:offer_hook = yes
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			add_hook = {
				type = favor_hook
				target = scope:puppet_or_actor
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_ai = yes
		}
		add_opinion = {
			target = scope:recipient
			modifier = grateful_opinion
			opinion = 30
		}
	}
	pam_offer_pilgrimage_effect = {
		ACTOR = scope:puppet_or_actor
		CLERIC = scope:recipient
	}
	#The cleric should lose spiritual fulfillment if they agree to supporting a wicked king or a tyrant
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				character_considered_wicked_trigger = { RITE = scope:recipient.rite }
				scope:recipient = {
					has_relation_rival = scope:puppet_or_actor
				}
				tyranny > medium_tyranny
				has_trait = excommunicated
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			# We never take more piety than the cleric has, or the Church goes into debt
			add_piety = {
				value = major_piety_value
				multiply = {
					value = scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier
					subtract = 1
				}
				max = piety
				min = 0
				multiply = -1
			}
			change_spiritual_fulfillment = massive_spiritual_fulfillment_loss
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:puppet_or_actor = { has_trait = excommunicated }
				}
				custom_tooltip = sp_ful_loss_due_to_excommunicated
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_relation_rival = scope:puppet_or_actor
				}
				custom_tooltip = sp_ful_loss_due_to_rival
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					scope:puppet_or_actor = { tyranny > medium_tyranny }
				}
				custom_tooltip = sp_ful_loss_due_to_tyranny
			}
			else_if = {
				custom_tooltip = sp_ful_loss_due_to_wicked
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				character_considered_morally_dubious_trigger = { RITE = scope:recipient.rite }
				tyranny >= low_tyranny
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			add_piety = {
				value = minor_piety_value
				multiply = {
					value = scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier
					subtract = 1
				}
				max = piety
				min = 0
				multiply = -1
			}
			change_spiritual_fulfillment = medium_spiritual_fulfillment_loss
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:puppet_or_actor = { tyranny >= low_tyranny }
				}
				custom_tooltip = sp_ful_loss_due_to_tyranny
			}
			else = {
				custom_tooltip = sp_ful_loss_due_to_immoral
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		scope:recipient = {
			add_piety = {
				value = miniscule_piety_value
				multiply = {
					value = scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier
					subtract = 1
				}
				max = piety
				min = 0
				multiply = -1
			}
		}
	}
	# Catalysts
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor.religion = like_christianity_religion_trigger = yes
			situation:the_christian_church ?= { situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_the_christian_church_requested_legitimacy }
		}
		situation:the_christian_church = { trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_the_christian_church_requested_legitimacy }
	}
}
#Add variable that makes title grant recipient (who is claimant) satisfied with claim-granting for a bit
claimant_jealousy_immunity_effect = {
	custom_tooltip = satisfied_claim_granting_tt
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:highest_claim.tier = tier_hegemony
		}
		add_to_variable_list = {
			name = hegemony_grant
			target = scope:puppet_or_actor
			years = 3
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			scope:highest_claim.tier = tier_empire
		}
		add_to_variable_list = {
			name = empire_grant
			target = scope:puppet_or_actor
			years = 3
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			scope:highest_claim.tier = tier_kingdom
		}
		add_to_variable_list = {
			name = kingdom_grant
			target = scope:puppet_or_actor
			years = 3
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			scope:highest_claim.tier = tier_duchy
		}
		add_to_variable_list = {
			name = duchy_grant
			target = scope:puppet_or_actor
			years = 3
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			scope:highest_claim.tier = tier_county
		}
		add_to_variable_list = {
			name = county_grant
			target = scope:puppet_or_actor
			years = 3
		}
	}
}
claimant_rivalry_mercy_effect = {
	custom_tooltip = strong_claim_given_away_rivalry_tt
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:highest_denied_claim = {
				tier >= tier_hegemony
			}
		}
		add_to_variable_list = {
			name = denied_hegemony
			target = scope:puppet_or_actor
			days = 31
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			scope:highest_denied_claim = {
				tier >= tier_empire
			}
		}
		add_to_variable_list = {
			name = denied_empire
			target = scope:puppet_or_actor
			days = 31
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			scope:highest_denied_claim = {
				tier >= tier_kingdom
			}
		}
		add_to_variable_list = {
			name = denied_kingdom
			target = scope:puppet_or_actor
			days = 31
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			scope:highest_denied_claim = {
				tier >= tier_duchy
			}
		}
		add_to_variable_list = {
			name = denied_duchy
			target = scope:puppet_or_actor
			days = 31
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			scope:highest_denied_claim = {
				tier >= tier_county
			}
		}
		add_to_variable_list = {
			name = denied_county
			target = scope:puppet_or_actor
			days = 31
		}
	}
	trigger_event = {
		id = pam_interactions_events.0040
		days = 30
	}
}
#Opinion loss with underlings who think they should get the title you're granting
title_granting_controversy_effect = {
	#populate vassal list
	every_vassal_or_below = {
		limit = {
			NOR = {
				this = scope:recipient
				this = $TITLE$.holder
				is_in_list = eligible_vassals
			}
			highest_held_title_tier > tier_barony
			#Vassal granting should only annoy people who could actually receive vassal
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:secondary_actor
				}
				highest_held_title_tier > $TITLE$.tier
			}
			#Children can't hold titles in admin govs
			trigger_if = {
				limit = {
					government_has_mechanic = administrative
				}
				is_adult = yes
				#Admin de jure/neighboring thing is a pain in the butt but should be acknowledged
				trigger_if = {
					limit = {
						is_ruler = yes
					}
					any_held_title = {
						OR = {
							# The target title is part of your de jure structure
							is_de_jure_liege_or_above_target = $TITLE$
							target_is_de_jure_liege_or_above = $TITLE$
						}
					}
				}
			}
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				is_character_interaction_valid = {
					recipient = prev
					interaction = $INTERACTION$
				}
			}
			#Shouldn't lose opinion when giving title to their child or parent
			NOR = {
				AND = {
					is_child_of = scope:recipient
					is_heir_of = scope:recipient
				}
				AND = {
					is_parent_of = scope:recipient
					exists = scope:recipient.house
					house ?= scope:recipient.house
				}
			}
		}
		add_to_list = eligible_vassals
	}
	#Also show effect on courtier claimants you actually care about (IE your family members)
	$TITLE$ = {
		every_claimant = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { this = scope:recipient }
				NOT = {
					is_in_list = eligible_vassals
				}
				#Vassal granting should only annoy people who could actually receive vassal
				trigger_if = {
					limit = {
						exists = scope:secondary_actor
					}
					highest_held_title_tier > $TITLE$.tier
				}
				trigger_if = {
					limit = {
						is_ruler = no
					}
					OR = {
						rite = {
							rite_has_parameter = gender_equal_law
						}
						AND = {
							is_male = yes
							rite = {
								rite_has_parameter = male_dominated_law
							}
						}
						AND = {
							is_female = yes
							rite = {
								rite_has_parameter = female_dominated_law
							}
						}
					}
				}
				in_diplomatic_range = scope:puppet_or_actor
				NOT = { has_strong_implicit_claim_on = $TITLE$ }
				any_claim = {
					pressed = yes
					this = $TITLE$
				}
				any_liege_or_above = {
					this = scope:puppet_or_actor
				}
				OR = {
					any_close_family_member = {
						this = scope:puppet_or_actor
					}
					AND = {
						exists = scope:puppet_or_actor.house
						house ?= scope:puppet_or_actor.house
					}
					any_spouse = {
						this = scope:puppet_or_actor
					}
					has_any_good_relationship_with_character_trigger = { CHARACTER = scope:puppet_or_actor }
				}
				#Children can't hold titles in admin govs
				trigger_if = {
					limit = {
						government_has_mechanic = administrative
					}
					is_adult = yes
					#Admin de jure/neighboring thing is a pain in the butt but should be acknowledged
					trigger_if = {
						limit = {
							is_ruler = yes
						}
						any_held_title = {
							OR = {
								# The target title is part of your de jure structure
								is_de_jure_liege_or_above_target = $TITLE$
								target_is_de_jure_liege_or_above = $TITLE$
							}
						}
					}
				}
				scope:puppet_or_actor = {
					is_character_interaction_valid = {
						recipient = prev
						interaction = $INTERACTION$
					}
				}
				#Shouldn't lose opinion when giving title to their child or parent
				NOR = {
					AND = {
						is_child_of = scope:recipient
						is_heir_of = scope:recipient
					}
					AND = {
						is_parent_of = scope:recipient
						exists = scope:recipient.house
						house ?= scope:recipient.house
					}
				}
			}
			add_to_list = eligible_vassals
		}
	}

	#Higher repercussions for higher tier
	#Higher repercussions for pressed claims than unpressed
	if = {
		limit = {
			$TITLE$ = {
				tier >= tier_kingdom
			}
		}
		every_in_list = {
			list = eligible_vassals
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_strong_implicit_claim_on = $TITLE$ }
				has_claim_on = $TITLE$
				#Wasn't granted a claim of same tier or better in recent memory
				NOR = {
					any_in_list = {
						variable = hegemony_grant
						this = scope:puppet_or_actor
					}
					AND = {
						$TITLE$.tier <= tier_empire
						any_in_list = {
							variable = empire_grant
							this = scope:puppet_or_actor
						}
					}
					AND = {
						$TITLE$.tier <= tier_kingdom
						any_in_list = {
							variable = kingdom_grant
							this = scope:puppet_or_actor
						}
					}
					AND = {
						$TITLE$.tier <= tier_duchy
						any_in_list = {
							variable = duchy_grant
							this = scope:puppet_or_actor
						}
					}
					AND = {
						$TITLE$.tier <= tier_county
						any_in_list = {
							variable = county_grant
							this = scope:puppet_or_actor
						}
					}
				}
			}
			#pressed claim
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_strong_claim_on = $TITLE$
				}
				add_to_list = strong_claimants
				add_opinion = {
					target = scope:puppet_or_actor
					modifier = gave_away_my_pressed_claim_opinion
					opinion = -50
				}
			}
			#unpressed claim
			else = {
				add_opinion = {
					target = scope:puppet_or_actor
					modifier = gave_away_my_unpressed_claim_opinion
					opinion = -30
				}
			}
		}
		#Upset they didn't get de jure vassal title
		every_in_list = {
			list = eligible_vassals
			limit = {
				any_held_title = {
					any_in_de_jure_hierarchy = {
						this = $TITLE$
					}
				}
			}
			#Less opinion loss if you're giving it to one of their vassals
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						exists = scope:secondary_actor
						any_vassal_or_below = {
							this = scope:recipient
						}
					}
				}
				add_opinion = {
					target = scope:puppet_or_actor
					modifier = gave_away_de_jure_vassal_title_opinion
					opinion = -25
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_opinion = {
					target = scope:puppet_or_actor
					modifier = gave_away_de_jure_vassal_title_opinion
					opinion = -40
				}
			}
		}
		#De Jure vassals upset they didn't get the title
		every_in_list = {
			list = eligible_vassals
			limit = {
				any_held_title = {
					tier >= $TITLE$.tier_minus_1
					any_this_title_or_de_jure_above = {
					 	this = $TITLE$
					}
				}
			}
			#Less opinion loss if you're giving it to their liege or above
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_liege_or_above = {
						this = scope:recipient
					}
				}
				add_opinion = {
					target = scope:puppet_or_actor
					modifier = gave_away_de_jure_liege_title_opinion
					opinion = -20
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_opinion = {
					target = scope:puppet_or_actor
					modifier = gave_away_de_jure_liege_title_opinion
					opinion = -30
				}
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			#Either title is a duchy or we're doing vassal transfer, not title granting
			OR = {
				$TITLE$ = {
					tier >= tier_duchy
				}
				exists = scope:secondary_actor
			}
		}
		every_in_list = {
			list = eligible_vassals
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_strong_implicit_claim_on = $TITLE$ }
				any_claim = {
					this = $TITLE$
					pressed = yes
				}
			}
			add_opinion = {
				target = scope:puppet_or_actor
				modifier = gave_away_my_pressed_claim_opinion
				opinion = -40
			}
		}
		every_in_list = {
			list = eligible_vassals
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_strong_implicit_claim_on = $TITLE$ }
				any_claim = {
					this = $TITLE$
					pressed = no
				}
			}
			add_opinion = {
				target = scope:puppet_or_actor
				modifier = gave_away_my_unpressed_claim_opinion
				opinion = -20
			}
		}
		#Upset they didn't get de jure vassal title
		every_in_list = {
			list = eligible_vassals
			limit = {
				any_held_title = {
					any_in_de_jure_hierarchy = {
						this = $TITLE$
					}
				}
			}
			#Less opinion loss if you're giving it to one of their vassals or it's a vassal transfer
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						exists = scope:secondary_actor
						any_vassal_or_below = {
							this = scope:recipient
						}
					}
				}
				add_opinion = {
					target = scope:puppet_or_actor
					modifier = gave_away_de_jure_vassal_title_opinion
					opinion = -20
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_opinion = {
					target = scope:puppet_or_actor
					modifier = gave_away_de_jure_vassal_title_opinion
					opinion = -30
				}
			}
		}
		#De Jure vassals upset they didn't get the title
		every_in_list = {
			list = eligible_vassals
			limit = {
				any_held_title = {
					tier >= $TITLE$.tier_minus_1
					any_this_title_or_de_jure_above = {
					 	this = $TITLE$
					}
				}
			}
			#Less opinion loss if you're giving it to their liege or above
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_liege_or_above = {
						this = scope:recipient
					}
				}
				add_opinion = {
					target = scope:puppet_or_actor
					modifier = gave_away_de_jure_liege_title_opinion
					opinion = -15
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_opinion = {
					target = scope:puppet_or_actor
					modifier = gave_away_de_jure_liege_title_opinion
					opinion = -20
				}
			}
		}
	}
	#Don't want to impose much mental load on giving away counties
	#PLUS you can give them away with the "give to random noble" button without repercussions
	#So we'll only do de jure lieges getting mad you are purposefully favoring another lord or courtier over them
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			$TITLE$ = { tier >= tier_county }
		}
		#Upset they didn't get de jure vassal title
		every_in_list = {
			list = eligible_vassals
			limit = {
				any_held_title = {
					any_in_de_jure_hierarchy = {
						this = $TITLE$
					}
				}
				NOT = {
					any_vassal_or_below = {
						this = scope:recipient
					}
				}
			}
			add_opinion = {
				target = scope:puppet_or_actor
				modifier = gave_away_de_jure_vassal_title_opinion
				opinion = -15
			}
		}
	}
}
strengthen_claim_interaction_effect = {
	scope:puppet_or_actor = { add_pressed_claim = scope:target }
	scope:recipient = { save_scope_as = cleric }
	scope:target.holder ?= { save_scope_as = owner }
	#Let's figure out how morally wrong the cleric feels this is
	#Either your shittiness or target's coolness has given the clergyman pause
	if = {
		limit = {
			clerical_hesitation_claim_interaction_value >= 9
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = yes
					piety <= 0
				}
				show_as_tooltip = {
					add_piety = {
						value = major_piety_loss
						multiply = {
							value = scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier
							subtract = 1
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_piety = {
					value = major_piety_loss
					multiply = {
						value = scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier
						subtract = 1
					}
				}
			}
			change_spiritual_fulfillment = massive_spiritual_fulfillment_loss
			if = {
				limit = {
					root ?= scope:cleric
				}
				custom_tooltip = clerical_hesitation_high_player_tt
			}
			else = {
				custom_tooltip = clerical_hesitation_high_tt
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			clerical_hesitation_claim_interaction_value >= 6
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = yes
					piety <= 0
				}
				show_as_tooltip = {
					add_piety = {
						value = medium_piety_loss
						multiply = {
							value = scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier
							subtract = 1
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_piety = {
					value = medium_piety_loss
					multiply = {
						value = scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier
						subtract = 1
					}
				}
			}
			change_spiritual_fulfillment = major_spiritual_fulfillment_loss
			if = {
				limit = {
					root ?= scope:cleric
				}
				custom_tooltip = clerical_hesitation_high_player_tt
			}
			else = {
				custom_tooltip = clerical_hesitation_high_tt
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			clerical_hesitation_claim_interaction_value >= 3
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = yes
					piety <= 0
				}
				show_as_tooltip = {
					add_piety = {
						value = minor_piety_loss
						multiply = {
							value = scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier
							subtract = 1
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_piety = {
					value = minor_piety_loss
					multiply = {
						value = scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier
						subtract = 1
					}
				}
			}
			change_spiritual_fulfillment = medium_spiritual_fulfillment_loss
			if = {
				limit = {
					root ?= scope:cleric
				}
				custom_tooltip = clerical_hesitation_high_player_tt
			}
			else = {
				custom_tooltip = clerical_hesitation_high_tt
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			clerical_hesitation_claim_interaction_value < 3
			clerical_hesitation_claim_interaction_value >= -2
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			add_piety = {
				value = miniscule_piety_loss
				multiply = {
					value = scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier
					subtract = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			clerical_hesitation_claim_interaction_value < -2
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = yes
					piety <= 0
				}
				show_as_tooltip = {
					add_piety = {
						value = miniscule_piety_loss
						multiply = {
							value = scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier
							subtract = 1
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_piety = {
					value = miniscule_piety_loss
					multiply = {
						value = scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier
						subtract = 1
					}
				}
			}
			change_spiritual_fulfillment = miniscule_spiritual_fulfillment_value
			if = {
				limit = {
					root ?= scope:cleric
				}
				custom_tooltip = clerical_hesitation_low_player_tt
			}
			else = {
				custom_tooltip = clerical_hesitation_low_tt
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			clerical_hesitation_claim_interaction_value >= -5
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = yes
					piety <= 0
				}
				show_as_tooltip = {
					add_piety = {
						value = miniscule_piety_loss
						multiply = {
							value = scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier
							subtract = 1
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_piety = {
					value = miniscule_piety_loss
					multiply = {
						value = scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier
						subtract = 1
					}
				}
			}
			change_spiritual_fulfillment = minor_spiritual_fulfillment_value
			if = {
				limit = {
					root ?= scope:cleric
				}
				custom_tooltip = clerical_hesitation_low_player_tt
			}
			else = {
				custom_tooltip = clerical_hesitation_low_tt
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			clerical_hesitation_claim_interaction_value >= -8
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = yes
					piety <= 0
				}
				show_as_tooltip = {
					add_piety = {
						value = miniscule_piety_loss
						multiply = {
							value = scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier
							subtract = 1
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_piety = {
					value = miniscule_piety_loss
					multiply = {
						value = scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier
						subtract = 1
					}
				}
			}
			change_spiritual_fulfillment = medium_spiritual_fulfillment_value
			if = {
				limit = {
					root ?= scope:cleric
				}
				custom_tooltip = clerical_hesitation_low_player_tt
			}
			else = {
				custom_tooltip = clerical_hesitation_low_tt
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		scope:recipient = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = yes
					piety <= 0
				}
				show_as_tooltip = {
					add_piety = {
						value = miniscule_piety_loss
						multiply = {
							value = scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier
							subtract = 1
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_piety = {
					value = miniscule_piety_loss
					multiply = {
						value = scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier
						subtract = 1
					}
				}
			}
			change_spiritual_fulfillment = major_spiritual_fulfillment_value
			if = {
				limit = {
					root ?= scope:cleric
				}
				custom_tooltip = clerical_hesitation_low_player_tt
			}
			else = {
				custom_tooltip = clerical_hesitation_low_tt
			}
		}
	}
	scope:target.holder ?= {
		add_opinion = {
			target = scope:puppet_or_actor
			modifier = church_claim_modifier
			opinion = -40
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:recipient = {
					is_ai = yes
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				add_opinion = {
					target = scope:recipient
					modifier = church_claim_modifier
					opinion = -30
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = scope:recipient
				modifier = church_claim_modifier
				opinion = -30
			}
		}
	}
	#Opinion loss with title's de jure liege - why you trying to steal this when it should be under me?
	scope:target = {
		every_this_title_or_de_jure_above = {
			limit = {
				exists = holder
				NOR = {
					this = scope:target
					holder = {
						OR = {
							this = scope:puppet_or_actor
							this = scope:recipient
							this = scope:target.holder
							#actor's liege being mad at them for contesting other vassals is kinda wrong
							is_liege_or_above_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_direct_de_jure_vassal_title = {
						this = scope:target
					}
				}
				holder = { add_to_list = de_jure_direct_lieges }
			}
			else = {
				holder = { add_to_list = de_jure_higher_lieges }
			}
		}
		every_in_list = {
			list = de_jure_direct_lieges
			#More loss for direct liege
			add_opinion = {
				target = scope:puppet_or_actor
				modifier = pressed_church_claim_de_jure_liege_modifier
				opinion = -25
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:recipient = {
						is_ai = yes
					}
				}
				hidden_effect = {
					add_opinion = {
						target = scope:recipient
						modifier = pressed_church_claim_de_jure_liege_modifier
						opinion = -10
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_opinion = {
					target = scope:recipient
					modifier = pressed_church_claim_de_jure_liege_modifier
					opinion = -10
				}
			}
		}
		every_in_list = {
			list = de_jure_higher_lieges
			limit = {
				NOT = { is_in_list = de_jure_direct_lieges }
			}
			add_opinion = {
				target = scope:puppet_or_actor
				modifier = pressed_church_claim_de_jure_liege_modifier
				opinion = -15
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:recipient = {
						is_ai = yes
					}
				}
				hidden_effect = {
					add_opinion = {
						target = scope:recipient
						modifier = pressed_church_claim_de_jure_liege_modifier
						opinion = -5
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_opinion = {
					target = scope:recipient
					modifier = pressed_church_claim_de_jure_liege_modifier
					opinion = -5
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_ai = yes
		}
		add_opinion = {
			target = scope:recipient
			modifier = pressed_my_claim
			opinion = {
				value = 10
				if = {
					limit = {
						highest_held_title_tier < scope:target.tier
					}
					multiply = 4
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						highest_held_title_tier = scope:target.tier
					}
					multiply = 3
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						highest_held_title_tier_minus_one = scope:target.tier
					}
					multiply = 2
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						highest_held_title_tier_minus_two = scope:target.tier
					}
					multiply = 1.5
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:donate_faith = yes
		}
		pay_short_term_gold = {
			target = scope:recipient
			gold = scope:puppet_or_actor.medium_gold_value
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:use_hook = yes
			has_usable_hook = scope:recipient
		}
		use_hook = scope:recipient
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:offer_hook = yes
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			add_hook = {
				type = favor_hook
				target = scope:puppet_or_actor
			}
		}
	}
	pam_offer_pilgrimage_effect = {
		ACTOR = scope:puppet_or_actor
		CLERIC = scope:recipient
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:threaten_priest = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:secondary_recipient = {
					this = scope:puppet_or_actor.faith.religious_head
				}
			}
			change_spiritual_fulfillment = massive_spiritual_fulfillment_loss
			add_piety_experience = massive_piety_loss
			custom_tooltip = hof_increase_piety_sp_fulfillment_loss_tt
		}
		else = {
			change_spiritual_fulfillment = major_spiritual_fulfillment_loss
			add_piety_experience = major_piety_loss
		}
		scope:secondary_recipient = {
			add_opinion = {
				modifier = threatened_church_opinion
				target = scope:puppet_or_actor
				opinion = -50
			}
		}
	}
	# Catalysts
	if = {
		limit = { # If you are strenghtening a claim against a Christian ruler
			scope:puppet_or_actor.religion = like_christianity_religion_trigger = yes
			scope:target.holder.religion ?= like_christianity_religion_trigger = yes
			situation:the_christian_church ?= { situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_the_christian_church_strenghtened_claim }
		}
		situation:the_christian_church = { trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_the_christian_church_strenghtened_claim }
	}
}

sell_claim_interaction_effect = {
	scope:recipient = { add_unpressed_claim = scope:target }
	# cost = { piety } is only charged to scope:actor, so a puppeteer's cleric pays here instead
	if = {
		limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			add_piety = {
				value = {
					add = pam_sell_claim_piety_cost_value
					max = piety
					multiply = -1
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { scope:sell_claim_gold_and_hook = yes }
		send_interface_toast = {
			title = sell_claim_interaction_effect_tt
			left_icon = scope:target
			right_icon = scope:recipient
			scope:recipient = {
				pay_short_term_gold = {
					target = scope:puppet_or_actor
					gold = minor_gold_value
				}
			}
		}
		scope:puppet_or_actor = { # Generates its own toast
			if = {
				limit = { has_hook = scope:recipient }
				custom_tooltip = already_has_hook_on_recipient
			}
			add_hook = {
				type = favor_hook
				target = scope:recipient
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			send_interface_toast = {
				title = sell_claim_interaction_effect_tt
				left_icon = scope:target
				right_icon = scope:recipient
				scope:recipient = {
					pay_short_term_gold = {
						target = scope:puppet_or_actor
						gold = pam_sell_claim_gold_cost_value
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes
			scope:actor != scope:recipient
		}
		scope:actor = {
			send_interface_toast = {
				title = sell_claim_interaction_effect_tt_puppeteer
				left_icon = scope:puppet_or_actor
				right_icon = scope:recipient
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = { has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes }
			situation:the_christian_church ?= {
				situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_clergy_sold_claim
			}
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				any_character_situation = {
					this = situation:the_christian_church
				}
			}
		}
		situation:the_christian_church = {
			trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_clergy_sold_claim
		}
	}
}

gift_claim_interaction_effect = {
	send_interface_toast = {
		type = event_toast_effect_neutral
		title = gift_claim_interaction_effect_tt
		left_icon = scope:target
		right_icon = scope:recipient
		remove_claim = scope:target
		scope:recipient = {
			add_unpressed_claim = scope:target
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:actor != scope:puppet_or_actor
			scope:actor != scope:recipient
		}
		scope:actor = {
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_neutral
				title = gift_claim_interaction_effect_tt_puppeteer
				left_icon = scope:puppet_or_actor
				right_icon = scope:recipient
			}
		}
	}
}

ecclesiastic_grant_claim_interaction_effect = {
	add_pressed_claim = scope:target
	scope:secondary_recipient = { save_scope_as = cleric }
	scope:recipient = { save_scope_as = owner }
	#Either your shittiness or target's coolness has given the clergyman pause
	if = {
		limit = {
			clerical_hesitation_claim_interaction_value >= 9
		}
		scope:secondary_recipient = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = yes
					piety <= 0
				}
				show_as_tooltip = {
					add_piety = {
						value = major_piety_loss
						multiply = {
							value = scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier
							subtract = 1
						}
					}
				}

			else = {

					value = major_piety_loss
					multiply = {
						value = scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier
						subtract = 1
					}
				}

				limit = {
					root ?= scope:cleric

				custom_tooltip = clerical_hesitation_high_tt

	else_if = {

					value = minor_piety_loss

			}
			change_spiritual_fulfillment = medium_spiritual_fulfillment_loss
			if = {
