				custom_tooltip = clerical_hesitation_high_player_tt
			}
			else = {
				custom_tooltip = clerical_hesitation_high_tt
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			clerical_hesitation_claim_interaction_value < 3
			clerical_hesitation_claim_interaction_value >= -2
		}
		scope:secondary_recipient = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = yes
					piety <= 0
				}
				show_as_tooltip = {
					add_piety = {
						value = miniscule_piety_loss
						multiply = {
							value = scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier
							subtract = 1
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_piety = {
					value = miniscule_piety_loss
					multiply = {
						value = scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier
						subtract = 1
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			clerical_hesitation_claim_interaction_value < -2
		}
		scope:secondary_recipient = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = yes
					piety <= 0
				}
				show_as_tooltip = {
					add_piety = {
						value = miniscule_piety_loss
						multiply = {
							value = scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier
							subtract = 1
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_piety = {
					value = miniscule_piety_loss
					multiply = {
						value = scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier
						subtract = 1
					}
				}
			}
			change_spiritual_fulfillment = miniscule_spiritual_fulfillment_value
			if = {
				limit = {
					root ?= scope:cleric
				}
				custom_tooltip = clerical_hesitation_low_player_tt
			}
			else = {
				custom_tooltip = clerical_hesitation_low_tt
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			clerical_hesitation_claim_interaction_value >= -5
		}
		scope:secondary_recipient = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = yes
					piety <= 0
				}
				show_as_tooltip = {
					add_piety = {
						value = miniscule_piety_loss
						multiply = {
							value = scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier
							subtract = 1
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_piety = {
					value = miniscule_piety_loss
					multiply = {
						value = scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier
						subtract = 1
					}
				}
			}
			change_spiritual_fulfillment = minor_spiritual_fulfillment_value
			if = {
				limit = {
					root ?= scope:cleric
				}
				custom_tooltip = clerical_hesitation_low_player_tt
			}
			else = {
				custom_tooltip = clerical_hesitation_low_tt
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			clerical_hesitation_claim_interaction_value >= -8
		}
		scope:secondary_recipient = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = yes
					piety <= 0
				}
				show_as_tooltip = {
					add_piety = {
						value = miniscule_piety_loss
						multiply = {
							value = scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier
							subtract = 1
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_piety = {
					value = miniscule_piety_loss
					multiply = {
						value = scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier
						subtract = 1
					}
				}
			}
			change_spiritual_fulfillment = medium_spiritual_fulfillment_value
			if = {
				limit = {
					root ?= scope:cleric
				}
				custom_tooltip = clerical_hesitation_low_player_tt
			}
			else = {
				custom_tooltip = clerical_hesitation_low_tt
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		scope:secondary_recipient = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = yes
					piety <= 0
				}
				show_as_tooltip = {
					add_piety = {
						value = miniscule_piety_loss
						multiply = {
							value = scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier
							subtract = 1
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_piety = {
					value = miniscule_piety_loss
					multiply = {
						value = scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier
						subtract = 1
					}
				}
			}
			change_spiritual_fulfillment = major_spiritual_fulfillment_value
			if = {
				limit = {
					root ?= scope:cleric
				}
				custom_tooltip = clerical_hesitation_low_player_tt
			}
			else = {
				custom_tooltip = clerical_hesitation_low_tt
			}
		}
	}
	scope:recipient ?= {
		add_opinion = {
			target = scope:puppet_or_actor
			modifier = claimed_my_title_opinion
			opinion = -60
		}
		progress_towards_rival_effect = {
			CHARACTER = scope:puppet_or_actor
			OPINION = 0
			REASON = rival_claimed_my_title
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = scope:secondary_recipient
				modifier = claimed_my_title_opinion
				opinion = -35
			}
		}
	}
	#Opinion loss with title's de jure liege - why you trying to steal this when it should be under me?
	scope:target = {
		every_this_title_or_de_jure_above = {
			limit = {
				exists = holder
				NOR = {
					this = scope:target
					holder = {
						OR = {
							this = scope:puppet_or_actor
							this = scope:recipient
							this = scope:target.holder
							#actor's liege being mad at them for contesting other vassals is kinda wrong
							is_liege_or_above_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_direct_de_jure_vassal_title = {
						this = scope:target
					}
				}
				holder = { add_to_list = de_jure_direct_lieges }
			}
			else = {
				holder = { add_to_list = de_jure_higher_lieges }
			}
		}
		every_in_list = {
			list = de_jure_direct_lieges
			#More loss for direct liege
			add_opinion = {
				target = scope:puppet_or_actor
				modifier = church_claim_de_jure_liege_modifier
				opinion = -25
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:secondary_recipient = { is_ai = yes }
				}
				hidden_effect = {
					add_opinion = {
						target = scope:secondary_recipient
						modifier = church_claim_de_jure_liege_modifier
						opinion = -10
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_opinion = {
					target = scope:secondary_recipient
					modifier = church_claim_de_jure_liege_modifier
					opinion = -10
				}
			}
		}
		every_in_list = {
			list = de_jure_higher_lieges
			limit = {
				NOT = { is_in_list = de_jure_direct_lieges }
			}
			add_opinion = {
				target = scope:puppet_or_actor
				modifier = church_claim_de_jure_liege_modifier
				opinion = -15
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:secondary_recipient = { is_ai = yes }
				}
				hidden_effect = {
					add_opinion = {
						target = scope:secondary_recipient
						modifier = church_claim_de_jure_liege_modifier
						opinion = -5
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_opinion = {
					target = scope:secondary_recipient
					modifier = church_claim_de_jure_liege_modifier
					opinion = -5
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_ai = yes
		}
		add_opinion = {
			target = scope:secondary_recipient
			modifier = granted_claim_opinion
			opinion = {
				value = 10
				if = {
					limit = {
						highest_held_title_tier < scope:target.tier
					}
					multiply = 5
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						highest_held_title_tier = scope:target.tier
					}
					multiply = 4
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						highest_held_title_tier_minus_one = scope:target.tier
					}
					multiply = 3
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						highest_held_title_tier_minus_two = scope:target.tier
					}
					multiply = 2
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:donate_faith = yes
		}
		pay_short_term_gold = {
			target = scope:secondary_recipient
			gold = scope:puppet_or_actor.medium_gold_value
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:use_hook = yes
			has_usable_hook = scope:secondary_recipient
		}
		use_hook = scope:secondary_recipient
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:offer_hook = yes
		}
		scope:secondary_recipient = {
			add_hook = {
				type = favor_hook
				target = scope:puppet_or_actor
			}
		}
	}
	pam_offer_pilgrimage_effect = {
		ACTOR = scope:puppet_or_actor
		CLERIC = scope:secondary_recipient
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:threaten_priest = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:secondary_recipient = {
					this = scope:puppet_or_actor.faith.religious_head
				}
			}
			change_spiritual_fulfillment = massive_spiritual_fulfillment_loss
			add_piety_experience = massive_piety_loss
			custom_tooltip = hof_increase_piety_sp_fulfillment_loss_secondary_tt
		}
		else = {
			change_spiritual_fulfillment = major_spiritual_fulfillment_loss
			add_piety_experience = major_piety_loss
		}
		scope:secondary_recipient = {
			add_opinion = {
				modifier = threatened_church_opinion
				target = scope:puppet_or_actor
				opinion = -50
			}
		}
	}
	# Catalysts
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor.religion = like_christianity_religion_trigger = yes
			scope:recipient.religion = like_christianity_religion_trigger = yes
			situation:the_christian_church ?= { situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_the_christian_church_ecclesiastical_grant_claim }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { scope:threaten_priest = yes }
			situation:the_christian_church = { trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_the_christian_church_threatened_ecclesiastical_grant_claim }
		}
		else = {
			situation:the_christian_church = { trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_the_christian_church_ecclesiastical_grant_claim }
		}
	}
}

# Called from on_personal_tenet_gain and on_piety_level_gain
reincarnation_piety_courage_gain_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_personal_tenet_flag = tenet_reincarnation_piety_level_courage
			piety_level >= high_piety_level
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = craven }
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = pam_reincarnation_courage_toast
				left_icon = root
				remove_trait = craven
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_trait = brave } }
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = pam_reincarnation_courage_toast
				left_icon = root
				add_trait = brave
			}
		}
	}
}

exaltation_of_pain_flagellant_gain_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_personal_tenet_flag = tenet_exaltation_of_pain_flagellant_trait
			NOT = { has_trait = flagellant }
		}
		add_trait = flagellant
	}
}

pam_kingdom_of_heaven_declaration_effect = {
	save_scope_as = kingdom_of_heaven_founder
	get_title = title:h_kingdom_of_heaven
	set_primary_title_to = title:h_kingdom_of_heaven
	set_realm_capital = title:c_jerusalem
	hidden_effect = {
		domicile ?= { move_domicile = root.capital_province }
	}
	dynasty ?= {
		add_dynasty_prestige = 10000
		add_dynasty_modifier = pam_founded_kingdom_of_heaven_modifier
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { exists = dynasty } }
		add_character_modifier = pam_founded_kingdom_of_heaven_modifier
	}
	custom_tooltip = {
		text = pam_kingdom_of_heaven_decision_subjugation_effect_tt
		set_variable = koh_subjugation_cb_allowed
	}
	set_global_variable = {
		name = created_kingdom_of_heaven
		value = yes
	}
	root.faith = {
		every_faith_ruler = {
			limit = {
				this != root
				is_independent_ruler = yes
				is_available_quick = {
					landed = yes
					ai = yes
					advanced_ruler = yes
				}
				is_playable_character = yes
				highest_held_title_tier >= tier_county
				highest_held_title_tier < tier_hegemony
				NOR = {
					has_relation_rival = root
					is_at_war_with = root
					government_has_flag = cannot_be_vassal_or_liege
				}
			}
			random = {
				chance = {
					value = 20
					if = {
						limit = { piety_level >= 1 }
						add = 5
					}
					if = {
						limit = { piety_level >= 2 }
						add = 8
					}
					if = {
						limit = { piety_level >= 3 }
						add = 10
					}
					if = {
						limit = { piety_level >= 4 }
						add = 15
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							opinion = {
								target = root
								value >= 30
							}
						}
						add = 10
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							opinion = {
								target = root
								value >= 60
							}
						}
						add = 15
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							exists = rite
							scope:kingdom_of_heaven_founder = { exists = rite }
							rite = scope:kingdom_of_heaven_founder.rite
						}
						add = 40
					}
				}
				add_to_list = new_vassals
				hidden_effect = {
					create_title_and_vassal_change = {
						type = swear_fealty
						save_scope_as = koh_fealty_change
						add_claim_on_loss = no
					}
					change_liege = {
						liege = root
						change = scope:koh_fealty_change
					}
					resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:koh_fealty_change
				}
				if = {
					limit = { highest_held_title_tier >= tier_kingdom }
					show_as_tooltip = {
						change_liege = {
							liege = root
							change = scope:koh_fealty_change
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	custom_tooltip = kingdom_of_heaven_lower_vassals_gained_tt
	custom_tooltip = {
		text = kingdom_of_heaven_lower_de_jure_gained_tt
		every_empire = {
			limit = {
				is_landless_type_title = no
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { exists = holder.top_liege }
					holder.top_liege = root
				}
				# really not landless
				any_in_de_jure_hierarchy = {
					tier = tier_kingdom
					is_landless_type_title = no
					any_in_de_jure_hierarchy = {
						tier = tier_duchy
						is_landless_type_title = no
					}
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = empire_temp
				OR = {
					holder ?= scope:kingdom_of_heaven_founder
					scope:kingdom_of_heaven_founder = { completely_controls = scope:empire_temp }
				}
			}
			set_de_jure_liege_title = title:h_kingdom_of_heaven
		}
	}
}

pam_create_theocratic_dynasty_extra_effects = {
	if = { # HoF doesn't like this
		limit = {
			exists = faith.religious_head
			root != faith.religious_head
			faith = { has_doctrine = doctrine_clerical_marriage_disallowed }
		}
		faith.religious_head = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = impious_opinion
				opinion = -40
			}
		}
		add_piety = major_piety_loss
		situation:the_christian_church ?= {
			if = {
				limit = {
					situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_the_christian_church_established_theocratic_dynasty
				}
				trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_the_christian_church_established_theocratic_dynasty
			}
		}
	}
}

pam_boost_clerical_appointment_score_effect = {
	$CHARACTER$.superior.primary_title ?= {
		change_appointment_investment = {
			target = $CHARACTER$
			value = $VALUE$
		}
	}
}

# Successful Siphon Treasury
pam_the_christian_church_clergy_embezzled_treasury_catalyst_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_clergy = yes
			any_character_situation = {
				situation_type = the_christian_church
			}
			situation:the_christian_church ?= {
				situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_clergy_embezzled_treasury
			}
		}
		situation:the_christian_church = {
			trigger_situation_catalyst = {
				catalyst = catalyst_clergy_embezzled_treasury
				character = root
			}
		}
	}
}

		}
	}
}

# Needs POPE (appointer), CARDINAL (new cardinal) and OTHERWISE (non-nepotistic catalyst), any scope
# Both branches guard their catalyst, so a phase listing neither books nothing
pam_the_christian_church_appointed_cardinal_catalyst_effect = {
	situation:the_christian_church ?= {
		if = {
			limit = {
				$CARDINAL$ = { pam_cardinal_is_nepotistic_appointment_trigger = { POPE = $POPE$ } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_the_christian_church_appointed_nepotistic_cardinal
				}
				trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_the_christian_church_appointed_nepotistic_cardinal
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					situation_top_has_catalyst = $OTHERWISE$
				}
				trigger_situation_catalyst = $OTHERWISE$
			}
		}
	}
}

# A non-Catholic ruler holds the de jure capital county of k_papal_state
pam_the_christian_church_catholic_hof_weak_papal_state_control_catalyst_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
			pam_catholic_hof_weak_papal_state_control_trigger = yes
			situation:the_christian_church ?= {
				situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_catholic_hof_weak_papal_state_control
			}
		}
		situation:the_christian_church = {
			trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_catholic_hof_weak_papal_state_control
		}
	}
}

@pam_papal_dynasty_capture_reigns = 3
pam_the_christian_church_papal_dynasty_capture_catalyst_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
			current_date_is_start_date_trigger = no
			like_christianity_religion_trigger = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { exists = dynasty }
			dynasty = { save_scope_as = pam_new_papal_dynasty }
		}
		situation:the_christian_church ?= {
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:pam_new_papal_dynasty
					has_variable = pam_papal_dynasty
					var:pam_papal_dynasty = scope:pam_new_papal_dynasty
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = pam_papal_dynasty_reigns
					add = 1
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { exists = scope:pam_new_papal_dynasty }
				set_variable = {
					name = pam_papal_dynasty
					value = scope:pam_new_papal_dynasty
				}
				set_variable = {
					name = pam_papal_dynasty_reigns
					value = 1
				}
				remove_variable = pam_papal_dynasty_capture_catalyst_fired
			}
			else = {
				remove_variable = pam_papal_dynasty
				remove_variable = pam_papal_dynasty_reigns
				remove_variable = pam_papal_dynasty_capture_catalyst_fired
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = pam_papal_dynasty_reigns
					var:pam_papal_dynasty_reigns >= @pam_papal_dynasty_capture_reigns
					NOT = { has_variable = pam_papal_dynasty_capture_catalyst_fired }
					situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_the_christian_church_papal_dynasty_capture
				}
				set_variable = pam_papal_dynasty_capture_catalyst_fired
				trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_the_christian_church_papal_dynasty_capture
			}
		}
	}
}

# A different Christian faith becomes Main Power
pam_the_christian_church_main_power_faith_changed_catalyst_effect = {
	situation:the_christian_church ?= {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = pam_main_power_faith_changed_catalyst_faith
				NOT = { var:tcc_main_power_faith = var:pam_main_power_faith_changed_catalyst_faith }
			}
			remove_variable = pam_the_christian_church_main_power_faith_changed_catalyst_fired
			remove_variable = pam_main_power_faith_changed_catalyst_faith
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:tcc_old_main_power_faith
				has_variable = tcc_main_power_faith
				var:tcc_main_power_faith != scope:tcc_old_main_power_faith
				NOT = { has_variable = pam_the_christian_church_main_power_faith_changed_catalyst_fired }
				situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_the_christian_church_main_power_faith_changed
			}
			set_variable = pam_the_christian_church_main_power_faith_changed_catalyst_fired
			set_variable = {
				name = pam_main_power_faith_changed_catalyst_faith
				value = var:tcc_main_power_faith
			}
			trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_the_christian_church_main_power_faith_changed
		}
	}
}

# Byzantine emperor is not Christian
pam_the_christian_church_byzantine_emperor_non_christian_catalyst_effect = {
	situation:the_christian_church ?= {
		if = {
			limit = {
				pam_byzantine_emperor_non_christian_trigger = no
			}
			remove_variable = pam_byzantine_emperor_non_christian_catalyst_fired
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				pam_byzantine_emperor_non_christian_trigger = yes
				NOT = { has_variable = pam_byzantine_emperor_non_christian_catalyst_fired }
				situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_byzantine_emperor_non_christian
			}
			set_variable = pam_byzantine_emperor_non_christian_catalyst_fired
			trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_byzantine_emperor_non_christian
		}
	}
}

# Non Christian presence in Europe
pam_the_christian_church_non_christian_kingdom_in_europe_catalyst_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			pam_non_christian_kingdom_holder_in_europe_trigger = yes
			situation:the_christian_church ?= {
				situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_non_christian_kingdom_in_europe
			}
		}
		situation:the_christian_church = {
			trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_non_christian_kingdom_in_europe
		}
		random_ruler = {
			limit = { pam_non_christian_kingdom_holder_in_europe_trigger = yes }
			set_variable = pam_non_christian_kingdom_holder_in_europe_trigger_var
		}
	}
}

# County conversion Catalysts
pam_the_christian_church_county_conversion_catalysts_effect = {
	if = { # PAM - Catalysts if non-Christian county is converted to Christianity
		limit = {
			faith != scope:old_faith
			scope:old_faith = {
				like_christianity_religion_trigger = no
			}
			faith.like_christianity_religion_trigger = yes
			situation:the_christian_church ?= {
				situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_county_converted_to_faith
			}
		}
		situation:the_christian_church = {
			trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_county_converted_to_faith
		}
	}
	if = { # PAM - Catalyst when Christian county is converted away from Christianity
		limit = {
			faith != scope:old_faith
			scope:old_faith = {
				like_christianity_religion_trigger = yes
			}
			faith.like_christianity_religion_trigger = no
			situation:the_christian_church ?= {
				situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_county_converted_from_faith
			}
		}
		situation:the_christian_church = {
			trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_county_converted_from_faith
		}
	}
	if = { # PAM - Jerusalem county is no longer Christian
		limit = {
			this = title:c_jerusalem
			scope:old_faith = {
				like_christianity_religion_trigger = yes
			}
			faith.like_christianity_religion_trigger = no
			situation:the_christian_church ?= {
				situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_lose_jerusalem
			}
		}
		situation:the_christian_church = {
			trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_lose_jerusalem
		}
	}
	if = { # PAM - Other Christian holy site county is no longer Christian
		limit = {
			NOT = { this = title:c_jerusalem }
			scope:old_faith = {
				like_christianity_religion_trigger = yes
			}
			faith.like_christianity_religion_trigger = no
			any_county_province = {
				barony = { is_holy_site_of = scope:old_faith }
			}
			situation:the_christian_church ?= {
				situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_lose_holy_site
			}
		}
		situation:the_christian_church = {
			trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_lose_holy_site
		}
	}
}

# Christian prelate hires mercenaries while at war with a non-Christian (on_mercenary_company_hired)
pam_the_christian_church_clergy_hired_mercenaries_catalyst_effect = {

			scope:employer = {
				has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
				is_clergy = yes
				like_christianity_religion_trigger = yes
				any_primary_war_enemy = {
					like_christianity_religion_trigger = no
				}
			}
			situation:the_christian_church ?= {
				situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_clergy_hired_mercenaries
