		}
	}
}

pam_the_christian_church_clergy_gained_landed_title_catalyst_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_variable = game_has_started  # Don't fire for the clerical title holders placed during setup
			has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
			is_clergy = yes
			like_christianity_religion_trigger = yes
			scope:title = {
				tier >= tier_county
				has_clerical_region = no
			}
			situation:the_christian_church ?= {
				situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_clergy_gained_landed_title
			}
		}
		situation:the_christian_church = {
			trigger_situation_catalyst = {
				catalyst = catalyst_clergy_gained_landed_title
				character = root
			}
		}
	}
}

# Excommunication war declared between two Christian rulers (excommunication_war on_declaration)
pam_the_christian_church_excommunication_war_catalyst_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:attacker = { has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes }
			scope:attacker = { like_christianity_religion_trigger = yes }
			scope:defender = { like_christianity_religion_trigger = yes }
			situation:the_christian_church ?= {
				situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_the_christian_church_excommunication_war_declared
			}
		}
		situation:the_christian_church = {
			trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_the_christian_church_excommunication_war_declared
		}
	}
}

# Humiliation war declared against a Christian ecclesiastical ruler
pam_the_christian_church_humiliation_war_catalyst_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:attacker = { has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes }
			scope:defender = {
				like_christianity_religion_trigger = yes
				government_has_flag = government_is_theocracy # Accounts for Ecclesiastical
			}
			situation:the_christian_church ?= {
				situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_the_christian_church_humiliation_war_declared
			}
		}
		situation:the_christian_church = {
			trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_the_christian_church_humiliation_war_declared
		}
	}
}

# Clergy has a child
pam_the_christian_church_clergy_has_child_catalyst_effect = {
	$CHARACTER$ = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
				is_clergy = yes
				rite = { rite_has_doctrine = doctrine_clerical_marriage_disallowed }
				any_character_situation = {
					situation_type = the_christian_church
					situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_clergy_has_child
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_held_title = { is_head_of_faith = yes }
				}
				situation:the_christian_church = {
					trigger_situation_catalyst = {
						catalyst = catalyst_the_christian_church_head_of_faith_has_child
						character = $CHARACTER$
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				situation:the_christian_church = {
					trigger_situation_catalyst = {
						catalyst = catalyst_clergy_has_child
						character = $CHARACTER$
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

pam_buy_ecclesiastical_title_interaction_effect = {
	scope:actor = {
		if = {
			limit = { government_has_flag = government_is_theocracy }
			save_scope_as = new_holder
		}
		else = {
			scope:secondary_recipient = { save_scope_as = new_holder }
		}
	}
	scope:actor = {
		pay_short_term_gold = {
			target = scope:recipient
			gold = pam_buy_ecclesiastical_title_gold_cost_value
		}
	}
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = granted
		save_scope_as = change
	}
	scope:target = {
		change_title_holder_include_vassals = {
			holder = scope:new_holder
			change = scope:change
			government_base = scope:recipient
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:change
	scope:new_holder = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { government_has_flag = government_is_ecclesiastical }
			}
			change_government = ecclesiastical_government
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:actor = { has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes }
			situation:the_christian_church ?= {
				situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_clergy_simony
			}
			scope:actor = {
				any_character_situation = {
					this = situation:the_christian_church
				}
			}
		}
		situation:the_christian_church = {
			trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_clergy_simony
		}
	}
}

ecclesiastic_transfer_land_interaction_immediate_effect = {
	scope:secondary_recipient = { save_scope_as = cleric }
	scope:recipient = { save_scope_as = owner }
	#Either your shittiness or target's coolness has given the clergyman pause
	if = {
		limit = {
			clerical_hesitation_claim_interaction_value >= 9
		}
		scope:secondary_recipient = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = yes
					piety <= 0
				}
				show_as_tooltip = {
					add_piety = {
						value = major_piety_loss
						multiply = {
							value = scope:actor.highest_held_title_tier
							subtract = 1
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_piety = {
					value = major_piety_loss
					multiply = {
						value = scope:actor.highest_held_title_tier
						subtract = 1
					}
				}
			}
			change_spiritual_fulfillment = massive_spiritual_fulfillment_loss
			if = {
				limit = {
					root ?= scope:cleric
				}
				custom_tooltip = clerical_hesitation_high_player_tt
			}
			else = {
				custom_tooltip = clerical_hesitation_high_tt
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			clerical_hesitation_claim_interaction_value >= 6
		}
		scope:secondary_recipient = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = yes
					piety <= 0
				}
				show_as_tooltip = {
					add_piety = {
						value = medium_piety_loss
						multiply = {
							value = scope:actor.highest_held_title_tier
							subtract = 1
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_piety = {
					value = medium_piety_loss
					multiply = {
						value = scope:actor.highest_held_title_tier
						subtract = 1
					}
				}
			}
			change_spiritual_fulfillment = major_spiritual_fulfillment_loss
			if = {
				limit = {
					root ?= scope:cleric
				}
				custom_tooltip = clerical_hesitation_high_player_tt
			}
			else = {
				custom_tooltip = clerical_hesitation_high_tt
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			clerical_hesitation_claim_interaction_value >= 3
		}
		scope:secondary_recipient = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = yes
					piety <= 0
				}
				show_as_tooltip = {
					add_piety = {
						value = minor_piety_loss
						multiply = {
							value = scope:actor.highest_held_title_tier
							subtract = 1
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_piety = {
					value = minor_piety_loss
					multiply = {
						value = scope:actor.highest_held_title_tier
						subtract = 1
					}
				}
			}
			change_spiritual_fulfillment = medium_spiritual_fulfillment_loss
			if = {
				limit = {
					root ?= scope:cleric
				}
				custom_tooltip = clerical_hesitation_high_player_tt
			}
			else = {
				custom_tooltip = clerical_hesitation_high_tt
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			clerical_hesitation_claim_interaction_value < 3
			clerical_hesitation_claim_interaction_value >= -2
		}
		scope:secondary_recipient = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = yes
					piety <= 0
				}
				show_as_tooltip = {
					add_piety = {
						value = miniscule_piety_loss
						multiply = {
							value = scope:actor.highest_held_title_tier
							subtract = 1
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_piety = {
					value = miniscule_piety_loss
					multiply = {
						value = scope:actor.highest_held_title_tier
						subtract = 1
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			clerical_hesitation_claim_interaction_value < -2
		}
		scope:secondary_recipient = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = yes
					piety <= 0
				}
				show_as_tooltip = {
					add_piety = {
						value = miniscule_piety_loss
						multiply = {
							value = scope:actor.highest_held_title_tier
							subtract = 1
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_piety = {
					value = miniscule_piety_loss
					multiply = {
						value = scope:actor.highest_held_title_tier
						subtract = 1
					}
				}
			}
			change_spiritual_fulfillment = miniscule_spiritual_fulfillment_value
			if = {
				limit = {
					root ?= scope:cleric
				}
				custom_tooltip = clerical_hesitation_low_player_tt
			}
			else = {
				custom_tooltip = clerical_hesitation_low_tt
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			clerical_hesitation_claim_interaction_value >= -5
		}
		scope:secondary_recipient = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = yes
					piety <= 0
				}
				show_as_tooltip = {
					add_piety = {
						value = miniscule_piety_loss
						multiply = {
							value = scope:actor.highest_held_title_tier
							subtract = 1
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_piety = {
					value = miniscule_piety_loss
					multiply = {
						value = scope:actor.highest_held_title_tier
						subtract = 1
					}
				}
			}
			change_spiritual_fulfillment = minor_spiritual_fulfillment_value
			if = {
				limit = {
					root ?= scope:cleric
				}
				custom_tooltip = clerical_hesitation_low_player_tt
			}
			else = {
				custom_tooltip = clerical_hesitation_low_tt
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			clerical_hesitation_claim_interaction_value >= -8
		}
		scope:secondary_recipient = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = yes
					piety <= 0
				}
				show_as_tooltip = {
					add_piety = {
						value = miniscule_piety_loss
						multiply = {
							value = scope:actor.highest_held_title_tier
							subtract = 1
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_piety = {
					value = miniscule_piety_loss
					multiply = {
						value = scope:actor.highest_held_title_tier
						subtract = 1
					}
				}
			}
			change_spiritual_fulfillment = medium_spiritual_fulfillment_value
			if = {
				limit = {
					root ?= scope:cleric
				}
				custom_tooltip = clerical_hesitation_low_player_tt
			}
			else = {
				custom_tooltip = clerical_hesitation_low_tt
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		scope:secondary_recipient = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = yes
					piety <= 0
				}
				show_as_tooltip = {
					add_piety = {
						value = miniscule_piety_loss
						multiply = {
							value = scope:actor.highest_held_title_tier
							subtract = 1
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_piety = {
					value = miniscule_piety_loss
					multiply = {
						value = scope:actor.highest_held_title_tier
						subtract = 1
					}
				}
			}
			change_spiritual_fulfillment = major_spiritual_fulfillment_value
			if = {
				limit = {
					root ?= scope:cleric
				}
				custom_tooltip = clerical_hesitation_low_player_tt
			}
			else = {
				custom_tooltip = clerical_hesitation_low_tt
			}
		}
	}
	#Opinion effects
	scope:target.holder ?= {
		#Hide this info from the cleric secondary_recipient
		if = {
			limit = {
				root ?= scope:secondary_recipient
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				add_opinion = {
					target = scope:actor
					modifier = demanded_my_title_opinion
					opinion = -50
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = scope:actor
				modifier = demanded_my_title_opinion
				opinion = -50
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:secondary_recipient = {
					is_ai = yes
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				add_opinion = {
					target = scope:secondary_recipient
					modifier = demanded_my_title_opinion
					opinion = -30
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:recipient = {
				NOT = {
					this = scope:target.holder
				}
			}
		}
		scope:recipient ?= {
			#Hide this info from the cleric secondary_recipient
			if = {
				limit = {
					root ?= scope:secondary_recipient
				}
				hidden_effect = {
					add_opinion = {
						target = scope:actor
						modifier = demanded_my_title_opinion
						opinion = -25
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_opinion = {
					target = scope:actor
					modifier = demanded_my_title_opinion
					opinion = -25
				}
			}


			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:secondary_recipient = {
						is_ai = yes
					}
				}
				hidden_effect = {
					add_opinion = {
						target = scope:secondary_recipient
						modifier = demanded_my_title_opinion
						opinion = -20
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_ai = yes
		}
		add_opinion = {
			target = scope:secondary_recipient
			modifier = clerical_transfer_title_opinion
			opinion = {
				value = 10
				if = {
					limit = {
						highest_held_title_tier < scope:target.tier
					}
					multiply = 8
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						highest_held_title_tier = scope:target.tier
					}
					multiply = 5
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						highest_held_title_tier_minus_one = scope:target.tier
					}
					multiply = 3
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						highest_held_title_tier_minus_two = scope:target.tier
					}
					multiply = 2
				}
			}
		}
	}
	#Send toast to actor to confirm that interaction option costs have been applied
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				scope:promise_church_succession = yes
				scope:donate_faith = yes
				AND = {
					scope:use_hook = yes
					has_usable_hook = scope:secondary_recipient
				}
				scope:offer_pilgrimage = yes
				scope:threaten_priest = yes
			}
		}
		trigger_event = pam_interactions_events.0038
		#You promised the church could inherit this title if it is ever without heirs
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:promise_church_succession = yes
			}
			scope:actor = {
				set_fallback_succession_title = scope:secondary_recipient.primary_title
			}
			scope:actor = {
				custom_tooltip = church_fallback_succession_promise_tt
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:donate_faith = yes
			}
			pay_short_term_gold = {
				target = scope:secondary_recipient
				gold = scope:actor.medium_gold_value
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:use_hook = yes
				has_usable_hook = scope:secondary_recipient
			}
			use_hook = scope:secondary_recipient
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:offer_hook = yes
			}
			scope:secondary_recipient = {
				add_hook = {
					type = strong_obligation_hook
					target = scope:actor
				}
			}
		}
		pam_offer_pilgrimage_effect = {
			ACTOR = scope:actor
			CLERIC = scope:secondary_recipient
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:threaten_priest = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:secondary_recipient = {
						this = scope:actor.faith.religious_head
					}
				}
				change_spiritual_fulfillment = massive_spiritual_fulfillment_loss
				add_piety_experience = massive_piety_loss
				custom_tooltip = hof_increase_piety_sp_fulfillment_loss_secondary_tt
			}
			else = {
				change_spiritual_fulfillment = major_spiritual_fulfillment_loss
				add_piety_experience = major_piety_loss
			}
			scope:secondary_recipient = {
				add_opinion = {
					modifier = threatened_church_opinion
					target = scope:actor
					opinion = -50
				}
			}
		}
	}
	#Catalyst towards saeculum_obscurum
	if = {
		limit = {
			#Only fire catalyst if clergyman is choosing a more immoral holder (has decently high hesitation)
			clerical_hesitation_claim_interaction_value >= -2
			any_character_situation = {
				this = situation:the_christian_church
			}
			situation:the_christian_church = {
				OR = {
					situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_ecclesiastical_transfer_land_interaction
					situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_ecclesiastical_transfer_land_interaction_major
				}
			}
		}
		situation:the_christian_church = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:target.tier >= tier_kingdom
				}
				trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_ecclesiastical_transfer_land_interaction_major
			}
			else = {
				trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_ecclesiastical_transfer_land_interaction
			}
		}

	}
}

ecclesiastic_transfer_land_interaction_transfer_effect = {
	#Transfer vassal
	#Need to inform player of why they were just transferred
	#Players are only valid if it is their primary title being transfered
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:target.holder ?= {
				NOT = {
					this = scope:recipient
				}
				is_ai = no
			}
		}
		#transfer vassal
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:target.holder = { is_governor_or_admin_count = no }
			}
			scope:target.holder = {
				add_to_list = already_transfered
				every_vassal_or_below = {
					limit = {
						highest_held_title_tier >= tier_county
					}
					add_to_list = already_transfered
				}
			}
			create_title_and_vassal_change = {
				type = granted
				save_scope_as = change
				add_claim_on_loss = no
			}
			scope:target.holder = {
				change_liege = {
					liege = scope:actor
					change = scope:change
				}
			}
			resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:change

			scope:target.holder = {
				#Notification event they're being transferred
				trigger_event = pam_interactions_events.0037
			}
		}
		#Admin - needs title revoked
		else = {
			every_held_title = {
				limit = {
					any_this_title_or_de_jure_above = {
						this = scope:target
					}
				}
				add_to_list = admin_target_transfer
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				create_title_and_vassal_change = {
					type = revoked
					save_scope_as = change
					add_claim_on_loss = yes
				}
				every_in_list = {
					list = admin_target_transfer
					change_title_holder = {
						holder = scope:recipient
						change = scope:change
					}
				}
				resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:change
			}
			scope:target.holder = {
				#Notification event to dispossessed admin
				trigger_event = pam_interactions_events.0039
			}
		}
	}
	#Transfer vassal if it is primary title of its holder
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			scope:target.holder.primary_title ?= scope:target
			scope:target.holder = {
				highest_held_title_tier = scope:target.tier
				is_governor_or_admin_count = no
			}
			OR = {
				#already low enough tier to transfer
				scope:target.holder.highest_held_title_tier < scope:actor.highest_held_title_tier
				#Will be low enough tier to transfer
				scope:target.holder = {
					NOT = {
						any_held_title = {
							NOT = {
								this = scope:target
							}
							tier = scope:target.tier
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:target.holder = { save_scope_as = old_holder }
		#Need to transfer vassals if getting taken down a tier
		if = {
			limit = {
				#Will be low enough tier to transfer
				scope:target.holder = {
					NOT = {
						any_held_title = {
							NOT = {
								this = scope:target
							}
							tier >= scope:target.tier
						}
					}
				}
			}
			scope:old_holder = {
				every_vassal = {
					limit = {
						highest_held_title_tier >= tier_county
						primary_title = {
							any_this_title_or_de_jure_above = {
								this = scope:target
							}
						}
						highest_held_title_tier >= scope:target.tier_minus_one
						is_governor_or_admin_count = no
					}
					add_to_list = transfer_vassals
				}
			}
		}
		#Transferring title still if needed to vassalize recipient
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:target.tier >= scope:actor.highest_held_title_tier
			}
			create_title_and_vassal_change = {
				type = granted
				save_scope_as = change
				add_claim_on_loss = yes
			}
			scope:target = {
				change_title_holder = {
					holder = scope:actor
					change = scope:change
				}
			}
			resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:change
		}
		scope:old_holder = {
			add_to_list = already_transfered
			every_vassal_or_below = {
				limit = {

				}
				add_to_list = already_transfered
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = clergy_transfer_vassal_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			create_title_and_vassal_change = {
				type = granted
				save_scope_as = change
				add_claim_on_loss = no
			}
			scope:old_holder = {
				change_liege = {
					liege = scope:actor
					change = scope:change
				}
			}
			resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:change
		}
	}
	#transfer the title without the holder
	else = {
		scope:target ?= {
			add_to_list = titles_to_grant
		}
	}
	scope:target ?= {
		#Revoke titles from admins in the transfer zone
		every_in_de_jure_hierarchy = {
			limit = {
				tier >= tier_county
				holder ?= {
					is_vassal_or_below_of = scope:recipient
					is_governor_or_admin_count = yes
				}
				NOT = { is_in_list = titles_to_grant }
			}
			add_to_list = admin_title_losses
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			create_title_and_vassal_change = {
				type = revoked
				save_scope_as = change
				add_claim_on_loss = yes
			}
			every_in_list = {
				list = admin_title_losses
				change_title_holder = {
					holder = scope:recipient
					change = scope:change
				}
			}
			resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:change
		}

		#Transfer vassals
		every_in_de_jure_hierarchy = {
			limit = {
				exists = holder
				holder = {
					is_governor_or_admin_count = no
				}
			}
			#Transfer vassals/sub-vassals of recipient that belong under target title
			#At this point, target holder may be transfered and thus their vassals would not be included in this
			if = {
				limit = {
					holder = {
						highest_held_title_tier >= tier_county
						any_liege_or_above = {
							this = scope:recipient
						}
						primary_title = {
							any_this_title_or_de_jure_above = {
								this = scope:target
							}
						}
						#Liege is also being transferred
						NOT = {
							any_liege_or_above = {
								NOT = {
									this = scope:target.holder
								}
								primary_title = {
									any_this_title_or_de_jure_above = {
										this = scope:target
									}
								}
							}
						}
						NOR = {
							is_in_list = transfer_vassals
							is_in_list = already_transfered
						}
						highest_held_title_tier < scope:actor.highest_held_title_tier
					}
				}
				holder = {	add_to_list = transfer_vassals }
			}
		}
	}
	#transfer title(s)
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_in_list = {
				list = titles_to_grant
				count >= 1
			}
		}
		create_title_and_vassal_change = {
			type = granted
			save_scope_as = change
			add_claim_on_loss = yes
		}
		every_in_list = {
			list = titles_to_grant
			limit = {
				tier >= tier_county
			}
			change_title_holder = {
				holder = scope:actor
				change = scope:change
			}
		}
		resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:change
	}
	#transfer vassals
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_in_list = {
				list = transfer_vassals
				count >= 1
				any_held_title = {
					is_landless_type_title = no
					NOT = {
						is_in_list = titles_to_grant
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_in_list = {
					list = transfer_vassals
					this = scope:target.holder
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = clergy_transfer_vassal_tt
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			create_title_and_vassal_change = {
				type = granted
				save_scope_as = change
				add_claim_on_loss = yes
			}
			every_in_list = {
				list = transfer_vassals
				change_liege = {
					liege = scope:actor
					change = scope:change
				}
			}
			resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:change
		}
	}
	#Make sure recipient isn't sneakily holding onto titles beneath the target title
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:recipient = {
				NOT = {
					is_vassal_of = scope:actor
				}
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			every_held_title = {
				limit = {
					tier >= tier_county
					any_this_title_or_de_jure_above = {
						this = scope:target
					}
				}
				add_to_list = recipient_mop_up
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			create_title_and_vassal_change = {
				type = granted
				save_scope_as = change
				add_claim_on_loss = yes
			}
			every_in_list = {
				list = recipient_mop_up
				limit = {
					tier >= tier_county
				}
				change_title_holder = {
					holder = scope:actor
					change = scope:change
				}
			}
			resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:change
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:target.tier >= highest_held_title_tier_minus_one
		}
		create_character_memory = {
			type = negotiated_for_land_memory
			participants = {
				recipient = scope:recipient
			}
		}
		scope:new_memory = {
			set_variable = {
				name = target_title
				value = scope:target
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:target.tier >= scope:recipient.highest_held_title_tier_minus_one
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			create_character_memory = {
				type = gave_away_land_memory
				participants = {
					actor = scope:actor
				}
			}
			scope:new_memory = {
				set_variable = {
					name = target_title
					value = scope:target
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

pam_negotiate_land_possible_refusal_notificaton_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:target.tier >= scope:recipient.highest_held_title_tier_minus_one
			scope:recipient = {
				has_legitimacy = no
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = { custom_tooltip = refuse_ecclesiastic_land_transfer_penalty_no_legit_tt }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			scope:recipient = {
				has_legitimacy = no
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = { custom_tooltip = refuse_ecclesiastic_land_transfer_penalty_minor_no_legit_tt }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			scope:target.tier >= scope:recipient.highest_held_title_tier_minus_one
		}
		scope:recipient = { custom_tooltip = refuse_ecclesiastic_land_transfer_penalty_tt }
	}
	else = {
		scope:recipient = { custom_tooltip = refuse_ecclesiastic_land_transfer_penalty_minor_tt }
	}
}
clergy_land_refusal_legitimacy_piety_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:target.tier >= scope:recipient.highest_held_title_tier_minus_one
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			add_piety = clergy_land_refusal_large_piety_value
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_legitimacy = yes
				}
				add_legitimacy = clergy_land_refusal_large_legitimacy_value
			}
			else = {
				add_prestige = clergy_land_refusal_large_prestige_value
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		scope:recipient = {
			add_piety = clergy_land_refusal_small_piety_value
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_legitimacy = yes
				}
				add_legitimacy = clergy_land_refusal_small_legitimacy_value
			}
			else = {
				add_prestige = clergy_land_refusal_small_prestige_value
			}
		}
	}
}

pam_available_negotiation_methods_tooltip_effect = {
	#show tooltips
	if = {
		limit = {
			pam_forced_negotiate_land_success_trigger = yes
		}
		#Do not show AI behaviour tooltips if this is recipient viewing
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { exists = scope:recipient_event }
			}
			custom_tooltip = pam_clergy_land_forced_success_tt
			custom_tooltip = pam_negotiate_land_force_by_tt
		}
		#FORCED FIRST
		if = {
			limit = {
				pam_negotiate_land_forced_hook_trigger = yes
			}
			custom_tooltip = pam_forced_negotiate_land_hook_tt
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				pam_negotiate_land_forced_relation_trigger = yes
			}
			custom_tooltip = pam_forced_negotiate_land_relation_tt
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				pam_negotiate_land_forced_piety_level_trigger = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:recipient.piety_level = 0
				}
				custom_tooltip = pam_forced_negotiate_land_piety_level_1_tt
			}
			else = {
				custom_tooltip = pam_forced_negotiate_land_piety_level_2_tt
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				pam_negotiate_land_forced_legitimacy_level_trigger = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:recipient.legitimacy_level = 0
				}
				custom_tooltip = pam_forced_negotiate_land_legitimacy_level_1_tt
			}
			else = {
				custom_tooltip = pam_forced_negotiate_land_legitimacy_level_2_tt
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				pam_negotiate_land_forced_gold_trigger = yes
			}
			show_as_tooltip = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_treasury = yes
					}
					pay_short_term_treasury = {
						target = scope:recipient
						gold = scope:recipient.pam_transfer_land_gold_value
					}
				}
				else = {
					pay_short_term_gold = {
						target = scope:recipient
						gold = scope:recipient.pam_transfer_land_gold_value
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		#Do not show AI behaviour tooltips if this is recipient viewing
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { exists = scope:recipient_event }
			}
			custom_tooltip = pam_clergy_land_possible_success_tt
		}
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			pam_possible_negotiate_land_success_trigger = yes
		}
		#Do not show AI behaviour tooltips if this is recipient viewing
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { exists = scope:recipient_event }
			}
			custom_tooltip = pam_negotiate_land_possible_by_tt
		}
		#POSSIBLE NEXT
		if = {
			limit = {
				pam_negotiate_land_possible_hook_trigger = yes
				pam_negotiate_land_forced_hook_trigger = no
			}
			custom_tooltip = pam_possible_negotiate_land_hook_tt
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				pam_negotiate_land_possible_relation_trigger = yes
				pam_negotiate_land_forced_relation_trigger = no
			}
			custom_tooltip = pam_possible_negotiate_land_relation_tt
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				pam_negotiate_land_possible_piety_level_trigger = yes
				pam_negotiate_land_forced_piety_level_trigger = no
			}
			custom_tooltip = pam_possible_negotiate_land_piety_level_tt
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				pam_negotiate_land_possible_legitimacy_level_trigger = yes
				pam_negotiate_land_forced_legitimacy_level_trigger = no
			}
			custom_tooltip = pam_possible_negotiate_land_legitimacy_level_tt
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				pam_negotiate_land_possible_gold_trigger = yes
				pam_negotiate_land_forced_gold_trigger = no
			}
			show_as_tooltip = {
				pam_negotiate_land_gold_effect = yes
			}
		}
	}
}
pam_negotiate_land_hook_effect = {
	use_hook = scope:recipient
}
pam_negotiate_land_relation_accept_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_relation_best_friend = scope:recipient
		}
		remove_relation_best_friend = scope:recipient
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_relation_friend = scope:recipient
		}
		remove_relation_friend = scope:recipient
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_relation_soulmate = scope:recipient
		}
		remove_relation_soulmate = scope:recipient
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_relation_lover = scope:recipient
		}
		remove_relation_lover = scope:recipient
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_relation_crush = scope:recipient
		}
		remove_relation_crush = scope:recipient
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_relation_student = scope:recipient
		}
		remove_relation_student = scope:recipient
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_relation_mentor = scope:recipient
		}
		remove_relation_mentor = scope:recipient
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_relation_guardian = scope:recipient
		}
		remove_relation_guardian = scope:recipient
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_relation_ward = scope:recipient
		}
		remove_relation_ward = scope:recipient
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_relation_potential_friend = scope:recipient
		}
		remove_relation_potential_friend = scope:recipient
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_relation_potential_lover = scope:recipient
		}
		remove_relation_potential_lover = scope:recipient
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_relation_blood_brother = scope:recipient
		}
		remove_relation_blood_brother = scope:recipient
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_relation_disciple = scope:recipient
		}
		remove_relation_disciple = scope:recipient
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_relation_elder = scope:recipient
		}
		remove_relation_elder = scope:recipient
	}
}
pam_negotiate_land_relation_effect = {
	pam_negotiate_land_relation_accept_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:forceable_relation
		}
		custom_tooltip = recipient_becomes_potential_rival_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_relation_potential_rival = {
				target = scope:recipient
				reason = rival_quarreled_over_land
			}
		}
		change_spiritual_fulfillment = medium_spiritual_fulfillment_loss
		scope:recipient = {
			change_spiritual_fulfillment = massive_spiritual_fulfillment_loss
		}
	}
	else = {
		custom_tooltip = recipient_becomes_rival_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_relation_rival = {
				target = scope:recipient
				reason = rival_quarreled_over_land
			}
		}
		change_spiritual_fulfillment = massive_spiritual_fulfillment_loss
		scope:recipient = {
			change_spiritual_fulfillment = massive_spiritual_fulfillment_loss
		}
	}
}
pam_negotiate_land_piety_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			pam_negotiate_land_forced_piety_level_trigger = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:recipient.piety_level = 0
			}
			add_piety_experience = major_piety_loss
			scope:recipient = { add_piety_experience = massive_piety_loss }
		}
		else = {
			add_piety_experience = massive_piety_loss
			scope:recipient = { add_piety_level = -1 }
		}
	}
	else = {
		add_piety_level = -1
		scope:recipient = { add_piety_level = -2 }
	}
}
pam_negotiate_land_legitimacy_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			pam_negotiate_land_forced_legitimacy_level_trigger = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:recipient.legitimacy_level = 0
			}
			add_legitimacy = medium_legitimacy_loss
			scope:recipient = { add_legitimacy = major_legitimacy_loss }
		}
		else = {
			add_legitimacy = major_legitimacy_loss
			scope:recipient = { add_legitimacy_level = -1 }
		}
	}
	else = {
		add_legitimacy_level = -1
		scope:recipient = { add_legitimacy_level = -2 }
	}
}
pam_negotiate_land_gold_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_treasury = yes
		}
		pay_short_term_treasury = {
			target = scope:recipient
			gold = scope:recipient.pam_transfer_land_gold_value
		}
	}
	else = {
		pay_short_term_gold = {
			target = scope:recipient
			gold = scope:recipient.pam_transfer_land_gold_value
		}
	}
}
pam_negotiate_land_tracking_variable_effect = {
	scope:recipient = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = clergy_land_granted
				var:clergy_land_granted <= scope:target.tier
			}
			remove_variable = clergy_land_granted
			custom_tooltip = clergy_land_request_reduction_tt
			set_variable = {
				name = clergy_land_granted
				value = scope:target.tier
				years = 5
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_variable = clergy_land_granted
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = clergy_land_request_reduction_tt
			set_variable = {
				name = clergy_land_granted
				value = scope:target.tier
				years = 5
			}
		}
	}
}

######################
### RITE DIVERGENCE DRIFT
######################


@hor_drift_forward_cap = 40
@hor_drift_backward_floor = 5

@hor_drift_step_high = 20
@hor_drift_step_normal = 10
@hor_drift_step_low = 5

@hor_drift_step_high_neg = -20
@hor_drift_step_normal_neg = -10
@hor_drift_step_low_neg = -5

@hor_drift_low_fervor = 40
@hor_drift_high_fervor = 70

rite_yearly_hor_divergence_drift_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			this != faith.main_rite
			# We leave rites that are already heretical alone, since heresy already covers that range
			"divergence(faith.main_rite)" < faith.heresy_threshold
			head_of_rite ?= {
				is_available_ai_adult = yes
				NOT = { has_character_flag = hor_already_drifted_rite_flag }
			}
		}
		random = {
			chance = {
				value = 4
				# Low fervor: lots of room for reinterpretation
				if = {
					limit = { faith.fervor <= @hor_drift_low_fervor }
					add = 6
				}
				# Stable faith: rare drift
				if = {
					limit = { faith.fervor >= @hor_drift_high_fervor }
					add = -2
				}
				# Unfulfilled HoR's do changes more often
				if = {
					limit = {
						head_of_rite ?= {
							spiritual_fulfillment <= 0
						}
					}
					add = 4
				}
				if = {
					limit = { head_of_rite ?= { has_trait = heresiarch } }
					add = 25
				}
				min = 1
			}

			rite_yearly_hor_divergence_drift_apply_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

rite_yearly_hor_divergence_drift_apply_effect = {
	save_scope_as = diverged_rite
	head_of_rite = { save_scope_as = diverged_hor }

	# Snapshot the rite's current tenets and (HoR-known) doctrines so we can identify what change_rite_divergence swapped in.
	every_rite_tenet = {
		add_to_list = drift_tenets_before
	}
	scope:diverged_hor = {
		every_character_doctrine = {
			rite_filter = scope:diverged_rite
			add_to_list = drift_doctrines_before
		}
	}

	random_list = {
		100 = {
			trigger = {
				"divergence(faith.main_rite)" <= 40
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				"divergence(faith.main_rite)" >= 30
			}
			modifier = {
				# Low fervor faiths drift outward more readily.
				factor = 1.5
				faith.fervor <= @hor_drift_low_fervor
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.6
				faith.fervor >= @hor_drift_high_fervor
			}

			save_scope_value_as = { name = diverged_change value = 1 }
			rite_yearly_hor_divergence_drift_forward_pick_effect = yes
		}
		100 = {
			trigger = {
				"divergence(faith.main_rite)" >= 10
				faith.rite_growth_rites_needed <= 0
				trigger_if = {
					limit = {
						exists = faith.religious_head
					}
					scope:diverged_hor = {
						OR = {
							opinion = {
								target = scope:diverged_rite.faith.religious_head
								value >= 50
							}
							has_dread_level_towards = {
								target = scope:diverged_rite.faith.religious_head
								level >= 1
							}
						}
					}
				}
				trigger_else = {
					always = yes
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				# Mirror the forward throttle: when divergence is small there's less room to walk it back.
				factor = 0.5
				"divergence(faith.main_rite)" < 20
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.3
				faith.fervor >= @hor_drift_high_fervor
			}
			modifier = {
				# Consolidation belongs to high-fervor faiths; sub-70 fervor is the divergence regime.
				factor = 0.2
				faith.fervor < @hor_drift_high_fervor
			}
			modifier = {
				# Without a HoF to anchor consolidation, backward drift is much rarer.
				factor = 0.2
				NOT = { exists = faith.religious_head }
			}
			save_scope_value_as = { name = diverged_change value = -1 }
			rite_yearly_hor_divergence_drift_backward_pick_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

rite_yearly_hor_divergence_drift_forward_pick_effect = {
	random_list = {
		1 = {
			# Only allow a +20 jump if we're far below the cap.
			trigger = {
				"divergence(faith.main_rite)" <= 20
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.4
				"divergence(faith.main_rite)" >= 15
			}
			save_scope_value_as = { name = diverged_change value = @hor_drift_step_high }
			change_rite_divergence = {
				value = @hor_drift_step_high
				override_tenets_first = no
			}
		}
		1 = {
			trigger = {
				"divergence(faith.main_rite)" <= 20
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.4
				"divergence(faith.main_rite)" >= 15
			}
			save_scope_value_as = { name = diverged_change value = @hor_drift_step_high }
			change_rite_divergence = {
				value = @hor_drift_step_high
				override_tenets_first = yes
			}
		}
		3 = {
			trigger = {
				"divergence(faith.main_rite)" <= 30
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				"divergence(faith.main_rite)" >= 25
			}
			save_scope_value_as = { name = diverged_change value = @hor_drift_step_normal }
			change_rite_divergence = {
				value = @hor_drift_step_normal
				override_tenets_first = no
			}
		}
		3 = {
			trigger = {
				"divergence(faith.main_rite)" <= 30
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				"divergence(faith.main_rite)" >= 25
			}
			save_scope_value_as = { name = diverged_change value = @hor_drift_step_normal }
			change_rite_divergence = {
				value = @hor_drift_step_normal
				override_tenets_first = yes
			}
		}
		8 = {
			trigger = {
				"divergence(faith.main_rite)" <= 40
			}
			save_scope_value_as = { name = diverged_change value = @hor_drift_step_low }
			change_rite_divergence = {
				value = @hor_drift_step_low
				override_tenets_first = no
			}
		}
		8 = {
			trigger = {
				"divergence(faith.main_rite)" <= 40
			}
			save_scope_value_as = { name = diverged_change value = @hor_drift_step_low }
			change_rite_divergence = {
				value = @hor_drift_step_low
				override_tenets_first = yes
			}
		}
	}
	rite_yearly_hor_divergence_drift_commit_effect = yes
}

rite_yearly_hor_divergence_drift_backward_pick_effect = {
	random_list = {
		1 = {
			trigger = {
				"divergence(faith.main_rite)" >= 25
			}
			save_scope_value_as = { name = diverged_change value = @hor_drift_step_high_neg }
			change_rite_divergence = {
				value = @hor_drift_step_high_neg
				override_tenets_first = no
			}
		}
		1 = {
			trigger = {
				"divergence(faith.main_rite)" >= 25
			}
			save_scope_value_as = { name = diverged_change value = @hor_drift_step_high_neg }
			change_rite_divergence = {
				value = @hor_drift_step_high_neg
				override_tenets_first = yes
			}
		}
		3 = {
			trigger = {
				"divergence(faith.main_rite)" >= 15
			}
			save_scope_value_as = { name = diverged_change value = @hor_drift_step_normal_neg }
			change_rite_divergence = {
				value = @hor_drift_step_normal_neg
				override_tenets_first = no
			}
		}
		3 = {
			trigger = {
				"divergence(faith.main_rite)" >= 15
			}
			save_scope_value_as = { name = diverged_change value = @hor_drift_step_normal_neg }
			change_rite_divergence = {
				value = @hor_drift_step_normal_neg
				override_tenets_first = yes
			}
		}
		8 = {
			trigger = {
				"divergence(faith.main_rite)" >= 10
			}
			save_scope_value_as = { name = diverged_change value = @hor_drift_step_low_neg }
			change_rite_divergence = {
				value = @hor_drift_step_low_neg
				override_tenets_first = no
			}
		}
		8 = {
			trigger = {
				"divergence(faith.main_rite)" >= 10
			}
			save_scope_value_as = { name = diverged_change value = @hor_drift_step_low_neg }
			change_rite_divergence = {
				value = @hor_drift_step_low_neg
				override_tenets_first = yes
			}
		}
	}
	rite_yearly_hor_divergence_drift_commit_effect = yes
}

rite_yearly_hor_divergence_drift_commit_effect = {
	random_rite_tenet = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { is_in_list = drift_tenets_before }
		}
		save_scope_as = diverged_added_tenet
	}
	scope:diverged_hor = {
		random_character_doctrine = {
			rite_filter = scope:diverged_rite
			limit = {
				NOT = { is_in_list = drift_doctrines_before }
			}
			save_scope_as = diverged_added_doctrine
		}
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				exists = scope:diverged_added_tenet
				exists = scope:diverged_added_doctrine
			}
		}
		scope:diverged_hor = {
			add_character_flag = hor_already_drifted_rite_flag
		}
		if = {
			limit = { scope:diverged_change <= -1 }
			scope:diverged_rite.faith = {
				change_fervor = {
					value = notable_fervor_value
					desc = fervor_gain_pam_rite_consolidated
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { scope:diverged_change >= 1 }
			scope:diverged_rite.faith = {
				change_fervor = {
					value = significant_fervor_loss
					desc = fervor_loss_pam_rite_diverged
				}
			}
		}
		rite_yearly_hor_divergence_drift_notify_effect = yes
	}
}

# Notify every ruler of the rite + the religious head, via letter event.
rite_yearly_hor_divergence_drift_notify_effect = {
	faith = {
		every_faith_ruler = {
			limit = {
				rite = scope:diverged_rite
				this != scope:diverged_hor
			}
			trigger_event = pam_tenet_events.0004
		}
	}
	# Religious head (if any) gets the letter too, even if they aren't iterated above.
	faith.religious_head ?= {
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { rite = scope:diverged_rite }
				this != scope:diverged_hor
			}
			trigger_event = pam_tenet_events.0004
		}
	}
}

pam_declare_rite_heresy_effect = {
	custom_tooltip = {
		text = pam_declare_rite_heresy_effect_tt
		detach_rite_to_new_faith = {
			target = scope:heresy_rite
		}
	}
	# Heresy rite types ship convert = no so they cannot be joined before the bull; branding has to flip it or nothing can convert in.
	scope:heresy_rite = {
		set_can_convert_to_rite = yes
	}
	faith = {
		change_fervor = {
			value = notable_fervor_value
			desc = fervor_gain_pam_rite_declared_heresy
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = rite_growth_recent_heresy
			value = yes
			days = rite_growth_heresy_cooldown_days
		}
	}
	# The declaring faith repudiates the now-heretical rite, so its core tenets lose standing there.
	if = {
		limit = { faith = { has_tenet_popularity_trigger = yes  } }
		faith = { save_scope_as = heresy_declaring_faith }
		scope:heresy_rite = {
			every_rite_tenet = {
				status = core
				save_scope_as = heresy_rite_tenet
				scope:heresy_declaring_faith = {
					change_tenet_popularity = {
						target = scope:heresy_rite_tenet
						change = major_tenet_popularity_loss
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:heresy_rite
			NOT = { scope:heresy_rite.faith = faith }
		}
		scope:heresy_rite.faith = {
			every_faith_character = {
				limit = {
					is_ruler = yes
					this != scope:bull_issuer
				}
				trigger_event = { id = pam_decision_events.0013 days = 1 }
			}
		}
	}
}

#This is only for use in pam_cathedral_01, pam_cathedral_02, and pam_cathedral_03
pam_cathedral_art_contribution_subject_event_effect = {
	save_scope_as = contributor
	scope:contributor = {
		trigger_event = {
			id = $EVENT$
			days = { 5 20 }
		}
	}
}

#This is only for use in pam_cathedral_01, pam_cathedral_02, and pam_cathedral_03
cathedral_great_project_funded_started_event_sender_effect = {
	scope:founder = {
		trigger_event = pam_great_projects_events.0005
	}
	scope:owner = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				this != scope:founder
			}
			trigger_event = pam_great_projects_events.0005
		}
	}
	every_contribution = {
		limit = {
			contribution_is_funded = yes
		}
		contributor ?= {
			if = {
				limit = {
					this != scope:owner
					this != scope:founder
				}
				save_scope_as = contributor
				trigger_event = pam_great_projects_events.0005
			}
		}
	}
}

cathedral_great_project_completed_event_sender_effect = {
	scope:founder = {
		trigger_event = pam_great_projects_events.0020
	}
	scope:owner = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				this != scope:founder
			}
			trigger_event = pam_great_projects_events.0020
		}
	}
	scope:founder_heir ?= {
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_alive = yes
				this != scope:founder
				this != scope:owner
			}
			trigger_event = pam_great_projects_events.0020
		}
	}
	every_contribution = {
		limit = {
			contribution_is_funded = yes
		}
		contributor ?= {
			if = {
				limit = {
					this != scope:owner
					this != scope:founder
					NOT = { this ?= scope:founder_heir }
				}
				save_scope_as = contributor
				trigger_event = pam_great_projects_events.0020
			}
		}
	}
}

# Create a New Ecclesiastical Title as HoF - on_accept
pam_create_new_ecclesiastical_title_interaction_effect = {
	scope:secondary_recipient = {
		save_scope_as = new_ecclesiastical_holder
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		save_scope_as = kingdom_holder
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:new_ecclesiastical_holder = {
					NOT = {
						is_courtier_of = scope:kingdom_holder
					}
					is_ruler = no
				}
			}
			add_courtier = scope:new_ecclesiastical_holder
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:new_ecclesiastical_holder = {
					NOT = { is_councillor_of = scope:kingdom_holder }
				}
			}
			assign_councillor_type = {
				type = councillor_court_chaplain
				remove_existing_councillor = yes
				target = scope:new_ecclesiastical_holder
			}
		}
	}
	scope:target = {
		save_scope_as = target_kingdom
	}
	scope:actor = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_character_situation = {
					situation_type = the_christian_church
					situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_the_christian_church_clerical_region_created
				}
			}
			situation:the_christian_church = {
				trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_the_christian_church_clerical_region_created
			}
		}
		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = create_new_ecclesiastical_title_toast
			left_icon = scope:actor
			right_icon = scope:new_ecclesiastical_holder
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = create_new_ecclesiastical_title_effect_tt
				create_title_and_vassal_change = {
					type = created
					save_scope_as = change
					add_claim_on_loss = no
				}
				scope:target_kingdom = {
					title_capital_county = {
						save_scope_as = clerical_region_anchor_county
						random_county_province = {
							limit = {
								is_county_capital = yes
							}
							save_scope_as = region_capital
							create_clerical_region = {
								domicile_type = ecclesiastical_domicile
								name = NEW_ECCLESIASTICAL_TITLE
								save_scope_as = new_title
							}
							scope:new_title ?= {
								set_coa = scope:target_kingdom
								set_color_from_title = scope:target_kingdom
								set_capital_county = scope:clerical_region_anchor_county
								set_landless_title = yes
								set_no_automatic_claims = yes
								set_definitive_form = yes
								change_title_holder = {
									holder = scope:new_ecclesiastical_holder
									change = scope:change
								}
								resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:change
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		reverse_add_opinion = {
			target = scope:new_ecclesiastical_holder
			modifier = grateful_opinion
			opinion = 50
		}
	}
	scope:new_ecclesiastical_holder = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { government_has_flag = government_is_ecclesiastical }
			}
			change_government = ecclesiastical_government
		}
		faith = {
			change_fervor = {
				value = notable_fervor_gain
				desc = fervor_gain_clerical_title_created
			}
		}
		situation:the_christian_church ?= {
			add_manual_participant = prev
		}
	}
}

# Expects scope:target and scope:pam_new_archbishop, and the caller must save pam_old_archbishop first
pam_grant_ecclesiastical_title_interaction_effect = {
	scope:actor = {
		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = pam_grant_ecclesiastical_title_toast
			left_icon = scope:actor
			right_icon = scope:pam_new_archbishop
			if = {
				limit = { exists = scope:pam_old_archbishop }
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = pam_grant_ecclesiastical_title_deposed_effect_tt
					create_title_and_vassal_change = {
						type = granted
						save_scope_as = change
						add_claim_on_loss = no
					}
					scope:pam_old_archbishop = {
						every_held_title = {
							limit = {
								OR = {
									has_clerical_region = yes
									is_landless_type_title = no
								}
							}
							change_title_holder_include_vassals = {
								holder = scope:pam_new_archbishop
								change = scope:change
								government_base = scope:pam_old_archbishop
							}
						}
					}
					resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:change
				}
			}
			else = {
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = pam_grant_ecclesiastical_title_vacant_effect_tt
					create_title_and_vassal_change = {
						type = granted
						save_scope_as = change
					}
					scope:target = {
						change_title_holder_include_vassals = {
							holder = scope:pam_new_archbishop
							change = scope:change
						}
					}
					resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:change
				}
			}
			add_hook = {
				type = granted_clerical_title_hook
				target = scope:pam_new_archbishop
			}
		}
		reverse_add_opinion = {
			target = scope:pam_new_archbishop
			modifier = grateful_opinion
			opinion = 50
		}
	}
	scope:pam_old_archbishop ?= {
		add_opinion = {
			target = scope:actor
			modifier = fp3_resentful_opinion
			opinion = -40
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:pam_old_archbishop ?= { rite != scope:actor.rite }
		}
		scope:actor.faith = {
			change_fervor = {
				value = significant_fervor_loss
				desc = fervor_loss_pam_archbishop_deposed
			}
		}
	}
	scope:pam_new_archbishop = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { government_has_flag = government_is_ecclesiastical }
			}
			change_government = ecclesiastical_government
		}
		situation:the_christian_church ?= {
			add_manual_participant = prev
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			situation:the_christian_church ?= {
				situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_clergy_elevated_to_archbishopric
			}
		}
		situation:the_christian_church = {
			trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_clergy_elevated_to_archbishopric
		}
	}
}

# Picks scope:target_character for a GHW after scope:target_kingdom is already set (e.g. bull kingdom selection).
pam_determine_ghw_target_character_from_kingdom_effect = {
	scope:target_kingdom = {
		random_in_de_jure_hierarchy = {
			continue = { tier > tier_county }
			limit = {
				tier = tier_county
				$FAITH$.main_rite = {
					is_hostile_enough_for_holy_war_trigger = { RITE = prev.holder.rite }
				}
				holder = {
					highest_held_title_tier >= 4
					any_realm_county = { count >= 5 }
				}
			}
			alternative_limit = {
				tier = tier_county
				$FAITH$.main_rite = {
					is_hostile_enough_for_holy_war_trigger = { RITE = prev.holder.rite }
				}
				holder = {
					highest_held_title_tier >= 3
					any_realm_county = { count >= 5 }
				}
			}
			alternative_limit = {
				tier = tier_county
				$FAITH$.main_rite = {
					is_hostile_enough_for_holy_war_trigger = { RITE = prev.holder.rite }
				}
				holder = {
					highest_held_title_tier >= 2
					any_realm_county = { count >= 5 }
				}
			}
			alternative_limit = {
				tier = tier_county
				$FAITH$.main_rite = {
					is_hostile_enough_for_holy_war_trigger = { RITE = prev.holder.rite }
				}
			}
			alternative_limit = {
				tier = tier_kingdom
				$FAITH$.main_rite = {
					is_hostile_enough_for_holy_war_trigger = { RITE = prev.holder.rite }
				}
			}
			holder = {
				save_scope_as = target_character
			}
		}
	}
}

pam_use_bull_selected_ghw_target_effect = {
	$GHW_SPONSOR$ = {
		var:pam_bull_ghw_target_kingdom = { save_scope_as = target_kingdom }
		remove_variable = pam_bull_ghw_target_kingdom
	}
	pam_determine_ghw_target_character_from_kingdom_effect = { FAITH = $FAITH$ }
}

# Starts an undirected GHW using the kingdom stored on the HoF by pick_crusade_target_decision.
pam_start_bull_great_holy_war_effect = {
	$GHW_SPONSOR$ = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = pam_bull_ghw_target_kingdom }
			religion = { set_variable = variable_ghw_unlocked }
			$FAITH$ = { trigger_event = great_holy_war.0001 }
		}
	}
}

pam_bull_notify_faith_rulers_effect = {
	scope:bull_issuer = {
		faith = {
			every_faith_character = {
				limit = {
					is_ruler = yes
					this != scope:bull_issuer
				}
				trigger_event = { id = pam_bull_events.0004 days = 1 }
			}
		}
	}
}

pam_apply_great_schism_creation_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_variable = pam_great_schism_happened } }
		set_global_variable = {
			name = pam_great_schism_happened
			value = current_year
		}
	}
	situation:the_christian_church.var:schism_mainline_rite = { save_scope_as = schism_mainline_rite }
	if = {
		limit = { scope:schism_mainline_rite = rite:roman_rite }
		faith:catholic = { save_scope_as = schism_mainline_faith }
		faith:orthodox = { save_scope_as = schism_competitor_faith }
	}
	else = {
		faith:orthodox = { save_scope_as = schism_mainline_faith }
		faith:catholic = { save_scope_as = schism_competitor_faith }
	}
	custom_tooltip = {
		text = pam_create_great_schism_effect_tt
		faith:christian_faith = {
			save_scope_as = old_rite
			every_faith_rite = {
				limit = { rite_has_doctrine = doctrine_no_head }
				change_rite_doctrine = doctrine_spiritual_head
			}
		}
		faith:catholic = { 
			set_religious_head_title = title:k_papal_state
		}
		faith:orthodox = { 
			set_religious_head_title = title:k_orthodox
		}
		rite:roman_rite = {
			save_scope_as = roman_rite
			set_can_convert_to_rite = yes
			set_parent_faith = {
				target = faith:catholic
				main = yes
			}
		}
		rite:insular_celtic = {
			set_can_convert_to_rite = yes
			set_parent_faith = {
				target = faith:catholic
				main = no
			}
		}
		rite:mozarabic_church = {
			set_can_convert_to_rite = yes
			set_parent_faith = {
				target = faith:catholic
				main = no
			}
		}
		rite:ambrosian_rite = {
			set_can_convert_to_rite = yes
			set_parent_faith = {
				target = faith:catholic
				main = no
			}
		}
		rite:byzantine_rite = {
			save_scope_as = byzantine_rite
			set_can_convert_to_rite = yes
			set_parent_faith = {
				target = faith:orthodox
				main = yes
			}
		}
		rite:georgian_rite = {
			set_can_convert_to_rite = yes
			set_parent_faith = {
				target = faith:orthodox
				main = no
			}
		}
		rite:slavic_rite = {
			set_can_convert_to_rite = yes
			set_parent_faith = {
				target = faith:orthodox
				main = no
			}
		}
		rite:carolingian_christianity = {
			set_can_convert_to_rite = no
			set_rite_enabled = no
			set_parent_faith = {
				target = faith:catholic
				main = no
			}
		}
		rite:beneventan_rite = {
			set_can_convert_to_rite = no
			set_rite_enabled = no
			set_parent_faith = {
				target = faith:catholic
				main = no
			}
		}
		struggle:iberian_struggle ?= {
			set_faith_as_involved = rite:mozarabic_church.faith
			set_faith_as_uninvolved = faith:christian_faith
		}
	}
	show_as_tooltip = {
		faith:catholic = { set_religious_head_title = title:k_papal_state }
	}
	hidden_effect = {
		situation:the_christian_church ?= {
			every_situation_participant = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						var:pam_schism_stance ?= flag:mainline
						var:pam_schism_stance ?= flag:competitor
					}
					OR = {
						faith = faith:christian_faith
						faith = scope:schism_mainline_faith
						faith = scope:schism_competitor_faith
					}
					NOR = {
						any_held_title = { is_head_of_faith = yes }
						primary_title ?= { is_holy_order = yes }
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						var:pam_schism_stance ?= flag:mainline
						NOT = { faith = scope:schism_mainline_faith }
					}
					set_character_faith = scope:schism_mainline_faith
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						var:pam_schism_stance ?= flag:competitor
						NOT = { faith = scope:schism_competitor_faith }
					}
					set_character_faith = scope:schism_competitor_faith
				}
			}
		}
		faith:christian_faith = {
			every_clerical_elector_title = {
				add_to_list = pam_schism_cardinalates
			}
		}
		every_in_list = {
			list = pam_schism_cardinalates
			save_temporary_scope_as = pam_schism_cardinalate
			if = {
				limit = {
					holder ?= {
						OR = {
							faith = faith:catholic
							AND = {
								faith = faith:christian_faith
								OR = {
									rite = rite:roman_rite
									rite = rite:insular_celtic
									rite = rite:mozarabic_church
									rite = rite:ambrosian_rite
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				holder = {
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { faith = faith:catholic } }
						set_character_faith = faith:catholic
					}
				}
				add_clerical_elector = faith:catholic
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { exists = holder }
				holder = {
					pam_destroy_cardinal_title_effect = { CARDINALATE = scope:pam_schism_cardinalate }
				}
			}
		}
		faith:christian_faith = { remove_doctrine = special_doctrine_has_clerical_electors }
		# Catholicism starts with a College so AI clergy do not spam the Pope to fill vacancies
		faith:catholic = { pam_seed_cardinals_effect = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { exists = situation:the_christian_church }
			the_christian_church_set_main_power_faith_effect = yes
			the_christian_church_recalculate_participant_groups_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

# Rite followers move with their rites
pam_unite_miaphysite_churches_effect = {
	save_scope_as = unifier
	if = {
		limit = { faith = faith:miaphysitism }
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = pam_unite_miaphysite_churches_effect_tt
			faith = { save_scope_as = uniting_faith }
			faith:armenian_apostolic = {
				save_scope_as = absorbed_faith
				remove_religious_head_title = yes
				every_faith_rite = {
					limit = { faith != scope:uniting_faith }
					set_parent_faith = {
						target = faith:miaphysitism
						main = no
						include_derived = no
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = pam_unite_miaphysite_churches_effect_tt
			faith = { save_scope_as = uniting_faith }
			faith:miaphysitism = {
				save_scope_as = absorbed_faith
				remove_religious_head_title = yes
				every_faith_rite = {
					limit = { faith != scope:uniting_faith }
					set_parent_faith = {
						target = faith:armenian_apostolic
						main = no
						include_derived = no
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	faith = {
		change_fervor = {
			value = medium_fervor_gain
			desc = fervor_gain_mended_schism
		}
	}
	hidden_effect = {
		every_player = {
			limit = {
				this != root
				faith = scope:uniting_faith
			}
			send_interface_message = {
				type = event_generic_good_with_text
				title = pam_unite_miaphysite_churches_message
				desc = pam_unite_miaphysite_churches_message_desc
				left_icon = scope:unifier
			}
		}
	}
	set_nickname_effect = { NICKNAME = nick_the_ecumenist }
}

pam_great_schismat_effect = {
	give_nickname = nick_the_schismat
	dynasty ?= {
		add_dynasty_modifier = { modifier = pam_caused_great_schism_dynasty_modifier }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { exists = dynasty } }
		add_character_modifier = pam_caused_great_schism_character_modifier
	}
	# 0061 already announces the Schism
	set_global_variable = {
		name = pam_skip_tcc_phase_start_event
		days = 30
	}
	situation:the_christian_church = {
		change_top_phase = { phase = concord }
	}
	save_scope_as = schismat
}

pam_create_great_schism_effect = {
	set_global_variable = {
		name = pam_skip_tcc_phase_start_event
		days = 30
	}
	pam_apply_great_schism_creation_effect = yes
	set_global_variable = pam_great_schism_decision_taken
	faith:catholic = { save_scope_as = schism_western_faith }
	faith:orthodox = { save_scope_as = schism_eastern_faith }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { exists = scope:schismat } }
		situation:the_christian_church = {
			change_top_phase = { phase = reform_phase }
		}
	}
	situation:the_christian_church ?= {
		every_situation_participant = {
			limit = {
				is_ruler = yes
				faith.like_christianity_religion_trigger = yes
			}
			trigger_event = pam_the_christian_church.0061
		}
	}
}

pam_prevent_great_schism_effect = {
	custom_tooltip = pam_prevent_great_schism_aftermath_tt
	give_nickname = nick_the_concordant
	dynasty ?= {
		add_dynasty_modifier = { modifier = pam_prevented_great_schism_dynasty_modifier }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { exists = dynasty } }
		add_character_modifier = pam_prevented_great_schism_character_modifier
	}
	# 0062 already announces the Schism
	set_global_variable = {
		name = pam_skip_tcc_phase_start_event
		days = 30
	}
	situation:the_christian_church = {
		change_top_phase = { phase = concord }
	}
}

# Run from either Mend the Great Schism decision, which pass the modifier pair matching their route.
# MODIFIER - dynasty modifier for the mender's dynasty.
# CHARACTER_MODIFIER - equivalent for a lowborn mender, who has no dynasty to mark.
pam_mend_great_schism_reward_effect = {
	dynasty ?= {
		add_dynasty_prestige = 10000
		add_dynasty_modifier = { modifier = $MODIFIER$ }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { exists = dynasty } }
		add_character_modifier = $CHARACTER_MODIFIER$
	}
	add_piety = monumental_piety_gain
	add_prestige = monumental_prestige_gain
	set_nickname_effect = { NICKNAME = nick_the_reconciler }
}

# Character scope, saved so the decision tooltip, 0062 option, and seating all name the same see
# Falls back to Rome so an unheld Constantinople does not leave the faith headless
pam_save_schism_head_of_faith_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			rite = rite:byzantine_rite
			exists = title:d_et_constantinople.holder
		}
		title:d_et_constantinople = { save_scope_as = new_head_of_faith_title }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			rite = rite:byzantine_rite
			exists = title:k_orthodox.holder
		}
		title:k_orthodox = { save_scope_as = new_head_of_faith_title }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { exists = title:k_papal_state.holder }
		title:k_papal_state = { save_scope_as = new_head_of_faith_title }
	}
	scope:new_head_of_faith_title.holder ?= { save_scope_as = new_head_of_faith }
}

# Needs pam_save_schism_head_of_faith_effect
pam_schism_seat_head_of_faith_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:new_head_of_faith_title }
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = pam_schism_seat_head_of_faith_tt
			faith:christian_faith = {
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_doctrine = doctrine_spiritual_head } }
					change_doctrine = doctrine_spiritual_head
				}
				set_religious_head_title = scope:new_head_of_faith_title
				# Carolingian Christianity has doctrine_no_head; leave it behind once the faith has a head
				hidden_effect = {
					every_faith_rite = {
						if = {
							limit = { rite_has_doctrine = doctrine_no_head }
							change_rite_doctrine = doctrine_spiritual_head
						}
						force_refresh_head_of_rite = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# The seat kept vacant. Chalcedonianism is already headless and keeps doctrine_spiritual_head, so
# there is nothing to change - doctrine_no_head would bar Establish a Head of Faith for good.
pam_schism_leave_faith_headless_effect = {
	custom_tooltip = pam_schism_leave_faith_headless_tt
}

# Apply an ancestral Khachkar in the chosen county (for decision confirmation event).
pam_apply_ancestral_khachkar_effects = {
	$LOCATION$ = {
		save_scope_as = khachkar_location
		set_variable = {
			name = pam_khachkar_ancestor
			value = $ANCESTOR$
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = pam_khachkar_commissioner
			value = root
		}
		add_county_modifier = {
			modifier = pam_khachkar_ancestral_county
			desc = pam_khachkar_ancestral_county_custom_desc
		}
	}
	add_prestige = medium_prestige_value
	add_piety = medium_piety_value
	add_legitimacy = 100
	remove_variable = pam_khachkar_ancestral_ancestor
}

# Remember a same-faith expansion outside the see's home kingdom so later petitions are harder to accept
pam_mark_recent_same_faith_clerical_expansion_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = $TITLE$
			pam_expand_target_is_same_faith_outside_home_kingdom_trigger = {
				TITLE = $TITLE$
				ARCHBISHOP = $ARCHBISHOP$
			}
		}
		$ARCHBISHOP$ = {
			hidden_effect = {
				add_character_flag = {
					flag = pam_recently_expanded_same_faith
					years = pam_expand_same_faith_recent_years
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Expand an Ecclesiastical Title as HoF - on_accept
pam_expand_ecclesiastical_title_interaction_effect = {
	scope:recipient = {
		save_scope_as = clerical_region_holder
		# We use the picker's region and skip alternative_limit, so we do not land on a non-bordering see
		if = {
			limit = {
				capital_province.county.clerical_region_title ?= {
					has_clerical_region = yes
					holder ?= scope:clerical_region_holder
				}
			}
			capital_province.county.clerical_region_title = {
				save_scope_as = clerical_region_title
			}
		}
	}
	scope:target = {
		save_scope_as = target_duchy
	}
	# is_adjacent_to_clerical_region matches the counties expand_clerical_region would take
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:clerical_region_title
			scope:clerical_region_title = { is_adjacent_to_clerical_region = scope:target_duchy }
		}
		scope:actor = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_character_situation = {
						situation_type = the_christian_church
						situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_the_christian_church_clerical_region_expanded
					}
				}
				situation:the_christian_church = {
					trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_the_christian_church_clerical_region_expanded
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_effect_good
					title = expand_ecclesiastical_title_toast
					left_icon = scope:clerical_region_holder
					right_icon = scope:clerical_region_title
					custom_tooltip = {
						text = expand_ecclesiastical_title_effect_tt
						scope:clerical_region_title = {
							expand_clerical_region = scope:target_duchy
						}
					}
				}
			}
			faith = {
				change_fervor = {
					value = notable_fervor_gain
					desc = fervor_gain_clerical_title_expanded
				}
			}
		}
		scope:clerical_region_holder = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { this = scope:actor }
				}
				add_opinion = {
					target = scope:actor
					modifier = grateful_opinion
					opinion = 50
				}
			}
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = expand_ecclesiastical_title_toast
				left_icon = scope:actor
				right_icon = scope:clerical_region_title
				custom_tooltip = expand_ecclesiastical_title_effect_tt
			}
			pam_mark_recent_same_faith_clerical_expansion_effect = {
				TITLE = scope:target_duchy
				ARCHBISHOP = this
			}
		}
	}
}

# Split an Ecclesiastical Title as HoF - on_accept
pam_split_ecclesiastical_title_interaction_effect = {
	scope:secondary_recipient = {
		save_scope_as = new_ecclesiastical_holder
	}
	scope:secondary_actor = {
		save_scope_as = secular_ruler
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		save_scope_as = clerical_region_holder
	}
	scope:secular_ruler.capital_county.clerical_region_title ?= {
		save_scope_as = clerical_region_title
	}
	# Interaction tooltip reads on_accept before the split
	show_as_tooltip = {
		pam_split_ecclesiastical_title_outcome_effect = yes
	}
	hidden_effect = {
		scope:secular_ruler = {
			capital_province = { save_scope_as = region_capital }
			split_clerical_region = {
				name = CLERICAL_REGION_PROVINCE_TITLE_NAME
				save_scope_as = new_title
			}
		}
		# A failed split leaves scope:new_title unset
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:new_title }
			pam_split_ecclesiastical_title_outcome_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

# Runs twice, once for the interaction window tooltip and once for real after a successful split
pam_split_ecclesiastical_title_outcome_effect = {
	scope:secular_ruler = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:new_ecclesiastical_holder = {
					NOT = {
						is_courtier_of = scope:secular_ruler
					}
					is_ruler = no
				}
			}
			add_courtier = scope:new_ecclesiastical_holder
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:new_ecclesiastical_holder = {
					NOT = { is_councillor_of = scope:secular_ruler }
				}
			}
			assign_councillor_type = {
				type = councillor_court_chaplain
				remove_existing_councillor = yes
				target = scope:new_ecclesiastical_holder
			}
		}
	}
	hidden_effect = {
		create_title_and_vassal_change = {
			type = created
			save_scope_as = change
			add_claim_on_loss = no
		}
		scope:new_title ?= {
			set_coa = scope:secular_ruler.primary_title
			set_color_from_title = scope:secular_ruler.primary_title
			set_capital_county = scope:secular_ruler.capital_county
			set_landless_title = yes
			set_no_automatic_claims = yes

			change_title_holder = {
				holder = scope:new_ecclesiastical_holder
				change = scope:change
			}
			resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:change
		}
	}
	scope:actor = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_character_situation = {
					situation_type = the_christian_church
					situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_the_christian_church_clerical_region_split
				}
			}
			situation:the_christian_church = {
				trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_the_christian_church_clerical_region_split
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = split_ecclesiastical_title_toast
				left_icon = scope:actor
				right_icon = scope:new_ecclesiastical_holder
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = split_ecclesiastical_title_effect_tt
				}
			}
		}
		reverse_add_opinion = {
			target = scope:new_ecclesiastical_holder
			modifier = grateful_opinion
			opinion = 50
		}
		reverse_add_opinion = {
			target = scope:secondary_actor
			modifier = pleased_opinion
			opinion = 25
		}
		if = {
			# On a self-split the HoF reorganises their own region, so there is no aggrieved superior to anger
			limit = { scope:recipient != scope:actor }
			reverse_add_opinion = {
				target = scope:recipient
				modifier = angry_opinion
				opinion = -50
			}
		}
		add_hook = {
			type = favor_hook
			target = scope:new_ecclesiastical_holder
		}
	}
	if = {
		# Skip the "your region was split" notification when the HoF split their own region
		limit = { scope:recipient != scope:actor }
		scope:recipient = {
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_bad
				title = split_ecclesiastical_title_toast
				left_icon = scope:actor
				right_icon = scope:new_ecclesiastical_holder
				custom_tooltip = split_ecclesiastical_title_effect_tt
			}
		}
	}
	scope:new_ecclesiastical_holder = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { government_has_flag = government_is_ecclesiastical }
			}
			change_government = ecclesiastical_government
		}
	}
}

# Merge an Ecclesiastical Title as HoF - on_accept
pam_merge_new_ecclesiastical_title_interaction_effect = {
	$ABSORBING_TITLE$ ?= {
		save_scope_as = absorbing_title
		holder ?= { save_scope_as = absorbing_holder }
	}
	$ABSORBED_TITLE$ = {
		save_scope_as = absorbed_title
		holder = {
			save_scope_as = absorbed_holder
			scope:absorbed_holder = {
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_effect_bad
					title = request_ecclesiastical_title_merger_absorbed_tt
					left_icon = scope:absorbing_holder
					right_icon = scope:absorbed_title
					if = {
						limit = { is_ai = yes }
						# Absorbed region holder is angry at the initiator
						add_opinion = {
							target = $SENDER$
							modifier = angry_opinion
							opinion = -75
						}
						progress_towards_rival_effect = {
							CHARACTER = $SENDER$
							OPINION = 0
							REASON = rival_ecclesiastical_title_merged
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	# Toast to the initiator
	$SENDER$ = {
		hidden_effect = {
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = merge_new_ecclesiastical_title_toast
				left_icon = scope:absorbing_holder
				right_icon = scope:absorbed_title
				# Perform the region merge
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = merge_new_ecclesiastical_title_effect_tt
					scope:absorbing_title = {
						grow_clerical_region_from = scope:absorbed_title
						set_variable = {
							name = merge_clerical_region_cooldown
							years = merge_clerical_region_cooldown_years_value
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	scope:absorbing_holder = {
		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = merge_new_ecclesiastical_title_toast
			left_icon = $SENDER$
			right_icon = scope:absorbed_title
			custom_tooltip = merge_new_ecclesiastical_title_effect_tt
			# The puppet variant sends on its own behalf, so there is no favour owed.
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { this = $SENDER$ } }
				if = {
					limit = { is_ai = yes }
					add_opinion = {
						target = $SENDER$
						modifier = grateful_opinion
						opinion = 50
					}
				}
				$SENDER$ = {
					add_hook = {
						type = favor_hook
						target = scope:absorbing_holder
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

pam_secure_pilgrim_routes_effect = {
	faith = {
		set_variable = {
			name = pam_secured_pilgrim_routes
			years = 25
		}
		main_rite = {
			remove_rite_tenet = scope:removed_tenet
			add_rite_tenet = tenet_armed_pilgrimages
		}
		# The removed tenet loses, and the new core tenet gains popularity.
		if = {
			limit = { has_tenet_popularity_trigger = yes }
			change_tenet_popularity = {
				target = scope:removed_tenet
				change = major_tenet_popularity_loss
			}
			change_tenet_popularity = {
				target = tenet:tenet_armed_pilgrimages
				change = major_tenet_popularity_gain
			}
		}
	}
	add_character_modifier = {
		modifier = pam_protector_of_pilgrims_modifier
		years = 25
	}
	custom_tooltip = pam_secure_pilgrim_routes_pilgrim_xp_tt
	custom_tooltip = pam_secure_pilgrim_routes_ai_pilgrimage_tt
}

spiritual_hof_or_realm_priest_superior_recipient_redirect_effect = {
	#redirect low tiers targeting HoF to archbishops
	if = {
		limit = {
			highest_held_title_tier < tier_kingdom
			faith.religious_head ?= scope:recipient
			OR = {
				exists = cp:councillor_court_chaplain.superior
				AND = {
					government_has_flag = government_is_theocracy
					exists = superior
				}
			}
		}
		#Actor is a priest themselves
		if = {
			limit = {
				AND = {
					government_has_flag = government_is_theocracy
					exists = superior
				}
			}
			superior = { save_scope_as = recipient }
		}
		#Actor is secular
		else = {
			cp:councillor_court_chaplain.superior = {
				save_scope_as = recipient
			}
		}
	}
	#redirect high tiers targeting archbishops to HoF
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			highest_held_title_tier >= tier_kingdom
			OR = {
				superior ?= scope:recipient
				cp:councillor_court_chaplain.superior ?= scope:recipient
			}
			faith = {
				has_doctrine = doctrine_spiritual_head
				religious_head ?= {
					NOR = {
						this = scope:recipient
						this = scope:puppet_or_actor
					}
				}
			}
		}
		faith.religious_head = {
			save_scope_as = recipient
		}
	}
}
non_pope_lifting_excommunication_penalty_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:puppet_or_actor = {
				has_character_flag = pope_excom
			}
			NOT = {
				scope:puppet_or_actor.faith.religious_head ?= scope:recipient
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			add_piety = massive_piety_loss
			add_piety_level = -2
		}
		scope:puppet_or_actor.faith.religious_head = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = scope:recipient
				opinion = -80
				modifier = betrayal_opinion
			}
		}
	}
}

seek_indulgences_refusal_effect = {
	scope:recipient = {
		add_prestige = {
			value = minor_prestige_value
			multiply = {
				value = scope:actor.highest_held_title_tier
				subtract = 1
			}
		}
	}
	scope:actor = {
		clerical_interaction_refusal_minor_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:recipient = {
					is_ai = yes
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:actor = {
						is_ai = yes
					}
				} 
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = pam_refused_indulgences_modifier_small
					years = 5
					desc = refused_indulgences_effect_tt
				}
				custom_tooltip = refused_indulgences_ai_personality_effect_tt
				custom_tooltip = refused_indulgences_ai_war_effect_tt
				custom_tooltip = refused_indulgences_ai_amenity_effect_tt
			}
			else = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = pam_refused_indulgences_modifier_small
					years = 5
				}
			}
			change_spiritual_fulfillment = minor_spiritual_fulfillment_loss
		}
		else = {
			change_spiritual_fulfillment = medium_spiritual_fulfillment_loss
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:actor = {
						is_ai = yes
					}
				}
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = pam_refused_indulgences_modifier
					years = 5
					desc = refused_indulgences_effect_tt
				}
				custom_tooltip = refused_indulgences_ai_personality_effect_tt
				custom_tooltip = refused_indulgences_ai_war_effect_tt
				custom_tooltip = refused_indulgences_ai_amenity_effect_tt
			}
			else = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = pam_refused_indulgences_modifier
					years = 5
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
divorce_house_feud_effect = {
	house_feud_start_effect = {
		# Feuding House Head
		ACTOR = scope:divorcer.house.house_head
		# Target House Head
		TARGET = scope:secondary_recipient.house.house_head
		# Feud Reason
		REASON = divorce
		# House Member attacker if relevant 
		ATTACKER = scope:divorcer
		# House Member victim if relevant
		VICTIM = scope:secondary_recipient
	}
	scope:secondary_recipient = {
		add_prestige_level = -1
	}
}
divorce_holy_order_lease_effect = {
	scope:barony_to_lease = { lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:lease_order }
}
divorce_get_hook_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:forcing_divorce
		}
		add_hook = {
			type = favor_hook
			target = scope:forcing_divorce
		}
	}
	else = {
		add_hook = {
			type = favor_hook
			target = scope:divorcer
		}
	}
	
}
divorce_put_spouse_in_monastery_effect = {
	scope:secondary_recipient = {
		add_trait = devoted
		scope:recipient = {
			add_courtier = scope:secondary_recipient
		}
	}
}

#############################
# Tenet popularity          #
