# on_personal_tenet_gain fires this, with root as the character and scope:tenet the gained tenet
# Popularity gain is the lowest possible value, scaled by title tier
pam_tenet_popularity_on_personal_tenet_gain_effect = {
	save_scope_as = tp_personal_tenet_changer
	faith = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_tenet_popularity_trigger = yes }
			change_tenet_popularity = {
				target = scope:tenet
				change = scope:tp_personal_tenet_changer.pam_personal_tenet_popularity_gain
			}
		}
	}
}

# on_personal_tenet_loss runs this with root as the character and scope:tenet as the dropped tenet
pam_tenet_popularity_on_personal_tenet_loss_effect = {
	save_scope_as = tp_personal_tenet_changer
	faith = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_tenet_popularity_trigger = yes }
			change_tenet_popularity = {
				target = scope:tenet
				change = scope:tp_personal_tenet_changer.pam_personal_tenet_popularity_loss
			}
		}
	}
}

# The newly elected pope is root
pam_tenet_popularity_on_new_pope_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { faith = { has_tenet_popularity_trigger = yes } }
		faith = { save_scope_as = tp_new_pope_faith }
		every_personal_tenet = {
			save_scope_as = tp_new_pope_tenet
			scope:tp_new_pope_faith = {
				change_tenet_popularity = {
					target = scope:tp_new_pope_tenet
					change = medium_tenet_popularity_gain
				}
			}
		}
		rite = {
			every_rite_tenet = {
				status = core
				save_scope_as = tp_new_pope_tenet
				scope:tp_new_pope_faith = {
					change_tenet_popularity = {
						target = scope:tp_new_pope_tenet
						change = medium_tenet_popularity_gain
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

pam_set_pope_rite_mainline_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = rite
			rite != faith.main_rite
		}
		rite = {
			set_main_rite = yes
		}
	}
}

pam_pope_core_tenet_popularity_catalyst_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
			exists = scope:tenet_to_add
			like_christianity_religion_trigger = yes
			exists = situation:the_christian_church
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				"faith.tenet_popularity(scope:tenet_to_add)" >= tenet_popularity_popular_threshold
				situation:the_christian_church = {
					situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_pope_declared_popular_core_tenet
				}
			}
			situation:the_christian_church = {
				trigger_situation_catalyst = {
					catalyst = catalyst_pope_declared_popular_core_tenet
					character = root
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				"faith.tenet_popularity(scope:tenet_to_add)" < tenet_popularity_popular_threshold
				situation:the_christian_church = {
					situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_pope_declared_unpopular_core_tenet
				}
			}
			situation:the_christian_church = {
				trigger_situation_catalyst = {
					catalyst = catalyst_pope_declared_unpopular_core_tenet
					character = root
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Head of Faith enacts a papal bull. Called from the after block of each bull event, where root is the issuer.
pam_the_christian_church_papal_bull_enacted_catalyst_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
			like_christianity_religion_trigger = yes
			situation:the_christian_church ?= {
				situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_the_christian_church_papal_bull_enacted
			}
		}
		situation:the_christian_church = {
			trigger_situation_catalyst = {
				catalyst = catalyst_the_christian_church_papal_bull_enacted
				character = root
			}
		}
	}
}

pam_react_to_new_pope_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
			current_date_is_start_date_trigger = no
			is_alive = yes
			is_ruler = yes
		}
		save_scope_as = new_pope
		faith = {
			every_faith_character = {
				limit = {
					is_ruler = yes
					this != scope:new_pope
				}
				hidden_effect = { remove_character_modifier = pam_denied_new_pope_modifier }
				if = {
					limit = { has_variable = pam_new_pope_events_as_feed_message }
					send_interface_message = {
						type = msg_pam_new_pope_elected
						title = {
							first_valid = {
								triggered_desc = {
									trigger = { scope:new_pope = { is_female = yes } }
									desc = pam_clergy_events.0030.t.female
								}
								desc = pam_clergy_events.0030.t
							}
						}
						desc = pam_clergy_events.0030.feed
						left_icon = scope:new_pope
					}
				}
				else = {
					trigger_event = pam_clergy_events.0030
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Root = the kinsman who just took the office. $TITLE$ = the office title, $KIND$ = flag:head_of_faith / flag:antipope / flag:archbishop
pam_family_becomes_prelate_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
			current_date_is_start_date_trigger = no
			is_alive = yes
			like_christianity_religion_trigger = yes
			# Every string in 0060 names the dynasty, so a lowborn prelate has no story to tell here
			exists = dynasty
		}
		save_scope_as = new_prelate
		$TITLE$ = { save_scope_as = prelate_title }
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = prelate_office
			value = $KIND$
		}
		every_player = {
			limit = {
				this != scope:new_prelate
				OR = {
					is_close_or_extended_family_of = scope:new_prelate
					house ?= scope:new_prelate.house
				}
				# Archdioceses change hands far more often than the great offices do
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { scope:prelate_office = flag:archbishop }
					NOT = { has_character_flag = pam_family_prelate_cooldown }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { scope:prelate_office = flag:archbishop }
				add_character_flag = {
					flag = pam_family_prelate_cooldown
					years = 10
				}
			}
			trigger_event = {
				id = pam_clergy_events.0060
				days = { 3 5 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# Root = the new archbishop. $TITLE$ = the clerical region title that changed hands.
pam_react_to_new_archbishop_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
			current_date_is_start_date_trigger = no
			is_alive = yes
		}
		save_scope_as = new_archbishop
		$TITLE$ = { save_scope_as = archdiocese }
		every_player = {
			limit = {
				this != scope:new_archbishop
				is_ruler = yes
				# The clerical_region_title is geographic, so a non-Christian holder would be told they answer to it
				like_christianity_religion_trigger = yes
				capital_county.clerical_region_title ?= scope:archdiocese
			}
			send_interface_message = {
				type = msg_pam_new_archbishop
				left_icon = scope:new_archbishop
				right_icon = scope:archdiocese
			}
		}
	}
}

pam_tenet_popularity_on_evangelized_tenet_effect = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = pam_evangelizer
	faith = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_tenet_popularity_trigger = yes }
			change_tenet_popularity = {
				target = $TENET$
				change = {
					value = $CHANGE$
					# A Faithful Flock dynasty legacy: evangelizing dynasty members spread their tenets further
					if = {
						limit = {
							scope:pam_evangelizer = {
								dynasty ?= { has_dynasty_perk = pam_legacy_3 }
							}
						}
						multiply = 1.5
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Root = preacher, $TENET$ = the tenet
# Once per preacher per activity, and we keep the record on the activity so it cannot leak
pam_spread_tenet_flat_popularity_once_effect = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = pam_spread_flat_preacher
	if = {
		limit = {
			involved_activity ?= {
				NAND = {
					has_variable_list = pam_spread_tenet_flat_paid
					is_target_in_variable_list = {
						name = pam_spread_tenet_flat_paid
						target = scope:pam_spread_flat_preacher
					}
				}
			}
		}
		involved_activity = {
			add_to_variable_list = {
				name = pam_spread_tenet_flat_paid
				target = scope:pam_spread_flat_preacher
			}
		}
		pam_tenet_popularity_on_evangelized_tenet_effect = {
			TENET = $TENET$
			CHANGE = medium_tenet_popularity_gain
		}
	}
}

# A converting character is root, scope:old_rite the previous rite
pam_tenet_popularity_on_rite_change_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { is_landed = yes }
		faith = { save_scope_as = tp_new_faith }
		rite = { save_scope_as = tp_new_rite }
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:old_rite
				scope:old_rite != scope:tp_new_rite
			}
			scope:old_rite.faith = { save_scope_as = tp_old_faith }
			if = {
				limit = { scope:tp_old_faith = { has_tenet_popularity_trigger = yes } }
				scope:old_rite = {
					every_rite_tenet = {
						status = core
						save_scope_as = tp_tenet
						scope:tp_old_faith = {
							change_tenet_popularity = {
								target = scope:tp_tenet
								change = miniscule_tenet_popularity_loss
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { scope:tp_new_faith = { has_tenet_popularity_trigger = yes } }
			scope:tp_new_rite = {
				every_rite_tenet = {
					status = core
					save_scope_as = tp_tenet
					scope:tp_new_faith = {
						change_tenet_popularity = {
							target = scope:tp_tenet
							change = miniscule_tenet_popularity_gain
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# on_county_faith_change sets root as the county and scope:old_faith as the previous faith
# var:tp_popularity_rite remembers the last credited rite so the old faith can be uncredited
pam_tenet_popularity_on_county_faith_change_effect = {
	faith = { save_scope_as = tp_new_faith }
	rite = { save_scope_as = tp_new_rite }
	if = {
		limit = { exists = var:tp_popularity_rite }
		var:tp_popularity_rite = { save_scope_as = tp_old_rite }
		if = {
			limit = { scope:old_faith = { has_tenet_popularity_trigger = yes } }
			scope:tp_old_rite = {
				every_rite_tenet = {
					status = core
					save_scope_as = tp_tenet
					scope:old_faith = {
						change_tenet_popularity = {
							target = scope:tp_tenet
							change = miniscule_tenet_popularity_loss
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { scope:tp_new_faith = { has_tenet_popularity_trigger = yes } }
		scope:tp_new_rite = {
			every_rite_tenet = {
				status = core
				save_scope_as = tp_tenet
				scope:tp_new_faith = {
					change_tenet_popularity = {
						target = scope:tp_tenet
						change = miniscule_tenet_popularity_gain
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	set_variable = {
		name = tp_popularity_rite
		value = rite
	}
}

# Study Rite: scope:discovered_tenet learned by root (a character).
pam_study_rite_tenet_popularity_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:discovered_tenet
			faith = { has_tenet_popularity_trigger = yes }
		}
		faith = {
			change_tenet_popularity = {
				target = scope:discovered_tenet
				change = minor_tenet_popularity_gain
			}
		}
	}
}

# on_rite_created. scope:new_rite must exist; root is founder.
pam_tenet_popularity_on_rite_created_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:new_rite.faith ?= { has_tenet_popularity_trigger = yes }
		}
		root = { save_scope_as = rite_founder }
		scope:new_rite = {
			save_scope_as = created_rite
			every_rite_tenet = {
				status = core
				save_scope_as = tenet
				scope:created_rite.faith = {
					change_tenet_popularity = {
						target = scope:tenet
						change = scope:created_rite.tenet_popularity_rite_created_gain
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Without this, clerical courts fill from generic pool templates and feel secular
pam_seed_monk_courtiers_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { pam_court_wants_monk_courtiers_trigger = yes }
		save_temporary_scope_as = monk_employer
		while = {
			count = 4 # Hard bound matching the highest quota; the limit below is what actually ends the loop
			limit = {
				any_courtier = {
					count < pam_monk_courtier_target_value
					OR = {
						has_trait = devoted
						has_trait = cleric
					}
				}
			}
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					create_character = { # No location: create_character rejects location and employer together
						employer = scope:monk_employer
						template = pam_monk_courtier_template
					}
				}
				50 = {
					create_character = {
						employer = scope:monk_employer
						template = pam_cleric_courtier_template
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Game start only, because if the three-year pulse ran this the split would creep toward all courts
pam_seed_nun_courtier_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			pam_court_wants_monk_courtiers_trigger = yes
			NOT = {
				any_courtier = {
					is_female = yes
					has_trait = devoted
				}
			}
		}
		save_temporary_scope_as = monk_employer
		random = {
			chance = 50
			create_character = {
				employer = scope:monk_employer
				template = pam_nun_courtier_template
			}
		}
	}
}

# Here we run this on the courtier, and it expects scope:pam_clerical_patron
pam_clerical_court_ordain_effect = {
	save_scope_as = pam_clerical_ordained
	scope:pam_clerical_patron = {
		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = pam_clerical_court_ordain_toast
			left_icon = scope:pam_clerical_ordained
			scope:pam_clerical_ordained = {
				add_trait = cleric
				if = {
					limit = { learning < scope:pam_clerical_patron.pam_clerical_court_learning_cap_value }
					add_learning_skill = pam_clerical_court_learning_gain_value
				}
				add_opinion = {
					target = scope:pam_clerical_patron
					modifier = grateful_for_calling_opinion
				}
			}
			add_piety = pam_clerical_court_piety_gain_value
		}
	}
}

# scope:pam_clerical_patron is required here, and we run this on the courtier
pam_clerical_court_instruct_effect = {
	save_scope_as = pam_clerical_instructed
	scope:pam_clerical_patron = {
		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = pam_clerical_court_instruct_toast
			left_icon = scope:pam_clerical_instructed
			scope:pam_clerical_instructed = {
				if = {
					limit = { learning < scope:pam_clerical_patron.pam_clerical_court_learning_cap_value }
					add_learning_skill = pam_clerical_court_learning_gain_value
				}
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_trait = lifestyle_scholar } }
					add_trait = lifestyle_scholar
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						has_trait_xp = { trait = lifestyle_scholar value < 100 }
					}
					add_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_scholar
						value = 20
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_trait = lifestyle_mystic } }
					add_trait = lifestyle_mystic
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						has_trait_xp = { trait = lifestyle_mystic value < 100 }
					}
					add_trait_xp = {
						trait = lifestyle_mystic
						value = 25
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Recruiting rulers run this, and it expects scope:pam_clerical_patron
pam_clerical_court_recruit_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:pam_clerical_novice_current }
		clear_saved_scope = pam_clerical_novice_current
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:pam_clerical_novice
			# Can cease to be in the pool between the event firing and the option executing
			scope:pam_clerical_novice = { is_pool_character = yes }
		}
		scope:pam_clerical_novice = { save_scope_as = pam_clerical_novice_current }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			any_pool_character = {
				province = scope:pam_clerical_patron.location
				pam_clerical_court_novice_trigger = { RULER = scope:pam_clerical_patron }
				faith = scope:pam_clerical_patron.faith
			}
		}
		random_pool_character = {
			province = scope:pam_clerical_patron.location
			limit = {
				pam_clerical_court_novice_trigger = { RULER = scope:pam_clerical_patron }
				faith = scope:pam_clerical_patron.faith
			}
			save_scope_as = pam_clerical_novice_current
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			any_pool_character = {
				province = scope:pam_clerical_patron.location
				pam_clerical_court_novice_trigger = { RULER = scope:pam_clerical_patron }
			}
		}
		random_pool_character = {
			province = scope:pam_clerical_patron.location
			limit = { pam_clerical_court_novice_trigger = { RULER = scope:pam_clerical_patron } }
			save_scope_as = pam_clerical_novice_current
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:pam_clerical_novice_current }
		add_courtier = scope:pam_clerical_novice_current
		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = pam_clerical_court_recruit_toast
			left_icon = scope:pam_clerical_novice_current
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { scope:pam_clerical_novice_current.faith = scope:pam_clerical_patron.faith }
				}
				scope:pam_clerical_novice_current = {
					set_character_faith = scope:pam_clerical_patron.faith
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { scope:pam_clerical_novice_current = { NOT = { rite = scope:pam_clerical_patron.rite } } }
				scope:pam_clerical_novice_current = {
					set_character_rite = scope:pam_clerical_patron.rite
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { scope:pam_clerical_novice_current = { NOT = { has_trait = cleric } } }
				scope:pam_clerical_novice_current = {
					add_trait = cleric
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# At game start we leave prohibited tenets at zero
pam_tenet_popularity_on_game_start_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes }
		religion:tyrism_religion = { #Warcraft
			every_faith = {
				limit = { num_county_followers >= 1 }
				save_scope_as = tp_faith
				main_rite = { save_scope_as = tp_rite }
				# Any follower works as the host scope for the tenet iterator; rite_filter drives the list.
				random_faith_county = {
					limit = { exists = holder }
					holder = {
						pam_tenet_popularity_seed_status_effect = { RITE = scope:tp_rite FAITH = scope:tp_faith STATUS = core VALUE = massive_tenet_popularity_value }
						pam_tenet_popularity_seed_status_effect = { RITE = scope:tp_rite FAITH = scope:tp_faith STATUS = permitted VALUE = major_tenet_popularity_value }
						pam_tenet_popularity_seed_status_effect = { RITE = scope:tp_rite FAITH = scope:tp_faith STATUS = known VALUE = medium_tenet_popularity_value }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# $RITE$ is the rite scope, run after a title adopts a new state rite
pam_tenet_popularity_boost_rite_effect = {
	$RITE$ = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
				faith.like_christianity_religion_trigger = yes
				faith = { num_county_followers >= 1 }
			}
			save_scope_as = tp_boost_rite
			faith = {
				save_scope_as = tp_boost_faith
				# Any follower works as the host scope for the tenet iterator; rite_filter drives the list.
				random_faith_county = {
					limit = { exists = holder }
					holder = {
						pam_tenet_popularity_seed_status_effect = { RITE = scope:tp_boost_rite FAITH = scope:tp_boost_faith STATUS = core VALUE = massive_tenet_popularity_value }
						pam_tenet_popularity_seed_status_effect = { RITE = scope:tp_boost_rite FAITH = scope:tp_boost_faith STATUS = permitted VALUE = major_tenet_popularity_value }
						pam_tenet_popularity_seed_status_effect = { RITE = scope:tp_boost_rite FAITH = scope:tp_boost_faith STATUS = known VALUE = medium_tenet_popularity_value }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Credits every $RITE$ tenet at $STATUS$ with $VALUE$ popularity for $FAITH$
# Root is only a host for the tenet iterator, and its own knowledge is ignored
pam_tenet_popularity_seed_status_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { $FAITH$ = { has_tenet_popularity_trigger = yes } }
		every_character_tenet = {
			rite_filter = $RITE$
			status = $STATUS$
			save_scope_as = tp_seed_tenet
			$FAITH$ = {
				change_tenet_popularity = {
					target = scope:tp_seed_tenet
					change = $VALUE$
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Root is character that will have their personal tenet replaced with TENET
pam_replace_personal_tenet_effect = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = tenet_char
	if = {
		limit = {
			free_personal_tenet_slots = 0
		}
		random_personal_tenet = {
			weight = {
				base = 10
				modifier = {
					add = pam_tenet_popularity_impact_value
				}
			}
			scope:tenet_char = {
				remove_personal_tenet = prev
			}
		}
	}
	set_personal_tenet = $TENET$
}

pam_notify_holy_war_instigator_effect_win = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:war ?= {
				has_variable = holy_instigator
				var:holy_instigator = {
					is_alive = yes
					save_temporary_scope_as = instigator_temp
				}
				NOT = {
					any_war_participant = {
						this = scope:instigator_temp
					}
				}
			}
		}
		every_in_list = {
			list = target_titles
			save_scope_as = target
		}
		
		scope:war = {
			var:holy_instigator = {
				save_scope_as = cleric
				send_interface_message = {
					type = event_generic_good
					title = pam_instigated_holy_war_won.t
					left_icon = scope:attacker
					right_icon = scope:defender

					if = {
						limit = {
							is_ai = no
						}
						add_prestige = pam_holy_war_inciter_prestige_value
					}
					else = {
						hidden_effect = {
							add_prestige = pam_holy_war_inciter_prestige_value
						}
					}

					custom_tooltip = pam_instigated_holy_war_won_tt
					if = {
						limit = {
							pam_can_expand_clerical_region_after_war_trigger = { HEATHEN = scope:defender }
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
								any_in_list = {
									list = target_titles
									tier = tier_county
								}
							}
							every_in_list = {
								list = target_titles
								duchy = {
									scope:cleric = {
										add_to_variable_list = {
											name = clergy_expands
											target = prev
										}
									}
								}
							}
						}
						else_if = {
							limit = {
								any_in_list = {
									list = target_titles
									tier = tier_duchy
								}
							}
							every_in_list = {
								list = target_titles
								scope:cleric = {
									add_to_variable_list = {
										name = clergy_expands
										target = prev
									}
								}
							}
						}
						else = {
							every_in_list = {
								list = target_titles
								every_direct_de_jure_vassal_title = {
									limit = {
										any_de_jure_county = {
											holder = {
												target_is_same_character_or_above = scope:defender
											}
										}
									}
									scope:cleric = {
										add_to_variable_list = {
											name = clergy_expands
											target = prev
										}
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
pam_notify_holy_war_instigator_effect_draw = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:war ?= {
				has_variable = holy_instigator
				var:holy_instigator = {
					is_alive = yes
					save_temporary_scope_as = instigator_temp
				}
				NOT = {
					any_war_participant = {
						this = scope:instigator_temp
					}
				}
			}
		}
		every_in_list = {
			list = target_titles
			save_scope_as = target
		}
		scope:war = {
			var:holy_instigator = {
				send_interface_message = {
					type = event_generic_bad
					title = pam_instigated_holy_war_draw.t
					left_icon = scope:attacker
					right_icon = scope:defender
					custom_tooltip = pam_instigated_holy_war_draw_tt
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
pam_notify_holy_war_instigator_effect_loss = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:war ?= {
				has_variable = holy_instigator
				var:holy_instigator = {
					is_alive = yes
					save_temporary_scope_as = instigator_temp
				}
				NOT = {
					any_war_participant = {
						this = scope:instigator_temp
					}
				}
			}
		}
		every_in_list = {
			list = target_titles
			save_scope_as = target
		}
		scope:war = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = holy_joiner
				}
				var:holy_joiner = {
					send_interface_message = {
						type = event_generic_bad
						title = pam_instigated_holy_war_lost.t
						left_icon = scope:attacker
						right_icon = scope:defender

						add_prestige_level = -2
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				var:holy_instigator = {
					send_interface_message = {
						type = event_generic_bad
						title = pam_instigated_holy_war_lost.t
						left_icon = scope:attacker
						right_icon = scope:defender

						custom_tooltip = pam_instigated_holy_war_lost_tt
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
pam_incite_holy_war_effect = {
	scope:puppet_or_actor = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				top_participant_group:the_christian_church ?= {
					has_participant_group_parameter = the_christian_church_incite_holy_war_interaction
				}
				NOR = {
					has_trait = forgiving
					has_trait = compassionate
				}
			}
			change_spiritual_fulfillment = {
				value = miniscule_spiritual_fulfillment_value
				multiply = {
					value = scope:target.tier
					subtract = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	scope:secondary_recipient = {
		save_scope_as = sponsored_holy_war_attacker
		add_piety = {
			value = medium_piety_value
			multiply = {
				value = scope:target.tier
				subtract = 1
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:puppet_or_actor = {
					top_participant_group:the_christian_church ?= {
						has_participant_group_parameter = the_christian_church_incite_holy_war_interaction
					}
				}
				NOR = {
					has_trait = forgiving
					has_trait = compassionate
				}
			}
			change_spiritual_fulfillment = {
				value = minor_spiritual_fulfillment_value
				multiply = {
					value = scope:target.tier
					subtract = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		save_scope_as = sponsored_holy_war_defender
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					root ?= scope:secondary_recipient
				}
			}
			add_opinion = {
				target = scope:puppet_or_actor
				modifier = incited_holy_war_opinion
				opinion = -50
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			root ?= scope:secondary_recipient
		}
		custom_tooltip = interaction_holy_war_attacker_tt
		show_as_tooltip = {
			scope:recipient = {
				add_opinion = {
					modifier = declared_war
					target = scope:secondary_recipient
				}
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		scope:recipient = {
			custom_tooltip = interaction_holy_war_attacked_tt
		}
	}
	
	scope:sponsored_holy_war_attacker = {
		add_character_flag = {
			flag = holy_war_incited
			days = 10
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:sponsored_holy_war_target.tier = tier_kingdom
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				start_war = {
					casus_belli = major_religious_war
					target = scope:sponsored_holy_war_defender
					target_title = scope:sponsored_holy_war_target
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				scope:sponsored_holy_war_target.tier = tier_duchy
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				start_war = {
					casus_belli = religious_war
					target = scope:sponsored_holy_war_defender
					target_title = scope:sponsored_holy_war_target
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			hidden_effect = {
				start_war = {
					casus_belli = minor_religious_war
					target = scope:sponsored_holy_war_defender
					target_title = scope:sponsored_holy_war_target
				}
			}
		}
		random_character_war = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					using_cb = major_religious_war
					using_cb = religious_war
					using_cb = minor_religious_war
				}
				primary_defender = scope:recipient
			}
			save_scope_as = holy_war
			set_variable = {
				name = holy_instigator
				value = scope:puppet_or_actor
			}
		}
	}
	scope:puppet_or_actor = {
		custom_tooltip = inciter_gain_prestige_if_holy_war_won_tt
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:fund_war ?= yes
			}
			pay_treasury_or_gold = {
				target = scope:secondary_recipient
				value = scope:puppet_or_actor.major_gold_value
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:join_war ?= yes
			}
			custom_tooltip = holy_war_interaction_join_war_tt
			custom_tooltip = holy_war_cleric_loss_penalty_tt
			scope:holy_war ?= {
				hidden_effect = { add_attacker = scope:puppet_or_actor }
				set_variable = {
					name = holy_joiner
					value = scope:puppet_or_actor
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:use_hook ?= yes
				has_usable_hook = scope:secondary_recipient
			}
			use_hook = scope:secondary_recipient
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:rally_crusaders ?= yes
			}
			scope:secondary_recipient = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						highest_held_title_tier < scope:puppet_or_actor.highest_held_title_tier
						current_military_strength < scope:puppet_or_actor.current_military_strength
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							is_ai = yes
						}
						add_character_modifier = {
							modifier = crusader_momentum_modifier_large
							years = 10
							desc = crusader_momentum_ai_behavior_large_tt
						}
						custom_tooltip = crusader_momentum_ai_behavior_large_tt
					}
					else = {
						add_character_modifier = {
							modifier = crusader_momentum_modifier_large
							years = 10
						}
					}
				}
				else = {
					if = {
						limit = {
							is_ai = yes
						}
						add_character_modifier = {
							modifier = crusader_momentum_modifier
							years = 10
							desc = crusader_momentum_ai_behavior_tt
						}
						custom_tooltip = crusader_momentum_ai_behavior_tt
					}
					else = {
						add_character_modifier = {
							modifier = crusader_momentum_modifier
							years = 10
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:target
				}
				add_prestige = {
					value = minor_prestige_loss
					multiply = {
						value = scope:target.tier
						subtract = 1
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_prestige = medium_prestige_loss
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:inspire_realm ?= yes
			}
			scope:secondary_recipient = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = holy_war_incited_throughout_realm_modifier
					years = 15
				}
			}
		}
		#Discount to expanding archbishopric into the area
		if = {
			limit = {
				pam_can_expand_clerical_region_after_war_trigger = { HEATHEN = scope:recipient }
			}
			custom_tooltip = can_expand_clerical_region_into_target_tt
		}
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		trigger_event = {
			id = pam_interactions_events.0072
			days = 3
		}
	}
	scope:puppet_or_actor = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:target
				scope:target.tier >= tier_kingdom
				any_character_situation = {
					situation_type = the_christian_church
				}
				situation:the_christian_church ?= {
					situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_major_holy_war_incited
				}
			}
			trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_major_holy_war_incited
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:target
				scope:target.tier >= tier_duchy
				any_character_situation = {
					situation_type = the_christian_church
				}
				situation:the_christian_church ?= {
					situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_holy_war_incited
				}
			}
			trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_holy_war_incited
		}
		else = {
			limit = {
				any_character_situation = {
					situation_type = the_christian_church
				}
				situation:the_christian_church ?= {
					situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_minor_holy_war_incited
				}
			}
			trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_minor_holy_war_incited
		}
	}
}
pam_incite_war_refusal_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:target.tier >= tier_kingdom
		}
		add_piety_level = -1 
	}
	else = {
		add_piety_experience = {
			value = major_piety_loss
			multiply = {
				value = scope:target.tier
				subtract = 1
			}
		}
	}
	
}
pam_redirect_request_excommunication_ruler_effect = {
	#Is the secondary recipient a theocrat? Only their superior can excommunicate them
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:secondary_recipient = {
				government_has_flag = government_is_theocracy
				exists = superior
				NOR = {
					superior = scope:secondary_recipient
					superior = scope:actor
				}
			}
		}
		scope:secondary_recipient.superior = {
			save_scope_as = recipient
		}
	}
	#Redirect to bishop superior of court chaplain
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			scope:secondary_recipient.cp:councillor_court_chaplain ?= {
				highest_held_title_tier < tier_duchy
				superior ?= {
					highest_held_title_tier >= tier_duchy
					is_clergy = yes
					NOT = {
						this = scope:actor
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:secondary_recipient.cp:councillor_court_chaplain.superior = {
			save_scope_as = recipient
		}
	}
	#Redirect to ecclesiastical/theocratic liege or above
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			scope:secondary_recipient = {
				any_liege_or_above = {
					highest_held_title_tier >= tier_duchy
					government_has_flag = government_is_theocracy
					faith ?= scope:secondary_recipient.faith
					NOT = {
						this = scope:actor
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:secondary_recipient = {
			ordered_liege_or_above = {
				order_by = highest_held_title_tier
				limit = {
					highest_held_title_tier >= tier_duchy
					government_has_flag = government_is_theocracy
					faith ?= scope:secondary_recipient.faith
					NOT = {
						this = scope:actor
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = recipient
			}
		}
	}
	#Redirect to court chaplain of liege or above
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			scope:secondary_recipient = {
				any_liege_or_above = {
					cp:councillor_court_chaplain ?= {
						highest_held_title_tier < tier_duchy
						is_clergy = yes
						NOT = {
							this = scope:actor
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:secondary_recipient = {
			ordered_liege_or_above = {
				order_by = {
					value = highest_held_title_tier
					multiply = -1
				}
				limit = {
					cp:councillor_court_chaplain ?= {
						highest_held_title_tier < tier_duchy
						is_clergy = yes
						NOT = {
							this = scope:actor
						}
					}
				}
				cp:councillor_court_chaplain = {
					save_scope_as = recipient
				}
			}
		}
	}
	#Redirect to bishop
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			scope:secondary_recipient.cp:councillor_court_chaplain ?= {
				highest_held_title_tier >= tier_duchy
				is_clergy = yes
				NOT = {
					this = scope:actor
				}
			}
		}
		scope:secondary_recipient.cp:councillor_court_chaplain = {
			save_scope_as = recipient
		}
	}
	#Redirect to clerical region_holder
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			scope:secondary_recipient.capital_county = {
				clerical_region_title ?= {
					holder ?= {
						highest_held_title_tier >= tier_duchy
						faith ?= scope:secondary_recipient.faith
						NOT = {
							this = scope:actor
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:secondary_recipient.capital_county.clerical_region_title.holder = {
			save_scope_as = recipient
		}
	}
	#Redirect to neighboring clerical region_holder
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			scope:secondary_recipient = {
				any_character_to_title_neighboring_county = {
					clerical_region_title ?= {
						holder ?= {
							highest_held_title_tier >= tier_duchy
							faith ?= scope:secondary_recipient.faith
							NOT = {
								this = scope:actor
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:secondary_recipient = {
			random_character_to_title_neighboring_county = {
				limit = {
					clerical_region_title ?= {
						holder ?= {
							highest_held_title_tier >= tier_duchy
							faith ?= scope:secondary_recipient.faith
							NOT = {
								this = scope:actor
							}
						}
					}
				}
				clerical_region_title.holder = {
					save_scope_as = recipient
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
pam_redirect_request_excommunication_courtier_effect = {
	#liege
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:secondary_recipient = {
				liege = {
					highest_held_title_tier >= tier_duchy
					government_has_flag = government_is_theocracy
					faith ?= scope:secondary_recipient.faith
					NOT = {
						this = scope:actor
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:secondary_recipient.liege = {
			save_scope_as = recipient
		}
	}
	#Redirect to court chaplain of liege or above
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			scope:secondary_recipient = {
				any_liege_or_above = {
					cp:councillor_court_chaplain ?= {
						highest_held_title_tier < tier_duchy
						is_clergy = yes
						NOT = {
							this = scope:actor
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:secondary_recipient = {
			ordered_liege_or_above = {
				order_by = {
					value = highest_held_title_tier
					multiply = -1
				}
				limit = {
					cp:councillor_court_chaplain ?= {
						highest_held_title_tier < tier_duchy
						is_clergy = yes
						NOT = {
							this = scope:actor
						}
					}
				}
				cp:councillor_court_chaplain = {
					save_scope_as = recipient
				}
			}
		}
	}
	#Redirect to clerical region_holder
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			scope:secondary_recipient.liege.capital_county = {
				clerical_region_title ?= {
					holder ?= {
						highest_held_title_tier >= tier_duchy
						faith ?= scope:secondary_recipient.faith
						NOT = {
							this = scope:actor
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:secondary_recipient.liege.capital_county.clerical_region_title.holder = {
			save_scope_as = recipient
		}
	}
	#Redirect to ecclesiastical/theocratic liege or above
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			scope:secondary_recipient = {
				any_liege_or_above = {
					highest_held_title_tier >= tier_duchy
					government_has_flag = government_is_theocracy
					faith ?= scope:secondary_recipient.faith
					NOT = {
						this = scope:actor
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:secondary_recipient = {
			ordered_liege_or_above = {
				order_by = {
					value = highest_held_title_tier
					multiply = -1
				}
				limit = {
					highest_held_title_tier >= tier_duchy
					government_has_flag = government_is_theocracy
					faith ?= scope:secondary_recipient.faith
					NOT = {
						this = scope:actor
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = recipient
			}
		}
	}
	#Redirect to bishop superior of court chaplain
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			scope:secondary_recipient.liege.cp:councillor_court_chaplain ?= {
				highest_held_title_tier < tier_duchy
				superior ?= {
					highest_held_title_tier >= tier_duchy
					is_clergy = yes
					NOT = {
						this = scope:actor
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:secondary_recipient.liege.cp:councillor_court_chaplain.superior = {
			save_scope_as = recipient
		}
	}
	#Redirect to neighboring clerical region_holder
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			scope:secondary_recipient.liege = {
				any_character_to_title_neighboring_county = {
					clerical_region_title ?= {
						holder ?= {
							highest_held_title_tier >= tier_duchy
							faith ?= scope:secondary_recipient.faith
							NOT = {
								this = scope:actor
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:secondary_recipient.liege = {
			random_character_to_title_neighboring_county = {
				limit = {
					clerical_region_title ?= {
						holder ?= {
							highest_held_title_tier >= tier_duchy
							faith ?= scope:secondary_recipient.faith
							NOT = {
								this = scope:actor
							}
						}
					}
				}
				clerical_region_title.holder = {
					save_scope_as = recipient
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
pam_redirect_request_excommunication_wanderer_effect = {
	#Location holder
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:secondary_recipient.location.county.holder ?= {
				highest_held_title_tier >= tier_duchy
				government_has_flag = government_is_theocracy
				faith ?= scope:secondary_recipient.faith
				NOT = {
					this = scope:actor
				}
			}
		}
		scope:secondary_recipient.location.county.holder ?= {
			save_scope_as = recipient
		}
	}
	#Location holder
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:secondary_recipient.location.county.holder.cp:councillor_court_chaplain ?= {
				highest_held_title_tier >= tier_duchy
				is_clergy = yes
				faith ?= scope:secondary_recipient.faith
				NOT = {
					this = scope:actor
				}
			}
		}
		scope:secondary_recipient.location.county.holder.cp:councillor_court_chaplain ?= {
			save_scope_as = recipient
		}
	}
	#Redirect to court chaplain of holder's liege or above
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			scope:secondary_recipient.location.county.holder ?= {
				any_liege_or_above = {
					cp:councillor_court_chaplain ?= {
						highest_held_title_tier < tier_duchy
						is_clergy = yes
						NOT = {
							this = scope:actor
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:secondary_recipient.location.county.holder ?= {
			ordered_liege_or_above = {
				order_by = {
					value = highest_held_title_tier
					multiply = -1
				}
				limit = {
					cp:councillor_court_chaplain ?= {
						highest_held_title_tier < tier_duchy
						is_clergy = yes
						NOT = {
							this = scope:actor
						}
					}
				}
				cp:councillor_court_chaplain = {
					save_scope_as = recipient
				}
			}
		}
	}
	#Redirect to ecclesiastical/theocratic liege or above
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			scope:secondary_recipient.location.county.holder ?= {
				any_liege_or_above = {
					highest_held_title_tier >= tier_duchy
					government_has_flag = government_is_theocracy
					faith ?= scope:secondary_recipient.faith
					NOT = {
						this = scope:actor
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:secondary_recipient.location.county.holder ?= {
			ordered_liege_or_above = {
				order_by = {
					value = highest_held_title_tier
					multiply = -1
				}
				limit = {
					highest_held_title_tier >= tier_duchy
					government_has_flag = government_is_theocracy
					faith ?= scope:secondary_recipient.faith
					NOT = {
						this = scope:actor
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = recipient
			}
		}
	}
	#Redirect to clerical region_holder
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			scope:secondary_recipient.location.county ?= {
				clerical_region_title ?= {
					holder ?= {
						highest_held_title_tier >= tier_duchy
						faith ?= scope:secondary_recipient.faith
						NOT = {
							this = scope:actor
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:secondary_recipient.location.county.clerical_region_title.holder = {
			save_scope_as = recipient
		}
	}
	#Redirect to neighboring clerical region_holder
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			scope:secondary_recipient.location.county ?= {
				any_neighboring_county = {
					clerical_region_title ?= {
						holder ?= {
							highest_held_title_tier >= tier_duchy
							faith ?= scope:secondary_recipient.faith
							NOT = {
								this = scope:actor
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:secondary_recipient.location.county = {
			random_neighboring_county = {
				limit = {
					clerical_region_title ?= {
						holder ?= {
							highest_held_title_tier >= tier_duchy
							faith ?= scope:secondary_recipient.faith
							NOT = {
								this = scope:actor
							}
						}
					}
				}
				clerical_region_title.holder = {
					save_scope_as = recipient
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
pam_christian_situation_hof_incite_holy_war_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			faith.religious_head ?= root
			is_ai = yes
			top_participant_group:the_christian_church ?= {
				has_participant_group_parameter = the_christian_church_incite_holy_war_interaction
			}
			piety >= 100
			piety_level >= 2
			is_available_quick = {
				adult = yes
				alive = yes
				imprisoned = no
				incapable = no
			}
			actor_incite_holy_war_shown_trigger = yes
		}
		save_scope_as = puppet_or_actor
		situation:the_christian_church = {
			situation_participant_group:christian_regular_main_power_rulers = {
				random_situation_group_participant = {
					limit = {
						faith = scope:puppet_or_actor.faith
						highest_held_title_tier >= tier_county
						is_at_war = no
						save_temporary_scope_as = secondary_temp
						#First need to find a neighbor who works...
						any_neighboring_top_liege_realm_owner = {
							save_temporary_scope_as = recipient_temp
							NOT = {
								faith = scope:secondary_temp.faith
							}
							scope:secondary_temp.faith = {
								faith_hostility_level = {
									target = scope:recipient_temp.faith
									value >= religious_cb_enabled_hostility_level
								}
							}
							NOR = {
								is_at_war_with = scope:puppet_or_actor
								is_at_war_with = scope:secondary_temp
							}
							current_military_strength < scope:secondary_temp.eighty_percent_of_current_military_strength
							scope:secondary_temp = {
								valid_holy_war_incited_attacker_trigger = { RECIPIENT = scope:recipient_temp }
							}
							incite_holy_war_ai_invalidation_trigger = { RECIPIENT = scope:recipient_temp SECONDARY = scope:secondary_temp }
							trigger_if = {
								limit = {
									scope:puppet_or_actor.piety_level >= 3
									highest_held_title_tier >= tier_duchy
									realm_size >= 8
								}
								any_realm_de_jure_kingdom = {
									incite_holy_war_title_can_be_picked_trigger = { RECIPIENT = scope:recipient_temp SECONDARY = scope:secondary_temp }
								}
							}
							trigger_else_if = {
								limit = {
									scope:puppet_or_actor.piety_level >= 2
									realm_size >= 2
								}
								any_realm_de_jure_duchy = {
									incite_holy_war_title_can_be_picked_trigger = { RECIPIENT = scope:recipient_temp SECONDARY = scope:secondary_temp }
								}
							}
							trigger_else = {
								any_realm_county = {
									incite_holy_war_title_can_be_picked_trigger = { RECIPIENT = scope:recipient_temp SECONDARY = scope:secondary_temp }
								}
							}
						}
					}
					save_scope_as = secondary_recipient
					ordered_neighboring_top_liege_realm_owner = {
						order_by = {
							value = current_military_strength 
							multiply = -1
						}
						limit = {
							save_temporary_scope_as = recipient_temp
							NOT = {
								faith = scope:secondary_recipient.faith
							}
							scope:secondary_recipient.faith = {
								faith_hostility_level = {
									target = scope:recipient_temp.faith
									value >= religious_cb_enabled_hostility_level
								}
							}
							NOR = {
								is_at_war_with = scope:puppet_or_actor
								is_at_war_with = scope:secondary_recipient
							}
							current_military_strength < scope:secondary_recipient.eighty_percent_of_current_military_strength
							scope:secondary_recipient = {
								valid_holy_war_incited_attacker_trigger = { RECIPIENT = scope:recipient_temp }
							}
							incite_holy_war_ai_invalidation_trigger = { RECIPIENT = scope:recipient_temp SECONDARY = scope:secondary_recipient }
							trigger_if = {
								limit = {
									scope:puppet_or_actor.piety_level >= 3
									highest_held_title_tier >= tier_duchy
									realm_size >= 8
								}
								any_realm_de_jure_kingdom = {
									incite_holy_war_title_can_be_picked_trigger = { RECIPIENT = scope:recipient_temp SECONDARY = scope:secondary_recipient }
								}
							}
							trigger_else_if = {
								limit = {
									scope:puppet_or_actor.piety_level >= 2
									realm_size >= 2
								}
								any_realm_de_jure_duchy = {
									incite_holy_war_title_can_be_picked_trigger = { RECIPIENT = scope:recipient_temp SECONDARY = scope:secondary_recipient }
								}
							}
							trigger_else = {
								any_realm_county = {
									incite_holy_war_title_can_be_picked_trigger = { RECIPIENT = scope:recipient_temp SECONDARY = scope:secondary_recipient }
								}
							}
						}
						save_scope_as = recipient
						#Save target
						if = {
							limit = {
								scope:puppet_or_actor.piety_level >= 3
								highest_held_title_tier >= tier_duchy
								realm_size >= 8
							}
							ordered_realm_de_jure_kingdom = {
								order_by = de_jure_counties_held_by_recipient_value
								limit = {
									incite_holy_war_title_can_be_picked_trigger = { RECIPIENT = scope:recipient SECONDARY = scope:secondary_recipient }
								}
								save_scope_as = target
							}
						}
						else_if = {
							limit = {
								scope:puppet_or_actor.piety_level >= 2
								realm_size >= 2
							}
							ordered_realm_de_jure_duchy = {
								order_by = de_jure_counties_held_by_recipient_value
								limit = {
									incite_holy_war_title_can_be_picked_trigger = { RECIPIENT = scope:recipient SECONDARY = scope:secondary_recipient }
								}
								save_scope_as = target
							}
						}
						else = {
							random_realm_county = {
								limit = {
									incite_holy_war_title_can_be_picked_trigger = { RECIPIENT = scope:recipient SECONDARY = scope:secondary_recipient }
								}
								save_scope_as = target
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		save_scope_as = actor
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:secondary_recipient
				exists = scope:recipient
			}
			ecclesiastic_incite_holy_war_interaction_effect = yes
		}
	}
}
ecclesiastic_incite_holy_war_interaction_effect = {
	scope:target = {
		save_scope_as = sponsored_holy_war_target
	}
	show_as_tooltip = {
		pam_incite_holy_war_effect = yes
		scope:recipient = { custom_tooltip = holy_war_joining_interaction_warning_tt }
	}
	#Secondary recipient gets to call this off if player
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:secondary_recipient = {
				is_ai = no
			}
		}
		scope:secondary_recipient = {
			trigger_event = pam_interactions_events.0070
		}
	}
	else = {
		scope:actor = {
			trigger_event = pam_interactions_events.0071
		}
	}
}

pam_notify_claim_war_instigator_effect_win = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:war ?= {
				has_variable = claim_instigator
				var:claim_instigator = {
					is_alive = yes
					save_temporary_scope_as = instigator_temp
				}
				OR = {
					has_variable = claim_joiner
					NOT = {
						any_war_participant = {
							this = scope:instigator_temp
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		every_in_list = {
			list = target_titles
			save_scope_as = target
		}
		scope:war = {
			var:claim_instigator = {
				send_interface_message = {
					type = event_generic_good
					title = pam_instigated_claim_war_won.t
					left_icon = scope:attacker
					right_icon = scope:defender
					if = {
						limit = {
							is_ai = no
						}
						add_prestige = pam_holy_war_inciter_prestige_value
					}
					else = {
						hidden_effect = {
							add_prestige = pam_holy_war_inciter_prestige_value
						}
					}
					custom_tooltip = pam_instigated_claim_war_won_tt
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
pam_notify_claim_war_instigator_effect_draw = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:war ?= {
				has_variable = claim_instigator
				var:claim_instigator = {
					is_alive = yes
					save_temporary_scope_as = instigator_temp
				}
				OR = {
					has_variable = claim_joiner
					NOT = {
						any_war_participant = {
							this = scope:instigator_temp
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		every_in_list = {
			list = target_titles
			save_scope_as = target
		}
		scope:war = {
			var:claim_instigator = {
				send_interface_message = {
					type = event_generic_bad
					title = pam_instigated_claim_war_draw.t
					left_icon = scope:attacker
					right_icon = scope:defender
					custom_tooltip = pam_instigated_claim_war_draw_tt
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
pam_notify_claim_war_instigator_effect_loss = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:war ?= {
				has_variable = claim_instigator
				var:claim_instigator = {
					is_alive = yes
					save_temporary_scope_as = instigator_temp
				}
				OR = {
					has_variable = claim_joiner
					NOT = {
						any_war_participant = {
							this = scope:instigator_temp
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		every_in_list = {
			list = target_titles
			save_scope_as = target
		}
		scope:war = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = claim_joiner
				}
				var:claim_joiner = {
					send_interface_message = {
						type = event_generic_bad
						title = pam_instigated_claim_war_lost.t
						left_icon = scope:attacker
						right_icon = scope:defender
						add_prestige_level = -2
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				var:claim_instigator = {
					send_interface_message = {
						type = event_generic_bad
						title = pam_instigated_claim_war_lost.t
						left_icon = scope:attacker
						right_icon = scope:defender
						custom_tooltip = pam_instigated_claim_war_lost_tt
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
pam_incite_claim_war_effect = {
	scope:secondary_recipient = {
		save_scope_as = sponsored_claim_war_attacker
		add_piety = {
			value = medium_piety_value
			multiply = {
				value = scope:target.tier
				subtract = 1
			}
		}
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		save_scope_as = sponsored_claim_war_defender
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					root ?= scope:secondary_recipient
				}
			}
			add_opinion = {
				target = scope:puppet_or_actor
				modifier = incited_claim_war_opinion
				opinion = -50
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			root ?= scope:secondary_recipient
		}
		custom_tooltip = interaction_claim_war_attacker_tt
		show_as_tooltip = {
			scope:recipient = {
				add_opinion = {
					modifier = declared_war
					target = scope:secondary_recipient
				}
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		scope:recipient = {
			custom_tooltip = interaction_claim_war_attacked_tt
		}
	}

	scope:sponsored_claim_war_attacker = {
		add_character_flag = {
			flag = temp_no_claim_war_cost
			days = 10
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			start_war = {
				casus_belli = claim_cb
				target = scope:sponsored_claim_war_defender
				claimant = scope:sponsored_claim_war_attacker
				target_title = scope:sponsored_claim_war_target
			}
			remove_character_flag = temp_no_claim_war_cost
		}
		random_character_war = {
			limit = {
				using_cb = claim_cb
				primary_defender = scope:recipient
			}
			save_scope_as = claim_war
			set_variable = {
				name = claim_instigator
				value = scope:puppet_or_actor
			}
		}
	}
	scope:puppet_or_actor = {
		custom_tooltip = inciter_gain_prestige_if_claim_war_won_tt
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:fund_war ?= yes
			}
			pay_treasury_or_gold = {
				target = scope:secondary_recipient
				value = scope:puppet_or_actor.major_gold_value
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:join_war ?= yes
			}
			custom_tooltip = claim_war_interaction_join_war_tt
			custom_tooltip = holy_war_cleric_loss_penalty_tt
			scope:claim_war ?= {
				hidden_effect = { add_attacker = scope:puppet_or_actor }
				set_variable = {
					name = claim_joiner
					value = scope:puppet_or_actor
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:use_hook ?= yes
				has_usable_hook = scope:secondary_recipient
			}
			use_hook = scope:secondary_recipient
		}
	}
}
ecclesiastic_incite_claim_war_interaction_effect = {
	scope:target = {
		save_scope_as = sponsored_claim_war_target
	}
	show_as_tooltip = {
		pam_incite_claim_war_effect = yes
	}
	hidden_effect = {
		scope:actor = {
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = ecclesiastic_incite_claim_war_interaction_toast
				desc = incite_claim_war_past_tense_tt
				left_icon = scope:secondary_recipient
				right_icon = scope:recipient
				pam_incite_claim_war_effect = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:secondary_recipient = {
					is_ai = no
					this != scope:actor
				}
			}
			scope:secondary_recipient = {
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_effect_neutral
					title = ecclesiastic_incite_claim_war_interaction_attacker_toast
					desc = incite_claim_war_past_tense_tt
					left_icon = scope:puppet_or_actor
					right_icon = scope:recipient
					if = {
						limit = { scope:join_war ?= yes }
						custom_tooltip = incite_claim_war_joined_tt
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { scope:fund_war ?= yes }
						custom_tooltip = incite_claim_war_funded_tt
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:recipient = {
					is_ai = no
					this != scope:actor
					this != scope:secondary_recipient
				}
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_effect_bad
					title = ecclesiastic_incite_claim_war_interaction_defender_toast
					desc = incite_claim_war_past_tense_tt
					left_icon = scope:secondary_recipient
					right_icon = scope:puppet_or_actor
					if = {
						limit = { scope:join_war ?= yes }
						custom_tooltip = incite_claim_war_joined_tt
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

request_white_peace_interaction_effect = {
	scope:puppet_or_actor = {
		random_character_war = {
			limit = {
				pam_request_white_peace_war_with_enemy_trigger = { ENEMY = scope:secondary_recipient }
			}
			save_scope_as = white_peace_war
			save_scope_as = war #release_prisoners_of_war_effect resolves the war through scope:war
		}
	}
	scope:white_peace_war = {
		casus_belli = {
			primary_attacker = {
				save_scope_as = attacker
			}
			primary_defender = {
				save_scope_as = defender
			}
		}
		release_prisoners_of_war_effect = yes
		end_war = white_peace
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = request_white_peace_interaction_recipient_toast
			left_icon = scope:puppet_or_actor
			right_icon = scope:secondary_recipient
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						rite = {
							OR = {
								has_tenet_status = { tenet = tenet_peace_of_god status = core }
								has_tenet_status = { tenet = tenet_pacifism status = core }
								has_tenet_status = { tenet = tenet_dharmic_pacifism status = core }
							}
						}
						any_personal_tenet = {
							OR = {
								this = tenet:tenet_peace_of_god
								this = tenet:tenet_pacifism
								this = tenet:tenet_dharmic_pacifism
							}
						}
					}
				}
				change_spiritual_fulfillment = minor_spiritual_fulfillment_value
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = pam_white_peace_enforcer_modifier }
				}
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = pam_white_peace_enforcer_modifier
					years = 10
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_any_nickname = no }
				hidden_effect = {
					random = {
						chance = {
							value = 1
							if = {
								limit = { is_ai = no }
								add = 9
							}
						}
						give_nickname = nick_the_peacemaker
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	scope:secondary_recipient = {
		if = {
			limit = { is_ai = yes }
			add_opinion = {
				target = scope:recipient
				modifier = pam_enforced_white_peace_opinion
			}
		}
		trigger_event = pam_interactions_events.0080
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			situation:the_christian_church ?= {
				situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_the_christian_church_white_peace_enforced
			}
		}
		situation:the_christian_church = {
			trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_the_christian_church_white_peace_enforced
		}
	}
	scope:puppet_or_actor = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:donate_faith = yes
			}
			pay_treasury_or_gold = {
				target = scope:recipient
				value = major_gold_value
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:offer_piety = yes
			}
			add_piety = major_piety_loss
			scope:recipient = {
				add_piety = major_piety_gain
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:offer_hook = yes
			}
			scope:recipient = {
				add_hook = {
					type = favor_hook
					target = scope:puppet_or_actor
				}
			}
		}
		pam_offer_pilgrimage_effect = {
			ACTOR = scope:puppet_or_actor
			CLERIC = scope:recipient
		}
		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_neutral
			title = request_white_peace_interaction_toast_title
			custom_tooltip = request_white_peace_interaction_toast
			left_icon = scope:recipient
			right_icon = scope:secondary_recipient
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes
			scope:actor != scope:recipient
		}
		scope:actor = {
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_neutral
				title = request_white_peace_interaction_toast_title
				custom_tooltip = request_white_peace_interaction_toast_puppeteer
				left_icon = scope:puppet_or_actor
				right_icon = scope:recipient
			}
		}
	}
}

request_victory_interaction_effect = {
	scope:puppet_or_actor = {
		random_character_war = {
			limit = {
				pam_request_victory_war_with_enemy_trigger = { ENEMY = scope:secondary_recipient }
			}
			save_scope_as = requested_victory_war
			save_scope_as = war #release_prisoners_of_war_effect resolves the war through scope:war
		}
	}
	if = {
		#The war can end during the reply window, leaving nothing to rule on
		limit = { exists = scope:requested_victory_war }
		scope:requested_victory_war = {
			casus_belli = {
				primary_attacker = {
					save_scope_as = attacker
				}
				primary_defender = {
					save_scope_as = defender
				}
			}
			release_prisoners_of_war_effect = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					casus_belli = {
						primary_attacker = scope:puppet_or_actor
					}
				}
				end_war = attacker
			}
			else = {
				end_war = defender
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_neutral
				title = request_victory_interaction_recipient_toast
				left_icon = scope:puppet_or_actor
				right_icon = scope:secondary_recipient
			}
		}
		#The Church makes it worth the loser's while, so that obeying a ruling is not simply a defeat
		scope:secondary_recipient = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = pam_church_compensation_modifier }
				}
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = pam_church_compensation_modifier
					years = 10
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_ai = yes }
				add_opinion = {
					target = scope:recipient
					modifier = pam_enforced_victory_opinion
				}
			}
			trigger_event = pam_interactions_events.0083
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				situation:the_christian_church ?= {
					situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_the_christian_church_victory_enforced
				}
			}
			situation:the_christian_church = {
				trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_the_christian_church_victory_enforced
			}
		}
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:donate_faith = yes
				}
				pay_treasury_or_gold = {
					target = scope:recipient
					value = major_gold_value
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:offer_piety = yes
				}
				add_piety = major_piety_loss
				scope:recipient = {
					add_piety = major_piety_gain
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:offer_hook = yes
				}
				scope:recipient = {
					add_hook = {
						type = favor_hook
						target = scope:puppet_or_actor
					}
				}
			}
			pam_offer_pilgrimage_effect = {
				ACTOR = scope:puppet_or_actor
				CLERIC = scope:recipient
			}
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_neutral
				title = request_victory_interaction_toast_title
				custom_tooltip = request_victory_interaction_toast
				left_icon = scope:recipient
				right_icon = scope:secondary_recipient
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes
				scope:actor != scope:recipient
			}
			scope:actor = {
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_effect_neutral
					title = request_victory_interaction_toast_title
					custom_tooltip = request_victory_interaction_toast_puppeteer
					left_icon = scope:puppet_or_actor
					right_icon = scope:recipient
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

pam_enforce_title_succession_effect = {
	scope:target = { save_scope_as = succession_title }
	scope:target.holder = { save_scope_as = succession_holder }
	scope:succession_title = {
		current_heir = { save_scope_as = enforced_heir }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:enforced_heir
			NOT = { scope:enforced_heir = scope:succession_holder }
		}
		create_title_and_vassal_change = {
			type = stepped_down
			save_scope_as = succession_title_change
			add_claim_on_loss = no
		}
		scope:succession_title = {
			change_title_holder_include_vassals = {
				holder = scope:enforced_heir
				change = scope:succession_title_change
			}
		}
		resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:succession_title_change
	}
	if = {
		limit = { scope:succession_holder = { is_ai = yes } }
		scope:succession_holder = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = scope:actor
				modifier = meddling_church_opinion
			}
		}
	}
}

pam_increase_papabile_score_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { scope:pam_papabile_gold_option = yes }
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = pam_increase_papabile_score_effect_20_tt
			if = {
				limit = { exists = var:pam_papabile_score }
				change_variable = {
					name = pam_papabile_score
					add = $VALUE$
				}
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = {
					name = pam_papabile_score
					value = $VALUE$
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { this != scope:actor }
			add_opinion = {
				target = scope:actor
				modifier = grateful_opinion
				opinion = 30
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = pam_increase_papabile_score_effect_10_tt
			if = {
				limit = { exists = var:pam_papabile_score }
				change_variable = {
					name = pam_papabile_score
					add = $VALUE$
				}
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = {
					name = pam_papabile_score
					value = $VALUE$
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { this != scope:actor }
			add_opinion = {
				target = scope:actor
				modifier = grateful_opinion
				opinion = 15
			}
		}
	}
	# Both branches above have already created the variable
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:actor ?= {
				has_personal_tenet_flag = tenet_apostolic_succession_papabile_influence
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = pam_increase_papabile_score_apostolic_succession_tt
			change_variable = {
				name = pam_papabile_score
				add = pam_apostolic_succession_papabile_bonus_value
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:actor }
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = pam_papabile_leaning_tt
			pam_spread_papabile_leaning_effect = { VALUE = $VALUE$ }
		}
	}
}

pam_spread_papabile_leaning_effect = {
	save_scope_as = pam_papabile_candidate
	faith = {
		every_clerical_elector_title = {
			limit = {
				holder ?= {
					this != scope:pam_papabile_candidate
					OR = {
						this = scope:actor
						is_vassal_or_below_of = scope:actor
						top_liege ?= scope:actor.top_liege
						is_close_or_extended_family_of = scope:actor
						scope:actor.puppet:theological_agent_puppet ?= this
						scope:actor.puppet:external_theological_agent_puppet ?= this
						opinion = {
							target = scope:actor
							value >= medium_positive_opinion
						}
					}
				}
			}
			holder ?= {
				pam_apply_papabile_leaning_effect = { VALUE = $VALUE$ }
			}
		}
	}
}

pam_apply_papabile_leaning_effect = {
	set_variable = {
		name = pam_papabile_leaning_target
		value = scope:pam_papabile_candidate
		years = 20
	}
	set_variable = {
		name = pam_papabile_leaning_value
		value = $VALUE$
		years = 20
	}
}

# We declare the cardinal for that seat, otherwise a holy candidate is listed as The Pious
pam_place_first_as_powerful_effect = {
	custom_tooltip = {
		text = pam_place_first_as_powerful_tt
		if = {
			limit = { exists = var:pam_papabile_score }
			change_variable = {
				name = pam_papabile_score
				add = $VALUE$
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = {
				name = pam_papabile_score
				value = $VALUE$
			}
		}
	}
	custom_tooltip = {
		text = pam_campaign_as_powerful_tt
		remove_variable = pam_campaign_as_populist
		set_variable = {
			name = pam_campaign_as_powerful
			years = 20
		}
	}
	# Deliberately the flat nudge rather than the purchased score: buying the seat should not also buy the vote.
	if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:actor }
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = pam_papabile_leaning_tt
			pam_spread_papabile_leaning_effect = { VALUE = 10 }
		}
	}
}

pam_place_first_as_populist_effect = {
	# Own variable, and the elector vote modifier also reads pam_propagate_word_of_my_piety the same way
	custom_tooltip = {
		text = pam_place_first_as_populist_tt
		if = {
			limit = { exists = var:pam_papabile_populist_score }
			change_variable = {
				name = pam_papabile_populist_score
				add = $VALUE$
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = {
				name = pam_papabile_populist_score
				value = $VALUE$
			}
		}
	}
	custom_tooltip = {
		text = pam_campaign_as_populist_tt
		remove_variable = pam_campaign_as_powerful
		set_variable = {
			name = pam_campaign_as_populist
			years = 20
		}
	}
	# Deliberately the flat nudge rather than the purchased score, matching the Powerful path.
	if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:actor }
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = pam_papabile_leaning_tt
			pam_spread_papabile_leaning_effect = { VALUE = 10 }
		}
	}
}

# $TT$ localizes the papabile loss for the losing side (defender on victory, attacker on defeat)
pam_humiliation_war_church_standing_loss_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				exists = clerical_elector_title
				exists = var:pam_papabile_score
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = $TT$
			if = {
				limit = { exists = var:pam_papabile_score }
				change_variable = {
					name = pam_papabile_score
					subtract = 25
				}
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = {
					name = pam_papabile_score
					value = -25
				}
			}
		}
	}
	# Only clerical rulers carry standing the disgrace can bite into; it feeds clerical appointment scoring
	if = {
		limit = { government_has_flag = government_uses_church_authority }
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = pam_clerical_disgrace_modifier
			years = 20
		}
	}
}

pam_on_conversion_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { is_ai = no }
		add_character_flag = {
			flag = faith_conversion_recently_converted
			years = pam_ui_conversion_cooldown
		}
	}
}

remove_personal_tenet_if_has = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_personal_tenet = $TENET$ }
		remove_personal_tenet = $TENET$
	}
}

add_known_tenet_if_not = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { knows_tenet = $TENET$ } }
		add_known_tenet = $TENET$
	}
}

update_syncretism_personal_tenet_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_available_quick = { advanced_ruler = yes }
			scope:old_faith.religion != faith.religion
		}
		scope:old_faith = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_doctrine = sinitic_hostility_doctrine }
				prev = { add_known_tenet_if_not = { TENET = tenet_sinitic_syncretism } }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_doctrine = eastern_hostility_doctrine }
				prev = { add_known_tenet_if_not = { TENET = tenet_eastern_syncretism } }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_doctrine = pagan_hostility_doctrine }
				prev = { add_known_tenet_if_not = { TENET = tenet_unreformed_syncretism } }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { religion_tag = christianity_religion }
				prev = { add_known_tenet_if_not = { TENET = tenet_christian_syncretism } }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { religion_tag = islam_religion }
				prev = { add_known_tenet_if_not = { TENET = tenet_islamic_syncretism } }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { religion_tag = judaism_religion }
				prev = { add_known_tenet_if_not = { TENET = tenet_jewish_syncretism } }
			}
		}
		faith = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_doctrine = sinitic_hostility_doctrine }
				prev = { remove_personal_tenet_if_has = { TENET = tenet_sinitic_syncretism } }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_doctrine = eastern_hostility_doctrine }
				prev = { remove_personal_tenet_if_has = { TENET = tenet_eastern_syncretism } }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_doctrine = pagan_hostility_doctrine }
				prev = { remove_personal_tenet_if_has = { TENET = tenet_unreformed_syncretism } }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { religion_tag = christianity_religion }
				prev = { remove_personal_tenet_if_has = { TENET = tenet_christian_syncretism } }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { religion_tag = islam_religion }
				prev = { remove_personal_tenet_if_has = { TENET = tenet_islamic_syncretism } }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { religion_tag = judaism_religion }
				prev = { remove_personal_tenet_if_has = { TENET = tenet_jewish_syncretism } }
			}
		}
	}
}

# Keep branches in sync with pam_sway_to_rite_variant_can_send_trigger
pam_open_sway_to_rite_variant_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { scope:recipient = { is_courtier_of = scope:actor } }
		open_interaction_window = {
			interaction = sway_courtier_to_my_rite_interaction
			actor = scope:actor
			recipient = scope:recipient
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			scope:recipient = {
				OR = {
					target_is_liege_or_above = scope:actor
					is_tributary_of = scope:actor
					pam_rite_sway_house_lane_trigger = {
						ACTOR = scope:actor
						RECIPIENT = scope:recipient
					}
				}
			}
		}
		open_interaction_window = {
			interaction = sway_vassal_to_my_rite_interaction
			actor = scope:actor
			recipient = scope:recipient
		}
	}
	else = {
		open_interaction_window = {
			interaction = sway_ruler_to_my_rite_interaction
			actor = scope:actor
			recipient = scope:recipient
		}
	}
}

# Shared on_accept for the three Sway to Rite interactions
# Option flags exist only on outreach, so the ?= checks do nothing elsewhere
pam_sway_to_rite_on_accept_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes }
		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
			if = {
				limit = { pam_sway_ruler_to_my_rite_cost_value > 0 }
				add_piety = {
					value = {
						add = pam_sway_ruler_to_my_rite_cost_value
						max = piety
						multiply = -1
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	scope:puppet_or_actor = {
		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = sway_ruler_to_my_rite_interaction_toast
			left_icon = scope:puppet_or_actor
			right_icon = scope:recipient
			scope:recipient = {
				set_character_rite = scope:puppet_or_actor.rite
				if = {
					limit = { is_ai = yes }
					add_opinion = {
						target = scope:puppet_or_actor
						modifier = pious_opinion
						opinion = 10
					}
				}
				if = { # Accepting the founding wave of a freshly created rite pulls the whole domain and court along, but only if they are actually tied to us
					limit = {
						is_ruler = yes
						is_landed = yes
						scope:puppet_or_actor.rite ?= {
							has_variable = pam_rite_founding_window
						}
						pam_rite_sway_connected_recipient_trigger = { ACTOR = scope:puppet_or_actor }
					}
					pam_convert_domain_counties_to_rite_effect = { RITE = scope:puppet_or_actor.rite }
					pam_convert_court_to_rite_effect = { RITE = scope:puppet_or_actor.rite }
				}
				else_if = { # We also convert their capital county
					limit = {
						is_ruler = yes
						is_landed = yes
						capital_county = {
							this.faith = scope:puppet_or_actor.faith # set_county_rite would drag a foreign-faith capital across faiths
							this.rite != scope:puppet_or_actor.rite
							any_neighboring_county = { # only if a neighbor shares the same liege or IS the liege, aka I'm not surrounded by complete strangers
								OR = {
									this.holder.liege = prev.holder.liege
									this.holder = prev.holder.liege
								}
							}
						}
					}
					capital_county = { set_county_rite = scope:puppet_or_actor.rite }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { scope:pam_sway_pledge_favor ?= yes }
					add_hook = {
						target = scope:puppet_or_actor
						type = favor_hook
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { scope:pam_sway_endowment ?= yes }
				scope:puppet_or_actor = {
					pay_treasury_or_gold = {
						target = scope:recipient
						value = pam_sway_to_rite_endowment_cost_value
					}
				}
			}
		}
		# Catalysts
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:puppet_or_actor.like_christianity_religion_trigger = yes
				situation:the_christian_church ?= { situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_the_christian_church_swayed_to_rite }
			}
			situation:the_christian_church = { trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_the_christian_church_swayed_to_rite }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes
			scope:actor != scope:recipient
		}
		scope:actor = {
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = sway_ruler_to_my_rite_interaction_toast_puppeteer
				left_icon = scope:puppet_or_actor
				right_icon = scope:recipient
			}
		}
	}
	scope:puppet_or_actor = {
		pam_tax_nonbelievers_conversion_fulfillment_effect = yes
	}
}

# Shared on_decline for the three Sway to Rite interactions.
pam_sway_to_rite_on_decline_effect = {
	scope:puppet_or_actor = {
		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_bad
			title = sway_ruler_to_my_rite_interaction_decline_toast
			right_icon = scope:recipient
			scope:recipient = {
				if = {
					limit = { is_ai = yes }
					add_opinion = {
						target = scope:puppet_or_actor
						modifier = annoyed_opinion
						opinion = -10
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:is_puppet_action ?= yes
			scope:actor != scope:recipient
		}
		scope:actor = {
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_bad
				title = sway_ruler_to_my_rite_interaction_decline_toast_puppeteer
				left_icon = scope:puppet_or_actor
				right_icon = scope:recipient
			}
		}
	}
}

# PAM - Converts every county in a ruler's domain that shares their faith to $RITE$
# The capital is skipped while a different-rite clerical region holder is seated in it, unless that holder is our puppet
pam_convert_domain_counties_to_rite_effect = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = domain_rite_converter
	every_held_title = {
		limit = {
			tier = tier_county
			faith = scope:domain_rite_converter.faith
			rite != $RITE$
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { scope:domain_rite_converter.capital_county ?= this }
				NOT = {
					clerical_region_title ?= {
						holder ?= {
							capital_county = scope:domain_rite_converter.capital_county
							faith = scope:domain_rite_converter.faith
							rite != $RITE$
							NOR = {
								scope:domain_rite_converter.puppet:theological_agent_puppet ?= this
								scope:domain_rite_converter.puppet:external_theological_agent_puppet ?= this
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		set_county_rite = $RITE$
	}
}

# PAM - Converts a ruler's court (present, away, and guests) that shares their faith to $RITE$, mirroring rite growth's court conversion
pam_convert_court_to_rite_effect = {
	every_courtier_or_guest = {
		limit = {
			is_physically_able_ai = yes
			faith = $RITE$.faith
			rite != $RITE$
			NOT = { has_character_flag = ai_will_not_convert }
		}
		set_character_rite = $RITE$
	}
	every_courtier_away = {
		limit = {
			is_physically_able_ai = yes
			faith = $RITE$.faith
			rite != $RITE$
			NOT = { has_character_flag = ai_will_not_convert }
		}
		set_character_rite = $RITE$
	}
}

# PAM - Faith counterpart of pam_convert_court_to_rite_effect: converts a ruler's court still on $OLD_FAITH$ to $FAITH$
pam_convert_court_to_faith_effect = {
	every_courtier_or_guest = {
		limit = {
			is_physically_able_ai = yes
			faith = $OLD_FAITH$
			NOT = { has_character_flag = ai_will_not_convert }
		}
		set_character_faith = $FAITH$
	}
	every_courtier_away = {
		limit = {
			is_physically_able_ai = yes
			faith = $OLD_FAITH$
			NOT = { has_character_flag = ai_will_not_convert }
		}
		set_character_faith = $FAITH$
	}
}

# PAM Great Schism: shows $TT$, and $VALUE$ goes up when positive (zero still shows the tooltip)
# Progress only goes up, and we do nothing once the schism has happened or been prevented
pam_change_great_schism_progress_effect = {
	custom_tooltip = $TT$
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = { pam_great_schism_progress_can_change_trigger = yes }
			if = {
				limit = { $VALUE$ > 0 }
				situation:the_christian_church = {
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_variable = pam_progress_towards_great_schism } }
						set_variable = { name = pam_progress_towards_great_schism value = 0 }
					}
					change_variable = {
						name = pam_progress_towards_great_schism
						add = $VALUE$
					}
				}
				every_player = {
					limit = {
						faith.like_christianity_religion_trigger = yes
						exists = top_participant_group:the_christian_church
					}
					send_interface_message = {
						type = msg_pam_great_schism_progress
						desc = $TT$
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					situation:the_christian_church = {
						NOT = { has_variable = pam_great_schism_status }
						pam_great_schism_progress_value >= pam_great_schism_progress_threshold_value
					}
				}
				situation:the_christian_church = {
					pam_update_schism_rite_variables_effect = yes
					var:schism_mainline_rite ?= { save_scope_as = schism_mainline_rite }
					var:schism_competitor_rite ?= { save_scope_as = schism_competitor_rite }
					set_variable = {
						name = pam_great_schism_status
						value = flag:threshold_reached
					}
				}
				scope:schism_mainline_rite.head_of_rite ?= { save_scope_as = schism_mainline_head }
				scope:schism_competitor_rite.head_of_rite ?= { save_scope_as = schism_competitor_head }
				every_player = {
					limit = {
						faith.like_christianity_religion_trigger = yes
						any_character_situation = { this = situation:the_christian_church }
					}
					trigger_event = pam_the_christian_church.0064
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Situation scope, and a third rite as mainline keeps the existing Rome and Constantinople order
pam_update_schism_rite_variables_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			#stop updating it if the schism is going to happen
			situation:the_christian_church ?= {
				NOT = { has_variable = pam_great_schism_status }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { faith:christian_faith.main_rite ?= rite:roman_rite }
			set_variable = { name = schism_mainline_rite value = rite:roman_rite }
			set_variable = { name = schism_competitor_rite value = rite:byzantine_rite }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { faith:christian_faith.main_rite ?= rite:byzantine_rite }
			set_variable = { name = schism_mainline_rite value = rite:byzantine_rite }
			set_variable = { name = schism_competitor_rite value = rite:roman_rite }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_variable = schism_mainline_rite } }
			set_variable = { name = schism_mainline_rite value = rite:roman_rite }
			set_variable = { name = schism_competitor_rite value = rite:byzantine_rite }
		}
	}
}

# Converters are root, with scope:old_rite the rite they left, when on_rite_change fires
# Flag spaces repeats and stops the event's own conversion from chaining
pam_schism_rite_conversion_dissent_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_landed = yes
			exists = scope:old_rite
			scope:old_rite != rite
			OR = {
				highest_held_title_tier >= tier_kingdom
				pam_is_investiture_clergy_trigger = yes
			}
			pam_schism_rite_in_pair_trigger = { RITE = scope:old_rite }
			pam_schism_rite_in_pair_trigger = { RITE = root.rite }
			pam_great_schism_progress_can_change_trigger = yes
