			NOT = { has_character_flag = pam_schism_rite_conversion_dissent_recently }
		}
		random = {
			chance = pam_schism_rite_conversion_dissent_chance_value
			scope:old_rite = { save_scope_as = pam_dissent_rite }
			add_character_flag = {
				flag = pam_schism_rite_conversion_dissent_recently
				years = 10
			}
			trigger_event = {
				id = pam_the_christian_church.0063
				days = { 3 10 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# $RITE$ is the rite they stayed with, evaluated on the character
# Shares pam_schism_rite_dissenter_valid_trigger with pam_the_christian_church.0063
pam_save_schism_rite_dissenter_effect = {
	every_courtier = {
		limit = { pam_schism_rite_dissenter_valid_trigger = { RITE = $RITE$ } }
		add_to_list = pam_schism_dissenter_candidates
	}
	every_vassal_or_below = {
		limit = { pam_schism_rite_dissenter_valid_trigger = { RITE = $RITE$ } }
		add_to_list = pam_schism_dissenter_candidates
	}
	every_pool_character = {
		province = root.location
		limit = { pam_schism_rite_dissenter_valid_trigger = { RITE = $RITE$ } }
		add_to_list = pam_schism_dissenter_candidates
	}
	random_in_list = {
		list = pam_schism_dissenter_candidates
		weight = {
			base = 1
			modifier = {
				add = 10
				pam_is_investiture_clergy_trigger = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 5
				highest_held_title_tier >= tier_county
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 5
				is_close_family_of = root
			}
		}
		save_scope_as = schism_dissenter
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { exists = scope:schism_dissenter }
		}
		create_character = {
			template = priest_character_template
			employer = root
			faith = root.faith
			rite = $RITE$
			culture = root.culture
			save_scope_as = schism_dissenter
			after_creation = {
				add_character_flag = created
			}
		}
	}
}

# A county is root when on_county_rite_change runs this, with scope:old_rite the rite it used to have
# A faith swap books nothing, because the schism is a quarrel within Christianity
pam_schism_county_conversion_progress_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:old_rite
			rite != scope:old_rite
			pam_schism_rite_in_pair_trigger = { RITE = scope:old_rite }
			any_county_holy_site = {
				any_holy_site_faith = { this = faith:christian_faith }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { pam_schism_county_rite_in_pair_trigger = { COUNTY = root } }
			pam_change_great_schism_progress_effect = {
				VALUE = pam_great_schism_progress_county_conversion_value
				TT = pam_great_schism_progress_county_conversion_tt
			}
		}
		else = { # Attribute to the pair rite losing the county (gainer is outside the pair)
			pam_change_great_schism_progress_effect = {
				VALUE = pam_great_schism_progress_county_conversion_edge_value
				TT = pam_great_schism_progress_county_conversion_edge_tt
			}
		}
	}
}

# Fire the Great Schism catalyst again on phase change to make sure Saeculum Obscurum happens
pam_refire_great_schism_catalyst_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			pam_great_schism_progress_can_change_trigger = yes
			var:pam_great_schism_status ?= flag:threshold_reached
			pam_great_schism_progress_value >= pam_great_schism_progress_threshold_value
			situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_the_christian_church_great_schism
		}
		trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_the_christian_church_great_schism
	}
}

# PAM - Great Schism: fire the schism start event for Chalcedonian rulers once conditions hold
pam_fire_great_schism_start_event_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { var:pam_great_schism_status ?= flag:threshold_reached }
		var:schism_mainline_rite = { save_scope_as = schism_mainline_rite }
		var:schism_competitor_rite = { save_scope_as = schism_competitor_rite }
		scope:schism_mainline_rite.head_of_rite ?= { save_scope_as = schism_mainline_head }
		scope:schism_competitor_rite.head_of_rite ?= { save_scope_as = schism_competitor_head }
		set_variable = {
			name = pam_great_schism_status
			value = flag:ongoing
		}
		every_situation_participant = {
			limit = {
				is_ruler = yes
				faith = faith:christian_faith
			}
			trigger_event = pam_the_christian_church.0060
		}
	}
}

pam_offer_great_schism_stance_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			pam_can_pick_great_schism_stance_trigger = yes
			NOR = {
				has_variable = pam_schism_stance
				has_character_flag = pam_offered_great_schism_stance
			}
		}
		pam_send_great_schism_stance_offer_effect = yes
	}
}

pam_offer_great_schism_stance_to_new_player_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { pam_can_pick_great_schism_stance_trigger = yes }
		pam_send_great_schism_stance_offer_effect = yes
	}
}

pam_send_great_schism_stance_offer_effect = {
	add_character_flag = pam_offered_great_schism_stance
	situation:the_christian_church = {
		var:schism_mainline_rite = { save_scope_as = schism_mainline_rite }
		var:schism_competitor_rite = { save_scope_as = schism_competitor_rite }
	}
	trigger_event = pam_the_christian_church.0060
}

# PAM - Sacred Childbirth: scaled piety (regular tenet) for a live birth. Also triggers spiritual fulfillment and stress loss rewards (personal tenet), shown in birth events
pam_childbirth_piety_reward_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { rite = { rite_has_parameter = piety_from_childbirth_active } }
		if = {
			limit = { scope:child = { has_any_negative_bastard_trait_trigger = no } }
			add_piety = {
				value = major_piety_gain
				if = { limit = { any_child = { is_twin_of = scope:child } } add = major_piety_gain }
				if = { limit = { any_child = { count >= 3 is_twin_of = scope:child } } add = major_piety_gain }
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_piety = {
				value = minor_piety_gain
				if = { limit = { any_child = { is_twin_of = scope:child } } add = minor_piety_gain }
				if = { limit = { any_child = { count >= 3 is_twin_of = scope:child } } add = minor_piety_gain }
			}
		}
	}
	pam_childbirth_spiritual_fulfillment_reward_effect = yes
}

# PAM - Carnal Exaltation: piety for a successful seduce scheme
pam_seduction_piety_reward_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { rite = { rite_has_parameter = piety_from_seduction_active } }
		add_piety = medium_piety_gain
	}
}

# PAM - Sacred Childbirth: scaled spiritual fulfillment + stress loss (personal tenet) for a live birth; called on its own for children conceived in secret. shown in birth events
pam_childbirth_spiritual_fulfillment_reward_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_personal_tenet_flag = spiritual_fulfillment_from_childbirth_personal_active
			has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { scope:child = { has_any_negative_bastard_trait_trigger = no } }
			change_spiritual_fulfillment = {
				value = medium_spiritual_fulfillment_value
				if = { limit = { any_child = { is_twin_of = scope:child } } add = medium_spiritual_fulfillment_value }
				if = { limit = { any_child = { count >= 3 is_twin_of = scope:child } } add = medium_spiritual_fulfillment_value }
			}
		}
		else = {
			change_spiritual_fulfillment = {
				value = minor_spiritual_fulfillment_value
				if = { limit = { any_child = { is_twin_of = scope:child } } add = minor_spiritual_fulfillment_value }
				if = { limit = { any_child = { count >= 3 is_twin_of = scope:child } } add = minor_spiritual_fulfillment_value }
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_personal_tenet_flag = stress_loss_from_childbirth_personal_active
			has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { scope:child = { has_any_negative_bastard_trait_trigger = no } }
			add_stress = {
				value = medium_stress_loss
				if = { limit = { any_child = { is_twin_of = scope:child } } add = medium_stress_loss }
				if = { limit = { any_child = { count >= 3 is_twin_of = scope:child } } add = medium_stress_loss }
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_stress = {
				value = minor_stress_loss
				if = { limit = { any_child = { is_twin_of = scope:child } } add = minor_stress_loss }
				if = { limit = { any_child = { count >= 3 is_twin_of = scope:child } } add = minor_stress_loss }
			}
		}
	}
}

# PAM - Sacred Childbirth: spiritual fulfillment loss for a child-death birth, shown in birth events
pam_childbirth_spiritual_fulfillment_penalty_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_personal_tenet_flag = spiritual_fulfillment_from_childbirth_personal_active }
		if = {
			limit = { has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes }
			change_spiritual_fulfillment = major_spiritual_fulfillment_loss
		}
	}
}

# PAM - Epidemics: spiritual fulfillment loss from pestilence as divine wrath. Exaltation of Pain (faith or personal tenet) exempts the character. Needs argument AMOUNT (spiritual fulfillment script value)
pam_epidemic_spiritual_fulfillment_loss_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_playable_character = yes
			NOR = {
				rite = { rite_has_parameter = no_epidemic_fulfillment_loss_active }
				has_personal_tenet_flag = no_epidemic_fulfillment_loss_personal_active
			}
		}
		change_spiritual_fulfillment = $AMOUNT$
	}
}

# PAM Epidemics: expects scope:epidemic, shown in epidemic arrival events
pam_epidemic_arrival_spiritual_fulfillment_loss_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { scope:epidemic = { outbreak_intensity = apocalyptic } }
		pam_epidemic_spiritual_fulfillment_loss_effect = { AMOUNT = massive_spiritual_fulfillment_loss }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { scope:epidemic = { outbreak_intensity = major } }
		pam_epidemic_spiritual_fulfillment_loss_effect = { AMOUNT = major_spiritual_fulfillment_loss }
	}
	else = {
		pam_epidemic_spiritual_fulfillment_loss_effect = { AMOUNT = medium_spiritual_fulfillment_loss }
	}
}

# PAM - Epidemics: spiritual fulfillment loss when your court physician treats you for an epidemic disease (chronic diseases are exempt). Expects scope:disease_type, shown in disease treatment events
pam_epidemic_disease_treatment_spiritual_fulfillment_loss_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				scope:disease_type = flag:bubonic_plague
				scope:disease_type = flag:smallpox
				scope:disease_type = flag:typhus
				scope:disease_type = flag:measles
				scope:disease_type = flag:dysentery
				scope:disease_type = flag:consumption
				scope:disease_type = flag:ergotism
			}
		}
		pam_epidemic_spiritual_fulfillment_loss_effect = { AMOUNT = medium_spiritual_fulfillment_loss }
	}
}

consecrate_kingdom_effect = {
	#If you don't have sway over the pope... they get sway over you
    if = {
    	limit = {
    		NOT = {
    			this = scope:religious_authority
    		}
    	}
    	if = {
    		limit = {
    			NOR = {
    				has_hook = scope:religious_authority
    				scope:king_aspirant.puppet:theological_agent_puppet ?= scope:religious_authority
    				scope:king_aspirant.puppet:external_theological_agent_puppet ?= scope:religious_authority
    			}
    		}
    		scope:religious_authority = {
    			add_hook = {
    				type = strong_obligation_hook
    				target = scope:king_aspirant
    			}
    		}
    	}
    	else = {
    		scope:religious_authority = {
    			add_hook = {
    				type = obligation_hook
    				target = scope:king_aspirant
    			}
    		}
    	}
    }
    if = {
    	limit = {
    		scope:religious_authority = {
    			this = scope:king_aspirant
    		}
    	}
    	every_held_title = {
    		limit = {
    			tier = tier_county
    		}
    		empire = {
    			every_de_jure_county = {
    				limit = {
    					holder ?= {
    						faith = scope:king_aspirant.faith
    						NOT = {
    							government_has_flag = government_is_theocracy
    						}
    					}	
    				}
    				holder = {
    					add_to_list = angry_seculars
    					every_liege_or_above = {
    						limit = {
    							faith = scope:king_aspirant.faith
    							NOT = {
    								government_has_flag = government_is_theocracy
    							}
    						}
    						add_to_list = angry_seculars
    					}
    				}
    			}
    		}
    	}
    	every_in_list = {
    		custom = custom.every_empire_secular_ruler
    		list = angry_seculars

    		add_opinion = {
    			modifier = power_hungry_priest_opinion
    			target = scope:king_aspirant
    			opinion = -30
    		}
    	}
    }
    create_custom_kingdom_effect = yes
    if = {
    	limit = {
    		NOT = {
    			this = scope:religious_authority
    		}
    		top_participant_group:the_christian_church ?= {
				OR = {
					participant_group_type = christian_clerical_main_power_rulers
					participant_group_type = christian_regular_main_power_rulers
				}
			}
			situation:the_christian_church ?= {
				situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_consecrated_kingdom_for_ruler
			}
			NOT = {
				scope:king_aspirant = { government_has_flag = government_is_theocracy }
			}
    	}
    	situation:the_christian_church = {
    		trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_consecrated_kingdom_for_ruler
    	}
    }
    else_if = {
    	limit = {
    		this = scope:religious_authority
			situation:the_christian_church ?= {
				situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_prelate_empowered_self_with_kingdom
			}
    	}
    	situation:the_christian_church = {
    		trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_prelate_empowered_self_with_kingdom
    	}
    }
    else_if = {
    	limit = {
    		top_participant_group:the_christian_church ?= {
				OR = {
					participant_group_type = christian_clerical_main_power_rulers
					participant_group_type = christian_regular_main_power_rulers
				}
			}
			situation:the_christian_church ?= {
				situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_consecrated_kingdom_for_theocrat
			}
			government_has_flag = government_is_theocracy
    	}
    	situation:the_christian_church = {
    		trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_consecrated_kingdom_for_theocrat
    	}
    }
    if = {
        limit = {
            is_ai = no
            NOT = { exists = global_var:found_kingdom_decision  }
        }
        set_global_variable = {
            name = found_kingdom_decision
            value = root
        }
        set_global_variable = {
            name = found_kingdom_decision_kingdom
            value = root.primary_title
        }
    }
}
consecrate_kingdom_player_cleric_effect = {
    if = {
		limit = {
			is_ai = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					exists = scope:interaction
				}
			}
    		add_opinion = {
				target = scope:religious_authority
				modifier = support_opinion
				opinion = 50
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = scope:religious_authority
				modifier = support_opinion
				opinion = 25
			}
		}
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = consecrated_king_modifier
			desc = consecrated_king_ai_behavior_large_tt
			years = 20
		}
		custom_tooltip = consecrated_king_ai_behavior_large_tt
	}
	else = {
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = consecrated_king_modifier_small
			years = 20
		}
	}
	scope:religious_authority = { save_scope_as = cleric }
   	ecclesiastical_title_bestowing_clerical_hesitation_effect = yes
}
grant_title_populate_concerned_de_jure_list_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			$TITLE_GAINER$ = {
				any_held_title = {
					tier = tier_duchy
					kingdom.holder ?= {
						religion = scope:puppet_or_actor.religion
						NOT = {
							this = scope:puppet_or_actor
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		$TITLE_GAINER$ = {
			every_held_title = {
				limit = {
					tier = tier_duchy
					kingdom.holder ?= {
						religion = scope:puppet_or_actor.religion
						NOT = {
							this = scope:puppet_or_actor
						}
					}
				}
				kingdom.holder = {
					add_to_list = grateful_refusal_chars
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
bestow_title_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:use_hook ?= yes
			scope:puppet_or_actor ?= { has_usable_hook = scope:recipient }
		}
		scope:puppet_or_actor = { use_hook = scope:recipient }
	}
	if = {
    	limit = {
    		scope:gain_weak_hook ?= yes
    	}
    	scope:puppet_or_actor = {
    		add_hook = {
    			type = obligation_hook
    			target = scope:recipient
    		}
    	}
    }
    else_if = {
    	limit = {
    		scope:gain_strong_hook ?= yes
    	}
    	scope:puppet_or_actor = {
    		add_hook = {
    			type = strong_obligation_hook
    			target = scope:recipient
    		}
    	}
    }
    else = {
    	if = {
    		limit = {
    			exists = scope:target
    		}
    		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
    			add_prestige = {
    				value = minor_prestige_gain
    				multiply = {
    					value = scope:target.tier
    					subtract = 2
    				}
    			}
    		}
    		
    	}
    	else = {
    		scope:puppet_or_actor = {
    			add_prestige = minor_prestige_gain
    		}
    	}
    }

	scope:recipient = { save_scope_as = bestowed_title_holder }
	scope:puppet_or_actor = { save_scope_as = bestowing_cleric }
	bestow_title_shared_effect = yes
}
# Creates the unheld title and hands it to its new holder. Runs in the new holder's scope; callers must save scope:bestowed_title_holder and scope:bestowing_cleric first, since the clergy and the ruler swap roles between the bestowal and the petition.
bestow_title_shared_effect = {
	create_title_and_vassal_change = {
		type = created
		save_scope_as = ecclesiastical_bestow_title_change
		add_claim_on_loss = no
	}
	scope:target = {
		change_title_holder = {
			holder = scope:bestowed_title_holder
			change = scope:ecclesiastical_bestow_title_change
		}
	}
	resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:ecclesiastical_bestow_title_change

	scope:target ?= {
		if = {
			limit = { tier = tier_empire }
			scope:bestowed_title_holder = {
				add_opinion = {
					modifier = received_title_empire
					target = scope:bestowing_cleric
					opinion = 50
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { tier = tier_kingdom }
			scope:bestowed_title_holder = {
				add_opinion = {
					modifier = received_title_kingdom
					target = scope:bestowing_cleric
					opinion = 25
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { tier = tier_duchy }
			scope:bestowed_title_holder = {
				add_opinion = {
					modifier = received_title_duchy
					target = scope:bestowing_cleric
					opinion = 15
				}
			}
		}
	}
    if = {
    	limit = {
    		NOT = { government_has_flag = government_is_theocracy }
    		top_participant_group:the_christian_church ?= {
				OR = {
					participant_group_type = christian_clerical_main_power_rulers
					participant_group_type = christian_regular_main_power_rulers
				}
			}
			situation:the_christian_church ?= {
				situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_hof_bestowed_title
			}
			scope:target.tier >= tier_kingdom
    	}
    	if = {
    		limit = {
    			exists = scope:target
    			scope:target.tier >= tier_empire
    		}
    		situation:the_christian_church = {
    			trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_hof_bestowed_title_major
    		}
    	}
    	else = {
    		situation:the_christian_church = {
    			trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_hof_bestowed_title
    		}
    	}
    }
    else_if = {
    	limit = {
    		government_has_flag = government_is_theocracy
    		top_participant_group:the_christian_church ?= {
				OR = {
					participant_group_type = christian_clerical_main_power_rulers
					participant_group_type = christian_regular_main_power_rulers
				}
			}
			situation:the_christian_church ?= {
				situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_hof_bestowed_title_clergy
			}
    	}
    	if = {
    		limit = {
    			exists = scope:target
    			scope:target.tier >= tier_empire
    		}
    		situation:the_christian_church = {
    			trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_hof_bestowed_title_clergy_major
    		}
    	}
    	else_if = {
    		limit = {
    			exists = scope:target
    			scope:target.tier >= tier_kingdom
    		}
    		situation:the_christian_church = {
    			trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_hof_bestowed_title_clergy
    		}
    	}
    	else = {
    		situation:the_christian_church = {
    			trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_hof_bestowed_title_clergy_minor
    		}
    	}
    }
	scope:bestowing_cleric = { save_scope_as = cleric }
    if = {
    	limit = {
    		scope:bestowing_cleric = {
    			is_ai = no
    		}
    	}
    	ecclesiastical_title_bestowing_clerical_hesitation_effect = yes
    }
    else = {
    	hidden_effect = {
    		ecclesiastical_title_bestowing_clerical_hesitation_effect = yes
    	}
    }
}
# The petition counterpart to bestow_title_effect: the clergy proclaim the unheld title and crown the petitioner. Runs in the petitioner's scope.
request_unformed_title_effect = {
	custom_tooltip = request_unformed_title_gain_tt
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:donate_faith ?= yes
		}
		pay_short_term_gold = {
			target = scope:recipient
			gold = major_gold_value
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:use_hook ?= yes
			has_usable_hook = scope:recipient
		}
		use_hook = scope:recipient
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:offer_hook ?= yes
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			add_hook = {
				type = favor_hook
				target = scope:puppet_or_actor
			}
		}
	}
	pam_offer_pilgrimage_effect = {
		ACTOR = scope:puppet_or_actor
		CLERIC = scope:recipient
	}

	scope:puppet_or_actor = { save_scope_as = bestowed_title_holder }
	scope:recipient = { save_scope_as = bestowing_cleric }
	bestow_title_shared_effect = yes

	#The clergy publicly stand behind the new crown, and their flock is expected to respect it
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_ai = yes
		}
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = consecrated_king_modifier
			desc = consecrated_king_ai_behavior_large_tt
			years = 20
		}
		custom_tooltip = consecrated_king_ai_behavior_large_tt
	}
	else = {
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = consecrated_king_modifier_small
			years = 20
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:promise_holy_war ?= yes
		}
		faith = { save_scope_as = promised_holy_war_faith }
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = bonus_holy_war_tt
			add_character_flag = {
				flag = bonus_major_holy_war
				years = 5
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = request_unformed_title_holy_war_deadline_tt
		#The clergy come looking for results; pam_interactions_events.0098 judges the vow
		trigger_event = {
			id = pam_interactions_events.0098
			years = 5
		}
	}
}

# Runs in the scope of the ruler being judged; the cleric weighing the act must be saved as scope:cleric by the caller.
ecclesiastical_title_bestowing_clerical_hesitation_effect = {
	save_scope_as = only_consideration
	if = {
		limit = {
			clerical_hesitation_claim_interaction_value >= 9
		}
		scope:cleric = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:target
				}
				change_spiritual_fulfillment = {
					value = major_spiritual_fulfillment_loss
					multiply = {
						value = scope:target.tier
						subtract = 2
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				change_spiritual_fulfillment = massive_spiritual_fulfillment_loss
			}
			custom_tooltip = clerical_hesitation_high_bestowing_tt
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			clerical_hesitation_claim_interaction_value >= 6
		}
		scope:cleric = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:target
				}
				change_spiritual_fulfillment = {
					value = medium_spiritual_fulfillment_loss
					multiply = {
						value = scope:target.tier
						subtract = 2
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				change_spiritual_fulfillment = major_spiritual_fulfillment_loss
			}
			custom_tooltip = clerical_hesitation_high_bestowing_tt
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			clerical_hesitation_claim_interaction_value >= 3
		}
		scope:cleric = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:target
				}
				change_spiritual_fulfillment = {
					value = minor_spiritual_fulfillment_loss
					multiply = {
						value = scope:target.tier
						subtract = 2
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				change_spiritual_fulfillment = medium_spiritual_fulfillment_loss
			}
			custom_tooltip = clerical_hesitation_high_bestowing_tt
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			clerical_hesitation_claim_interaction_value >= 1
		}
		scope:cleric = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:target
				}
				change_spiritual_fulfillment = {
					value = miniscule_spiritual_fulfillment_loss
					multiply = {
						value = scope:target.tier
						subtract = 2
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				change_spiritual_fulfillment = minor_spiritual_fulfillment_loss
			}
			custom_tooltip = clerical_hesitation_high_bestowing_tt
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			clerical_hesitation_claim_interaction_value < 1
			clerical_hesitation_claim_interaction_value >= -2
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			clerical_hesitation_claim_interaction_value >= -3
		}
		scope:cleric = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:target
				}
				change_spiritual_fulfillment = {
					value = miniscule_spiritual_fulfillment_value
					divide = 2
					multiply = {
						value = scope:target.tier
						subtract = 2
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				change_spiritual_fulfillment = miniscule_spiritual_fulfillment_value
			}
			custom_tooltip = clerical_hesitation_low_bestowing_tt
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			clerical_hesitation_claim_interaction_value >= -5
		}
		scope:cleric = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:target
				}
				change_spiritual_fulfillment = {
					value = miniscule_spiritual_fulfillment_value
					multiply = {
						value = scope:target.tier
						subtract = 2
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				change_spiritual_fulfillment = minor_spiritual_fulfillment_value
			}
			custom_tooltip = clerical_hesitation_low_bestowing_tt
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			clerical_hesitation_claim_interaction_value >= -8
		}
		scope:cleric = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:target
				}
				change_spiritual_fulfillment = {
					value = minor_spiritual_fulfillment_value
					multiply = {
						value = scope:target.tier
						subtract = 2
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				change_spiritual_fulfillment = medium_spiritual_fulfillment_value
			}
			custom_tooltip = clerical_hesitation_low_bestowing_tt
		}
	}
	else = {
		scope:cleric = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:target
				}
				change_spiritual_fulfillment = {
					value = medium_spiritual_fulfillment_value
					multiply = {
						value = scope:target.tier
						subtract = 2
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				change_spiritual_fulfillment = major_spiritual_fulfillment_value
			}
			custom_tooltip = clerical_hesitation_low_bestowing_tt
		}
	}
}

# Never pair with a firing stress_and_fulfillment_impact on the same option
# Punishing/executing a proven witch
pam_witch_justice_fulfillment_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { rite = { rite_has_parameter = witchcraft_illegal } }
		change_spiritual_fulfillment = medium_spiritual_fulfillment_value
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { rite = { rite_has_parameter = witchcraft_accepted } }
		change_spiritual_fulfillment = major_spiritual_fulfillment_loss
	}
	# witchcraft_shunned: no SF either way
}

# Knowingly sparing/releasing a proven witch.
pam_witch_leniency_fulfillment_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { rite = { rite_has_parameter = witchcraft_illegal } }
		change_spiritual_fulfillment = medium_spiritual_fulfillment_loss
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { rite = { rite_has_parameter = witchcraft_accepted } }
		change_spiritual_fulfillment = minor_spiritual_fulfillment_value
	}
}

# Recruiting/protecting/allying with a witch.
pam_witch_embrace_fulfillment_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { rite = { rite_has_parameter = witchcraft_illegal } }
		change_spiritual_fulfillment = major_spiritual_fulfillment_loss
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { rite = { rite_has_doctrine = doctrine_witchcraft_virtuous } }
		change_spiritual_fulfillment = medium_spiritual_fulfillment_value
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { rite = { rite_has_parameter = witchcraft_accepted } }
		change_spiritual_fulfillment = minor_spiritual_fulfillment_value
	}
}

add_clerical_region_with_non_christian_capital_effect = {
	$REGION_TITLE$ ?= {
		if = {
			limit = {
				holder.religion ?= religion:tyrism_religion #Warcraft
				NOT = { title_province.religion ?= religion:tyrism_religion }
			}
			scope:core_region = { add_clerical_realm_to_sub_region = prev }
		}
	}
}

ecclesiastical_de_jure_shifting_effect = {
	custom_tooltip = gain_de_jure_target_title_tt
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:promise_domination ?= yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_legitimacy = yes
			}
			custom_tooltip = domination_promise_tt
		}
		else = {
			custom_tooltip = domination_promise_no_legitimacy_tt
		}
		trigger_event = {
			id = pam_interactions_events.0093
			years = 5
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:donate_faith ?= yes
		}
		pay_short_term_gold = {
			target = $CLERIC$
			gold = major_gold_value
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:use_hook ?= yes
			has_usable_hook = $CLERIC$
		}
		use_hook = $CLERIC$
	}
	pam_offer_pilgrimage_effect = {
		ACTOR = $DE_JURE_MASTER$
		CLERIC = $CLERIC$
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:promise_conversion ?= yes
		}
		remove_treasury_or_gold = {
				value = {
					value = $DE_JURE_MASTER$.miniscule_gold_value
					if = {
						limit = {
							exists = scope:target
						}
						scope:target = {
							every_de_jure_county = {
								limit = {
									OR = {
										NOT = {
											faith = $DE_JURE_MASTER$.faith
										}
										trigger_if = {
											limit = {
												$DE_JURE_MASTER$.rite = $CLERIC$.rite
											}
											AND = {
												NOT = {
													rite = $DE_JURE_MASTER$.rite
												}
												rite = {
													"divergence(scope:de_jure_master.rite)" >= medium_rite_divergence_value
												}
											}
										}
									}
								}
								add = $DE_JURE_MASTER$.miniscule_gold_value
							}
						}
					}
					divide = 2
				}
		}
		add_piety_experience = {
				value = {
					value = $DE_JURE_MASTER$.miniscule_piety_gain
					if = {
						limit = {
							exists = scope:target
						}
						scope:target = {
							every_de_jure_county = {
								limit = {
									OR = {
										NOT = {
											faith = $DE_JURE_MASTER$.faith
										}
										trigger_if = {
											limit = {
												$DE_JURE_MASTER$.rite = $CLERIC$.rite
											}
											AND = {
												NOT = {
													rite = $DE_JURE_MASTER$.rite
												}
												rite = {
													"divergence(scope:de_jure_master.rite)" >= medium_rite_divergence_value
												}
											}
										}
									}
								}
								add = $DE_JURE_MASTER$.miniscule_piety_gain
							}
						}
					}
					divide = 2
				}
		}
		scope:target = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						$DE_JURE_MASTER$.rite = $CLERIC$.rite
						scope:target ?= {
							any_de_jure_county = {
								faith = $DE_JURE_MASTER$.faith
								NOR = {
									rite = $DE_JURE_MASTER$.rite
									holder = {
										is_ai = no
										NOR = {
											this = $DE_JURE_MASTER$
											this = $CLERIC$
										}
									}
								}
								rite = {
									"divergence(scope:de_jure_master.rite)" >= medium_rite_divergence_value
									# Freshly founded rites get a grace period before missionaries convert their counties back
									NOT = { has_variable = pam_rite_grace_period }
								}
							}
						}
					}
					every_de_jure_county = {
						custom = custom.every_non_recipient_rite_county_in_target
						limit = {
							faith = $DE_JURE_MASTER$.faith
							NOR = {
								rite = $DE_JURE_MASTER$.rite
								holder = {
									is_ai = no
									NOR = {
										this = $DE_JURE_MASTER$
										this = $CLERIC$
									}
								}
							}
							rite = {
								"divergence(scope:de_jure_master.rite)" >= medium_rite_divergence_value
								# Freshly founded rites get a grace period before missionaries convert their counties back
								NOT = { has_variable = pam_rite_grace_period }
							}
						}
						custom_tooltip = dispatch_missionaries_random_chance_of_counties_converting_rite_tt
						hidden_effect = {
							random = {
								chance = 20
								set_county_rite = $DE_JURE_MASTER$.rite
							}
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:target ?= {
							any_de_jure_county = {
								NOR = {
									faith = $DE_JURE_MASTER$.faith
									holder = {
										is_ai = no
										NOR = {
											this = $DE_JURE_MASTER$
											this = $CLERIC$
										}
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
					every_de_jure_county = {
						custom = custom.every_non_actor_faith_county_in_target
						limit = {
							NOR = {
								faith = $DE_JURE_MASTER$.faith
								holder = {
									is_ai = no
									NOR = {
										this = $DE_JURE_MASTER$
										this = $CLERIC$
									}
								}
							}
						}
						custom_tooltip = dispatch_missionaries_random_chance_of_counties_converting_faith_tt
						hidden_effect = {
							random = {
								chance = 10
								set_county_faith = $DE_JURE_MASTER$.faith
							}
						}
					}
				}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:offer_hook ?= yes
		}
		$CLERIC$ = {
			add_hook = {
				type = favor_hook
				target = $DE_JURE_MASTER$
			}
		}
	}

	scope:target = {
		hidden_effect = { set_de_jure_liege_title = $DE_JURE_MASTER$.primary_title }
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:recipient = $CLERIC$
			}
			every_de_jure_county = {
				custom = custom.every_non_actor_faith_county_controlled_in_target
				limit = {
					NOT = { faith = $DE_JURE_MASTER$.faith }
					holder = {
						target_is_same_character_or_above = $DE_JURE_MASTER$
					}
				}
				change_county_control = extreme_county_control_loss
			}
		}
		else = {
			every_de_jure_county = {
				custom = custom.every_non_recipient_faith_county_controlled_in_target
				limit = {
					NOT = { faith = $DE_JURE_MASTER$.faith }
					holder = {
						target_is_same_character_or_above = $DE_JURE_MASTER$
					}
				}
				change_county_control = extreme_county_control_loss
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:recipient = $CLERIC$
			}
			every_de_jure_county = {
				custom = custom.every_non_recipient_rite_county_controlled_in_target
				limit = {
					faith = $DE_JURE_MASTER$.faith
					NOT = {
						rite = $CLERIC$.rite
					}
					holder = {
						target_is_same_character_or_above = $DE_JURE_MASTER$
					}
				}
				change_county_control = medium_county_control_loss
			}
		}
		else = {
			every_de_jure_county = {
				custom = custom.every_non_actor_rite_county_controlled_in_target
				limit = {
					faith = $DE_JURE_MASTER$.faith
					NOT = {
						rite = $CLERIC$.rite
					}
					holder = {
						target_is_same_character_or_above = $DE_JURE_MASTER$
					}
				}
				change_county_control = medium_county_control_loss
			}
		}
	}
	scope:puppet_or_actor ?= {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_legitimacy = yes
				exists = scope:target
			}
			add_legitimacy = {
				value = medium_legitimacy_loss
				multiply = {
					value = scope:target.tier
					subtract = 2
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

pam_cardinal_vacancy_on_death_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
			OR = {
				exists = clerical_elector_title
				has_character_flag = cardinal_flag
			}
			OR = {
				exists = faith.religious_head
				exists = title:k_papal_state.holder
			}
			faith = {
				has_doctrine_parameter = has_clerical_electors
				any_clerical_elector_title = { count < 10 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { exists = faith.religious_head }
			faith.religious_head = { save_scope_as = head_of_faith }
		}
		else = {
			title:k_papal_state.holder = { save_scope_as = head_of_faith }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:head_of_faith = { is_ai = yes }
			}
			faith = {
				ordered_clerical_region_title = {
					limit = {
						holder ?= {
							is_alive = yes
							is_adult = yes
							is_incapable = no
							NOT = { has_trait = excommunicated }
							NOT = { exists = clerical_elector_title }
							this != scope:head_of_faith
							pam_can_hold_cardinalate_trigger = yes
							pam_pope_is_head_of_faith_or_rite_trigger = yes
						}
					}
					order_by = holder.piety_level
					max = 1
					check_range_bounds = no
					holder = {
						save_scope_as = cardinal_candidate
						trigger_event = {
							id = pam_clergy_events.0040
							days = 3
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			faith = {
				ordered_clerical_region_title = {
					limit = {
						holder ?= {
							is_ai = yes
							is_alive = yes
							is_adult = yes
							is_incapable = no
							NOT = { has_trait = excommunicated }
							NOT = { exists = clerical_elector_title }
							this != scope:head_of_faith
							pam_can_hold_cardinalate_trigger = yes
							pam_pope_is_head_of_faith_or_rite_trigger = yes
						}
					}
					order_by = holder.piety_level
					max = 1
					check_range_bounds = no
					holder = {
						save_scope_as = cardinal_candidate
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:cardinal_candidate
				}
				scope:head_of_faith = {
					trigger_event = {
						id = pam_clergy_events.0041
						days = 3
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
demand_church_succession_side_effects = {
	primary_title = {
		every_title_heir = {
			limit = {
				NOR = {
					this = scope:actor
					this = scope:puppet_or_actor
				}
				prev = {
					place_in_line_of_succession = {
						target = prev
						value <= 3
					}
				}		
			}
			add_opinion = {
				target = scope:puppet_or_actor
				modifier = meddling_church_opinion
			}
		}
	}
}
# A Realm Priest Puppet who has grown into a titled cleric belongs in the Clerical Ruler Puppet slot,
# which also frees the Realm Priest slot for whoever takes over the council seat.
# ROOT = the puppet, $PUPPETEER$ = the ruler holding them.
# No-op if the target slot is taken, or if they still hold the court chaplain seat.
pam_make_clerical_ruler_puppet_effect = {
	# Both scopes are saved up front because clear_puppet_status severs the puppeteer link
	save_temporary_scope_as = pam_clerical_ruler_puppet
	$PUPPETEER$ = { save_temporary_scope_as = pam_clerical_puppeteer }
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:pam_clerical_puppeteer = {
				NOR = {
					exists = puppet:external_theological_agent_puppet
					cp:councillor_court_chaplain ?= scope:pam_clerical_ruler_puppet
				}
			}
		}
		# Silent: the slot they move into is what the player is told about, not the one they leave
		hidden_effect = { clear_puppet_status = yes }
		scope:pam_clerical_puppeteer = {
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = make_external_clerical_puppet_on_action_notification
				left_icon = scope:pam_clerical_puppeteer
				right_icon = scope:pam_clerical_ruler_puppet
				set_puppet = {
					target = scope:pam_clerical_ruler_puppet
					type = external_theological_agent_puppet
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

pam_generic_set_puppet_interaction_side_effects = {
	if = {
		limit = { scope:hook ?= yes }
		use_hook = scope:recipient
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:skill_support ?= yes
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:actor = {
						highest_skill = diplomacy
					}
				}
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = skilled_puppeteer_modifier_diplomacy
					desc = skilled_puppeteer_behavior_tt
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					scope:actor = {
						highest_skill = martial
					}
				}
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = skilled_puppeteer_modifier_martial
					desc = skilled_puppeteer_behavior_tt
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					scope:actor = {
						highest_skill = stewardship
					}
				}
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = skilled_puppeteer_modifier_stewardship
					desc = skilled_puppeteer_behavior_tt
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					scope:actor = {
						highest_skill = intrigue
					}
				}
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = skilled_puppeteer_modifier_intrigue
					desc = skilled_puppeteer_behavior_tt
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					scope:actor = {
						highest_skill = learning
					}
				}
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = skilled_puppeteer_modifier_learning
					desc = skilled_puppeteer_behavior_tt
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = skilled_puppeteer_modifier_general
					desc = skilled_puppeteer_behavior_tt
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = skilled_puppeteer_behavior_tt
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:piety_support ?= yes
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_playable_character = yes }	# Only playable characters have spiritual fulfillment
				change_spiritual_fulfillment = medium_spiritual_fulfillment_value
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_clergy = yes
				}
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = spiritual_puppeteer_modifier_cleric
					desc = spiritual_puppeteer_behavior_tt
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					modifier = spiritual_puppeteer_modifier_secular
					desc = spiritual_puppeteer_behavior_tt
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = spiritual_puppeteer_behavior_tt
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:dread_support ?= yes
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_trait = compassionate
						has_trait = forgiving
						has_trait = generous
						has_trait = just
					}
				}
				add_stress = medium_stress_gain
			}
			add_character_modifier = {
				modifier = dreadful_puppeteer_modifier
				desc = dreadful_puppeteer_behavior_tt
			}
			custom_tooltip = dreadful_puppeteer_behavior_tt
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:friendly_support ?= yes
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:actor = {
						is_ai = no
					}
					OR = {
						has_trait = trusting
						has_trait = compassionate
						has_trait = generous
						has_trait = forgiving
						has_trait = just
						has_trait = humble
					}
				}
				add_trait = loyal
			}
			else = {
				random = {
					chance = {
						value = 25
						if = {
							limit = {
								OR = {
									has_trait = trusting
									has_trait = compassionate
									has_trait = generous
									has_trait = forgiving
									has_trait = just
									has_trait = humble
								}
							}
							add = 25
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
								scope:actor = {
									is_ai = no
								}
							}
							add = 25
						}
					}
					add_trait = loyal
				}
			}
			add_character_modifier = {
				modifier = beloved_puppeteer_modifier
				desc = beloved_puppeteer_behavior_tt
			}
			custom_tooltip = beloved_puppeteer_behavior_tt
		}
		stress_impact = {
			sadistic = medium_stress_impact_gain
			arbitrary = medium_stress_impact_gain
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:lunacy_support ?= yes
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_adult = yes
				}
				add_stress = massive_stress_gain
				change_spiritual_fulfillment = massive_spiritual_fulfillment_loss
			}
			else = {
				add_stress = major_stress_gain
				change_spiritual_fulfillment = major_spiritual_fulfillment_loss
			}
			add_character_modifier = {
				modifier = deranging_puppeteer_modifier
				desc = deranging_puppeteer_behavior_tt
			}
			custom_tooltip = deranging_puppeteer_behavior_tt
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				scope:lunacy_support ?= yes
				scope:dread_support ?= yes
			}
		}
		stress_impact = {
			compassionate = major_stress_impact_gain
			just = major_stress_impact_gain
			humble = major_stress_impact_gain
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:friendly_support ?= yes
		}
		stress_impact = {
			compassionate = minor_stress_impact_gain
			trusting = minor_stress_impact_gain
			humble = minor_stress_impact_gain
			generous = minor_stress_impact_gain
			forgiving = minor_stress_impact_gain
			honest = minor_stress_impact_gain
			just = minor_stress_impact_gain
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				scope:skill_support ?= yes
				scope:piety_support ?= yes
				scope:dread_support ?= yes
				scope:friendly_support ?= yes
				scope:lunacy_support ?= yes
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			custom_tooltip = modifier_removed_when_puppeting_ends_tt
		}
	}
	#Tracking variable that makes it easier to re-puppet former puppets
	scope:recipient = {
		add_to_variable_list = {
			name = puppeteers
			target = scope:actor
		}
	}
}
remove_puppet_modifiers_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_character_modifier = skilled_puppeteer_modifier_diplomacy
				has_character_modifier = skilled_puppeteer_modifier_martial
				has_character_modifier = skilled_puppeteer_modifier_stewardship
				has_character_modifier = skilled_puppeteer_modifier_intrigue
				has_character_modifier = skilled_puppeteer_modifier_learning
				has_character_modifier = skilled_puppeteer_modifier_general
				has_character_modifier = spiritual_puppeteer_modifier_cleric
				has_character_modifier = spiritual_puppeteer_modifier_secular
				has_character_modifier = dreadful_puppeteer_modifier
				has_character_modifier = beloved_puppeteer_modifier
				has_character_modifier = deranging_puppeteer_modifier
			}
		}
		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_neutral
			title = puppet_modifier_removal.t
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_character_modifier = skilled_puppeteer_modifier_diplomacy
				}
				remove_character_modifier = skilled_puppeteer_modifier_diplomacy
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_character_modifier = skilled_puppeteer_modifier_martial
				}
				remove_character_modifier = skilled_puppeteer_modifier_martial
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_character_modifier = skilled_puppeteer_modifier_stewardship
				}
				remove_character_modifier = skilled_puppeteer_modifier_stewardship
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_character_modifier = skilled_puppeteer_modifier_intrigue
				}
				remove_character_modifier = skilled_puppeteer_modifier_intrigue
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_character_modifier = skilled_puppeteer_modifier_learning
				}
				remove_character_modifier = skilled_puppeteer_modifier_learning
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_character_modifier = skilled_puppeteer_modifier_general
				}
				remove_character_modifier = skilled_puppeteer_modifier_general
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_character_modifier = spiritual_puppeteer_modifier_cleric
				}
				remove_character_modifier = spiritual_puppeteer_modifier_cleric
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_character_modifier = spiritual_puppeteer_modifier_secular
				}
				remove_character_modifier = spiritual_puppeteer_modifier_secular
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_character_modifier = dreadful_puppeteer_modifier
				}
				remove_character_modifier = dreadful_puppeteer_modifier
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_character_modifier = beloved_puppeteer_modifier
				}
				remove_character_modifier = beloved_puppeteer_modifier
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_character_modifier = deranging_puppeteer_modifier
				}
				remove_character_modifier = deranging_puppeteer_modifier
			}
		}
	}
}
gnostic_tenet_learning_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_personal_tenet_flag = gnosticism_other_tenets
			NAND = {
				knows_tenet = tenet_astrology
				knows_tenet = tenet_inner_journey
				knows_tenet = tenet_esotericism
				knows_tenet = tenet_miaphysitism
				knows_tenet = tenet_asceticism
				knows_tenet = tenet_vows_of_poverty
				knows_tenet = tenet_pacifism
				knows_tenet = tenet_mortification_of_the_flesh
				knows_tenet = tenet_aniconism
				knows_tenet = tenet_natural_primitivism
				knows_tenet = tenet_false_conversion_sanction
				knows_tenet = tenet_consolamentum
				knows_tenet = tenet_scholasticism
				knows_tenet = tenet_pursuit_of_knowledge
				knows_tenet = tenet_alexandrian_catechism
				knows_tenet = tenet_mendicant_preachers
				knows_tenet = tenet_reincarnation
			}
		}
		random = {
			chance = {
				value = 10
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_trait_xp = {
							trait = lifestyle_scholar
							value >= 60
						}
					}
					add = 10
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_trait_xp = {
							trait = lifestyle_scholar
							value >= 80
						}
					}
					add = 15
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_trait_xp = {
							trait = lifestyle_scholar
							value >= 100
						}
					}
					add = 15
				}
			}
			random_list = {
				1 = {
					trigger = {
						NOT = {
							knows_tenet = tenet_astrology
						}
					}
					tenet:tenet_astrology = { save_scope_as = gnostic_tenet }
				}
				1 = {
					trigger = {
						NOT = {
							knows_tenet = tenet_inner_journey
						}
					}
					tenet:tenet_inner_journey = { save_scope_as = gnostic_tenet }
				}
				1 = {
					trigger = {
						NOT = {
							knows_tenet = tenet_esotericism
						}
					}
					tenet:tenet_esotericism = { save_scope_as = gnostic_tenet }
				}
				1 = {
					trigger = {
						NOT = {
							knows_tenet = tenet_miaphysitism
						}
					}
					tenet:tenet_miaphysitism = { save_scope_as = gnostic_tenet }
				}
				1 = {
					trigger = {
						NOT = {
							knows_tenet = tenet_asceticism
						}
					}
					tenet:tenet_asceticism = { save_scope_as = gnostic_tenet }
				}
				1 = {
					trigger = {
						NOT = {
							knows_tenet = tenet_vows_of_poverty
						}
					}
					tenet:tenet_vows_of_poverty = { save_scope_as = gnostic_tenet }
				}
				1 = {
					trigger = {
						NOT = {
							knows_tenet = tenet_pacifism
						}
					}
					tenet:tenet_pacifism = { save_scope_as = gnostic_tenet }
				}
				1 = {
					trigger = {
						NOT = {
							knows_tenet = tenet_mortification_of_the_flesh
						}
					}
					tenet:tenet_mortification_of_the_flesh = { save_scope_as = gnostic_tenet }
				}
				1 = {
					trigger = {
						NOT = {
							knows_tenet = tenet_aniconism
						}
					}
					tenet:tenet_aniconism = { save_scope_as = gnostic_tenet }
				}
				1 = {
					trigger = {
						NOT = {
							knows_tenet = tenet_natural_primitivism
						}
					}
					tenet:tenet_natural_primitivism = { save_scope_as = gnostic_tenet }
				}
				1 = {
					trigger = {
						NOT = {
							knows_tenet = tenet_false_conversion_sanction
						}
					}
					tenet:tenet_false_conversion_sanction = { save_scope_as = gnostic_tenet }
				}
				1 = {
					trigger = {
						NOT = {
							knows_tenet = tenet_consolamentum
						}
					}
					tenet:tenet_consolamentum = { save_scope_as = gnostic_tenet }
				}
				1 = {
					trigger = {
						NOT = {
							knows_tenet = tenet_scholasticism
						}
					}
					tenet:tenet_scholasticism = { save_scope_as = gnostic_tenet }
				}
				1 = {
					trigger = {
						NOT = {
							knows_tenet = tenet_pursuit_of_knowledge
						}
					}
					tenet:tenet_pursuit_of_knowledge = { save_scope_as = gnostic_tenet }
				}
				1 = {
					trigger = {
						NOT = {
							knows_tenet = tenet_alexandrian_catechism
						}
					}
					tenet:tenet_alexandrian_catechism = { save_scope_as = gnostic_tenet }
				}
				1 = {
					trigger = {
						NOT = {
							knows_tenet = tenet_mendicant_preachers
						}
					}
					tenet:tenet_mendicant_preachers = { save_scope_as = gnostic_tenet }
				}
				1 = {
					trigger = {
						NOT = {
							knows_tenet = tenet_reincarnation
						}
					}
					tenet:tenet_reincarnation = { save_scope_as = gnostic_tenet }
				}
			}
			send_interface_message = {
				type = msg_passive_learned_tenet_special
				desc = gnostic_tenet_learning.t
				left_icon = root
				right_icon = scope:gnostic_tenet

				hidden_effect = { add_known_tenet = scope:gnostic_tenet }
				change_spiritual_fulfillment = minor_spiritual_fulfillment_value
			}
		}
	}
}

# Character scope. Runs from yearly_playable_pulse
# The modifier stacks, so remove-all first
pam_harmonious_society_five_bonds_effect = {
	remove_all_character_modifier_instances = tenet_harmonious_society_five_bonds_modifier

	set_local_variable = {
		name = five_bonds_tally
		value = pam_five_bonds_tally_value
	}

	set_local_variable = {
		name = five_bonds_change
		value = local_var:five_bonds_tally
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = pam_five_bonds_tally_var }
		change_local_variable = {
			name = five_bonds_change
			subtract = var:pam_five_bonds_tally_var
		}
	}

	# The pulse runs every year; only say something when the tally has actually moved.
	if = {
		limit = { local_var:five_bonds_change > 0 }
		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = pam_harmonious_society_five_bonds_toast
			desc = pam_harmonious_society_five_bonds_toast_desc
			left_icon = root
			pam_harmonious_society_apply_five_bonds_effect = yes
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:five_bonds_change < 0 }
		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_bad
			title = pam_harmonious_society_five_bonds_toast
			desc = pam_harmonious_society_five_bonds_toast_desc
			left_icon = root
			pam_harmonious_society_apply_five_bonds_effect = yes
		}
	}
	else = {
		hidden_effect = {
			pam_harmonious_society_apply_five_bonds_effect = yes
		}
	}

	set_variable = {
		name = pam_five_bonds_tally_var
		value = local_var:five_bonds_tally
	}
}

# Character scope. One stack for each bond kept, named individually so the toast reads as a tally.
pam_harmonious_society_apply_five_bonds_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { pam_five_bonds_ruler_subject_trigger = yes }
		custom_tooltip = pam_five_bonds_ruler_subject_tt
		add_character_modifier = tenet_harmonious_society_five_bonds_modifier
	}
	if = {
		limit = { pam_five_bonds_parent_child_trigger = yes }
		custom_tooltip = pam_five_bonds_parent_child_tt
		add_character_modifier = tenet_harmonious_society_five_bonds_modifier
	}
	if = {
		limit = { pam_five_bonds_spouse_trigger = yes }
		custom_tooltip = pam_five_bonds_spouse_tt
		add_character_modifier = tenet_harmonious_society_five_bonds_modifier
	}
	if = {
		limit = { pam_five_bonds_sibling_trigger = yes }
		custom_tooltip = pam_five_bonds_sibling_tt
		add_character_modifier = tenet_harmonious_society_five_bonds_modifier
	}
	if = {
		limit = { pam_five_bonds_friend_trigger = yes }
		custom_tooltip = pam_five_bonds_friend_tt
		add_character_modifier = tenet_harmonious_society_five_bonds_modifier
	}
}

# One stack of $MODIFIER$ per trait the rite counts as a sin
pam_add_modifier_per_sin_trait_effect = {
	remove_all_character_modifier_instances = $MODIFIER$

	every_character_trait = {
		limit = {
			root.rite = { trait_is_sin_rite = prev }
		}
		root = {
			add_character_modifier = $MODIFIER$
		}
	}
}

pam_tenet_purgatory_sin_penalty_effect = {
	pam_add_modifier_per_sin_trait_effect = { MODIFIER = tenet_purgatory_sin_penalty_modifier }
}

pam_tenet_original_sin_insight_effect = {
	pam_add_modifier_per_sin_trait_effect = { MODIFIER = tenet_original_sin_sinners_insight_modifier }
}

# New lover or soulmate; one payout a year
pam_tenet_polyamory_new_beloved_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_personal_tenet_flag = tenet_polyamory_new_beloved
			NOT = { has_character_flag = pam_polyamory_new_beloved_cooldown }
		}
		send_interface_toast = {
			type = event_toast_effect_good
			title = tenet_polyamory_new_beloved_toast
			left_icon = root
			change_spiritual_fulfillment = minor_spiritual_fulfillment_value
			add_stress = medium_stress_loss
			add_character_modifier = {
				modifier = tenet_polyamory_new_beloved_modifier
				years = 5
			}
		}
		add_character_flag = {
			flag = pam_polyamory_new_beloved_cooldown
			days = 365
		}
	}
}

pam_tenet_apostolic_succession_episcopate_effect = {
	remove_character_modifier = tenet_apostolic_succession_episcopate_modifier

	if = {
		limit = {
			is_valid_for_legitimacy_change = yes
			pam_apostolic_succession_bishop_count_value > 0
		}
		add_character_modifier = tenet_apostolic_succession_episcopate_modifier
	}
}

pam_tenet_divine_liturgy_choirs_effect = {
	remove_character_modifier = tenet_divine_liturgy_choirs_modifier

	if = {
		limit = {
			pam_divine_liturgy_temple_count_value > 0
		}
		add_character_modifier = tenet_divine_liturgy_choirs_modifier
	}
}

pam_tenet_divine_liturgy_officiant_effect = {
	remove_character_modifier = tenet_divine_liturgy_officiant_modifier

	if = {
		limit = {
			pam_divine_liturgy_chaplain_learning_value > 0
		}
		add_character_modifier = tenet_divine_liturgy_officiant_modifier
	}

	# Timed past yearly retally so a lapsed chaplain ages out
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_personal_tenet_flag = tenet_divine_liturgy_schola_cantorum
			cp:councillor_court_chaplain ?= { faith = root.faith }
		}
		cp:councillor_court_chaplain = {
			add_character_modifier = {
				modifier = tenet_divine_liturgy_schola_cantorum_modifier
				months = 13
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################
# Holy Myron

# OWNER - character receiving the ampulla (artifact is created in their scope)
# CREATOR - the cleric who gathered the balsams and consecrated the oil
# RARITY - common | masterwork | famed | illustrious
pam_create_holy_myron_artifact_effect = {
	get_artifact_quality_effect = yes
	get_artifact_wealth_effect = yes

	$OWNER$ = {
		create_artifact = {
			name = pam_holy_myron_artifact_name
			description = pam_holy_myron_artifact_desc_$RARITY$
			creator = $CREATOR$
			visuals = flask
			type = miscellaneous
			template = pam_holy_myron_template
			rarity = $RARITY$
			modifier = artifact_placeholder_modifier
			decaying = no
			save_scope_as = newly_created_artifact
			wealth = scope:wealth
			quality = scope:quality
		}
	}
	scope:newly_created_artifact = {
		save_scope_as = pam_myron_vessel
		if = {
			limit = { rarity = common }
			set_variable = {
				name = pam_holy_myron_charges
				value = 1
			}
			add_artifact_modifier = pam_holy_myron_vessel_modifier
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { rarity = masterwork }
			set_variable = {
				name = pam_holy_myron_charges
				value = 2
			}
			add_artifact_modifier = pam_holy_myron_vessel_modifier
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { rarity = famed }
			set_variable = {
				name = pam_holy_myron_charges
				value = 3
			}
			add_artifact_modifier = pam_holy_myron_vessel_potent_modifier
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = {
				name = pam_holy_myron_charges
				value = 4
			}
			add_artifact_modifier = pam_holy_myron_vessel_potent_modifier
		}
		# We set the description again once the charge variable exists, reading it through scope:pam_myron_vessel
		set_artifact_description = pam_holy_myron_artifact_desc_charged_$RARITY$
		remove_artifact_modifier = artifact_placeholder_modifier
	}
}

# Character scope: saves the owned ampulla as scope:pam_holy_myron_artifact.
pam_save_holy_myron_artifact_effect = {
	random_character_artifact = {
		limit = { pam_is_holy_myron_artifact_trigger = yes }
		save_scope_as = pam_holy_myron_artifact
	}
}

# $ARTIFACT$ = the ampulla
# trigger_event with no delay runs after this script, so destroy (0002) never leaves a live empty vessel
pam_holy_myron_consume_charge_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { exists = $ARTIFACT$ }
		$ARTIFACT$ = {
			change_variable = {
				name = pam_holy_myron_charges
				subtract = 1
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				$ARTIFACT$ = { var:pam_holy_myron_charges <= 0 }
			}
			$ARTIFACT$ = { save_scope_as = pam_holy_myron_spent_vessel }
			$ARTIFACT$.artifact_owner ?= {
				trigger_event = pam_holy_myron.0002
			}
		}
		else = {
			$ARTIFACT$ = {
				save_scope_as = pam_myron_vessel
				if = {
					limit = { rarity = masterwork }
					set_artifact_description = pam_holy_myron_artifact_desc_charged_masterwork
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { rarity = famed }
					set_artifact_description = pam_holy_myron_artifact_desc_charged_famed
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { rarity = illustrious }
					set_artifact_description = pam_holy_myron_artifact_desc_charged_illustrious
				}
				else = {
					set_artifact_description = pam_holy_myron_artifact_desc_charged_common
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Character scope on the owner, run alongside pam_holy_myron_consume_charge_effect at every spend site
pam_holy_myron_spend_warning_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_character_artifact = {
				pam_is_holy_myron_artifact_trigger = yes
				trigger_if = {
					limit = { has_variable = pam_holy_myron_charges }
					var:pam_holy_myron_charges <= 1
				}
				trigger_else = {
					always = no
				}
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = pam_holy_myron_spend_last_tt
	}
	else = {
		custom_tooltip = pam_holy_myron_spend_tt
	}
}

# $PARENT$ is the devoted parent, and the child is sealed once by whichever parent we reach first
pam_holy_myron_chrismation_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:child = {
				is_alive = yes
				NOT = { has_variable = pam_holy_myron_chrismated }
			}
		}
		# Twins are one ceremony, and later paths cover the sibling through scope:pam_myron_twin
		scope:child = {
			set_variable = pam_holy_myron_chrismated
			if = {
				limit = { has_trait = twin }
				random_sibling = {
					limit = {
						is_twin_of = scope:child
						is_alive = yes
						NOT = { has_variable = pam_holy_myron_chrismated }
					}
					save_scope_as = pam_myron_twin
				}
			}
		}
		scope:pam_myron_twin ?= {
			set_variable = pam_holy_myron_chrismated
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				$PARENT$ = {
					pam_has_usable_holy_myron_trigger = yes
					is_available = yes
					exists = cp:councillor_court_chaplain
				}
			}
			$PARENT$ = {
				trigger_event = {
					id = pam_holy_myron.0300
					days = 3
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			$PARENT$ = {
				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_generic_good
					title = pam_holy_myron_chrismation_toast
					left_icon = scope:child
					right_icon = $PARENT$
					scope:child = {
						if = {
							limit = {
								NOR = {
									has_character_modifier = pam_chrismated_child_modifier
									has_character_modifier = pam_myron_chrismated_child_modifier
								}
							}
							add_character_modifier = {
								modifier = pam_chrismated_child_modifier
								years = 16
							}
						}
					}
					scope:pam_myron_twin ?= {
						if = {
							limit = {
								is_alive = yes
								NOR = {
									has_character_modifier = pam_chrismated_child_modifier
									has_character_modifier = pam_myron_chrismated_child_modifier
								}
							}
							add_character_modifier = {
								modifier = pam_chrismated_child_modifier
								years = 16
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Chaplain scope: clears all Holy Myron journey bookkeeping (journey end, death, or abort).
pam_holy_myron_journey_cleanup_effect = {
	var:pam_holy_myron_liege ?= {
		remove_variable = pam_holy_myron_journey_active
	}
	remove_variable = pam_holy_myron_journey_tier
	remove_variable = pam_holy_myron_legs
	remove_variable = pam_holy_myron_expected_legs
	remove_variable = pam_holy_myron_robbed
	remove_variable = pam_holy_myron_ransom_fired
	remove_variable = pam_holy_myron_fever_fired
	remove_variable = pam_holy_myron_liege
	remove_character_modifier = pam_holy_myron_pilgrim_modifier
}

# Artifact scope: rarity-scaled Religious Study phase-speed modifier for devotional books
add_scaled_artifact_modifier_study_scripture_scheme_phase_duration_add_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { rarity = common }
		add_artifact_modifier = artifact_study_scripture_scheme_phase_duration_add_1_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { rarity = masterwork }
		add_artifact_modifier = artifact_study_scripture_scheme_phase_duration_add_2_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { rarity = famed }
		add_artifact_modifier = artifact_study_scripture_scheme_phase_duration_add_3_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { rarity = illustrious }
		add_artifact_modifier = artifact_study_scripture_scheme_phase_duration_add_4_modifier
	}
}

pam_evangelize_personal_tenet_effect = {
	scope:recipient = {
		pam_replace_personal_tenet_effect = { TENET = scope:pam_evangelize_tenet }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:hook = yes
			has_usable_hook = scope:recipient
		}
		use_hook = scope:recipient
	}
	change_spiritual_fulfillment = miniscule_spiritual_fulfillment_value
	pam_tenet_popularity_on_evangelized_tenet_effect = {
		TENET = scope:pam_evangelize_tenet
		CHANGE = scope:recipient.pam_evangelize_tenet_popularity_gain
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			faith.main_rite = {
				has_tenet_status = {
					tenet = scope:pam_evangelize_tenet
					status = prohibited
				}
			}
		}
		every_secret = {
			type = secret_forbidden_personal_tenet
			limit = { NOT = { is_known_by = scope:recipient } }
			reveal_to = scope:recipient
		}
		show_as_tooltip = {
			scope:recipient = {
				add_secret = { type = secret_forbidden_personal_tenet }
				every_secret = {
					type = secret_forbidden_personal_tenet
					reveal_to = scope:actor
				}
			}
		}
		scope:recipient = {
			set_variable = {
				name = tenet_evangelized_by
				value = scope:actor
				days = 1
			}
		}
	}
}

pam_evangelize_personal_tenet_decline_effect = {
	change_spiritual_fulfillment = miniscule_spiritual_fulfillment_loss
	if = {
		limit = {
			faith.main_rite = {
				has_tenet_status = {
					tenet = scope:pam_evangelize_tenet
					status = prohibited
				}
			}
		}
		every_secret = {
			type = secret_forbidden_personal_tenet
			limit = { NOT = { is_known_by = scope:recipient } }
			reveal_to = scope:recipient
		}
	}
}

pam_reform_order_effect = {
	pam_save_selected_interaction_tenet_effect = yes

	scope:actor.rite = { save_scope_as = pam_reform_order_rite }
	scope:recipient = {
		holy_order = {
			save_scope_as = pam_reform_order
			title = { save_scope_as = pam_reform_order_title }
			if = {
				limit = { exists = scope:pam_selected_interaction_tenet }
				custom_tooltip = { # Order adopts the tenet
					text = pam_reform_order_adopt_tenet_tt
					hidden_effect = {
						scope:pam_reform_order = { set_order_tenet = scope:pam_selected_interaction_tenet }
					}
				}
			}
			if = { # Order adopts rite
				limit = {
					NOT = { order_rite = scope:pam_reform_order_rite }
				}
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = pam_reform_order_adopt_rite_tt
					hidden_effect = {
						scope:pam_reform_order = {
							set_order_rite = scope:pam_reform_order_rite
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = { # Adopt the PT
			limit = { exists = scope:pam_selected_interaction_tenet }
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_personal_tenet = scope:pam_selected_interaction_tenet }
				}
				pam_replace_personal_tenet_effect = { TENET = scope:pam_selected_interaction_tenet }
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = pam_reform_order_courtiers_adopt_tenet_tt
				every_courtier = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							pam_is_monastic_order_member_trigger = yes
							pam_is_military_order_member_trigger = yes
						}
						NOT = { has_personal_tenet = scope:pam_selected_interaction_tenet }
					}
					pam_replace_personal_tenet_effect = { TENET = scope:pam_selected_interaction_tenet }
				}
			}
		}
		if = { # Adopt the rite
			limit = { exists = scope:pam_reform_order_rite }
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { rite = scope:pam_reform_order_rite }
				}
				set_character_rite = scope:pam_reform_order_rite
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = pam_reform_order_courtiers_adopt_rite_tt
				every_courtier = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							pam_is_monastic_order_member_trigger = yes
							pam_is_military_order_member_trigger = yes
						}
						NOT = { rite = scope:pam_reform_order_rite }
					}
					set_character_rite = scope:pam_reform_order_rite
				}
			}
		}
	}

	hidden_effect = {
		scope:actor = {
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = pam_reform_order_interaction_toast
				right_icon = scope:recipient
				custom_tooltip = pam_reform_order_interaction_toast_tt
			}
		}
	}
}
